## 5. Gradle：up-to-date 快照、输入输出注解与 build cache

### 5.1 up-to-date 检查（快照、比对、跳过、upToDateWhen）

#### 机制名：输入/输出 fingerprint（快照）与比对

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：任务第一次执行前，Gradle 对 **inputs 取一份 fingerprint**（含输入文件路径 + 每个文件内容的哈希），执行成功后对 **outputs 再取一份 fingerprint**（含输出文件集合 + 每个文件内容的哈希），两份都持久化。之后每次执行前重新取 inputs/outputs fingerprint，与上次持久化的比对：相同则判定 up-to-date 并跳过任务动作，不同则执行。默认指纹内容来自文档章节 “How does it work?”，该章节同时给出了三条关键的**不追踪/会退化**行为：① 若文件的 `lastModified` 与 `size` 未变，Gradle 复用上一次的文件 fingerprint，因此**不检测这类改动**；② 任务/动作/嵌套输入的实现代码本身算输入；③ 顺序敏感的文件属性（如 Java classpath）连顺序变化都会导致 out-of-date。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:how_does_it_work`（章节 “Task inputs and outputs → How does it work?”）关键原句：“Before a task is executed for the first time, Gradle takes a fingerprint of the inputs. This fingerprint contains the paths of input files and a hash of the contents of each file.”“If the new fingerprints are the same as the previous fingerprints, Gradle assumes that the outputs are up to date and skips the task.”“If the stats of a file (i.e. `lastModified` and `size`) did not change, Gradle will reuse the file's fingerprint from the previous run. That means that Gradle does not detect changes when the stats of a file did not change.”“Gradle also considers the code of the task as part of the inputs to the task.”“The inputs for the task are also used to calculate the build cache key used to load task outputs when enabled.”
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/history/ExecutionInputState.java`（master）：`ImplementationSnapshot getImplementation();` / `ImmutableList<ImplementationSnapshot> getAdditionalImplementations();` / `ImmutableSortedMap<String, ValueSnapshot> getInputProperties();` / `ImmutableSortedMap<String, ? extends FileCollectionFingerprint> getInputFileProperties();`
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/history/BeforeExecutionState.java`（master）：`ImmutableSortedMap<String, FileSystemSnapshot> getOutputFileLocationSnapshots();`
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_inputs_outputs` 关键原句：“Also note that incremental build won't work unless a task has at least one task output, although tasks usually have at least one input as well.”
- **对本场景**：可迁移，是本场景最核心的替代物。「改 1 个源文件 → 6 份生成物要不要重新生成」这个人工判断，在 Gradle 里等价于「生成物 task 的 inputs 快照是否变了」——834 个同步点变成由工具自动比对,不再依赖人的记忆。代价：必须为每个生成器把**全部**影响输出的输入声明完整（漏一个 = 该重跑不重跑，比手工同步更隐蔽）；另外 docs 明说 `lastModified`+`size` 不变就复用旧指纹，所以脚本若用「保留时间戳/回写mtime」的方式改文件，Gradle 会看不见——这是唯一比人工更弱的地方，迁移时必须避免这类写盘方式。

#### 机制名：跳过执行 → `UP-TO-DATE`（执行引擎里的判定点）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：执行引擎在 `ResolveChangesStep` 里先把「配置输入变更」「无历史」「未追踪」「校验失败」等情况折算成一组 rebuild reasons，再交给 `SkipUpToDateStep` 决定是跳过还是执行；`SkipUpToDateStep.execute()` 里 **只有 `context.getChanges()` 为空（且没有 rebuild reason）时才跳过**，跳过时生成 `Execution.skipped(UP_TO_DATE, work)` 并打日志 “Skipping … as it is up-to-date.”。用户可见的标签语义在任务结果标签表里：`UP-TO-DATE` = “The task's inputs and outputs haven't changed since the last run, so it was skipped.”
- **出处**：
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/steps/SkipUpToDateStep.java`（master）：`return context.getChanges().filter(__ -> reasons.isEmpty()).map(changes -> skipExecution(work, context)).orElseGet(() -> executeBecause(work, reasons, context));`；`LOGGER.info("Skipping {} as it is up-to-date.", work.getDisplayName());`；`Try<Execution> execution = Try.successful(Execution.skipped(UP_TO_DATE, work));`
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/Execution.java`（master）：`UP_TO_DATE` 的 Javadoc 原文 “The outputs haven't been changed, because the work is already up-to-date (i.e. its inputs and outputs match that of the previous execution in the same workspace).”
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/steps/ResolveChangesStep.java`（master）：`private static final ImmutableList<String> NO_HISTORY = ImmutableList.of("No history is available.");` / `UNTRACKED = ImmutableList.of("Change tracking is disabled.");` / `VALIDATION_FAILED = ImmutableList.of("Incremental execution has been disabled to ensure correctness. Please consult deprecation warnings for more details.");`，以及 `changeDetector.detectChanges(work, previousExecution, beforeExecution, incrementalInputProperties, context.getDetectedOverlappingOutputs().isPresent())`
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/gradle_optimizations.html#sec:task_outcome_labels`（章节 “Task Outcome Labels”）关键原句：“`UP-TO-DATE` … The task's inputs and outputs haven't changed since the last run, so it was skipped.”“`FROM-CACHE` … The task was skipped and its outputs were restored from the local or remote build cache.”“`NO-SOURCE` … The task had no source files to process (e.g., no Java files to compile), so it was skipped.”
- **对本场景**：可迁移。834 个同步点里真正需要重做的是闭包，其余是 `UP-TO-DATE` 空跑（几乎零成本），这就是「改 1 个源文件」不必再全量重生成 6 份的机制来源。代价：`UP-TO-DATE` 是**信任式**判定——一旦某个生成器的输入没声明完整，它会安静地跳过并留下过期产物；迁移时建议先跑一轮 `--info` 观察 “is not up-to-date because:” 的 reason 列表做核对。

#### 机制名：`TaskOutputs.upToDateWhen` 的语义

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：`upToDateWhen` 是**附加的谓词（additive predicate）**，不是「覆盖默认判定」：每次调用都往 `AndSpec` 上 and 上去，多个谓词全部为 true 才允许复用旧产物；任一为 false 则任务 out-of-date 并执行，**且不会从 build cache 加载任何输出**。谓词在**任务执行期**求值（传入 task 作为参数）。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/javadoc/org/gradle/api/tasks/TaskOutputs.html#upToDateWhen(org.gradle.api.specs.Spec)`（章节 “Method Details → upToDateWhen”）关键原句：“Adds a predicate to determine whether previous outputs of this task can be reused… If the spec returns false, previous outputs of this task cannot be reused and the task will be executed. That means the task is out-of-date and no outputs will be loaded from the build cache. You can add multiple such predicates. The task outputs cannot be reused when any predicate returns false.”
  - 源码 `subprojects/core/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/internal/tasks/DefaultTaskOutputs.java`（master）关键行：第 57 行 `private AndSpec<TaskInternal> upToDateSpec = AndSpec.empty();`；第 87–98 行 `upToDateWhen(final Closure upToDateClosure) { taskMutator.mutate("TaskOutputs.upToDateWhen(Closure)", () -> { upToDateSpec = upToDateSpec.and(upToDateClosure); }); }`（Spec 版本同理）
  - 源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/TaskOutputs.java`（master）：`void upToDateWhen(Closure upToDateClosure);` / `void upToDateWhen(Spec<? super Task> upToDateSpec);` / `boolean getHasOutput();` / `void cacheIf(Spec<? super Task> spec);` / `void doNotCacheIf(String cachingDisabledReason, Spec<? super Task> spec);`
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:custom_up_to_date_logic`（章节 “Providing custom up-to-date logic”）关键原句：“That's where the `upToDateWhen()` method on `TaskOutputs` comes in.”“Remember that your checks will always run if the task is in the execution task graph.”“One common mistake is to use `upToDateWhen()` instead of `Task.onlyIf()`.”
- **对本场景**：**不建议迁移使用**。本场景的诉求是「生成物是否与源一致」，这是标准 inputs/outputs 问题，用 `upToDateWhen` 反而是把手工同步逻辑搬进构建脚本。只有在生成物落在文件系统之外（例如写数据库）时才需要它。

#### 机制名：`upToDateWhen` 的坑（官方明确劝阻 + 共享输出目录的反直觉行为）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：官方「解决常见缓存问题」章节直接劝阻使用 `outputs.upToDateWhen`，理由与「不要根据任务是否执行来做构建逻辑」同源：从 build cache 加载输出同样会改变产物，用自定义谓词判断会与缓存语义冲突。第二个坑在 up-to-date 章节里：**若任务声明了输出目录，之后往该目录里新增的文件不会导致任务 out-of-date**（这是为了让不相关的任务共享输出目录），官方给的对策恰好还是 `upToDateWhen` —— 两条指引彼此留了缺口，实践中应优先用独立输出目录而不是 `upToDateWhen` 去补。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/common_caching_problems.html#logic_based_on_task_outcome`（章节 “Build logic based on the outcome of a task”）关键原句：“For the very same reason using `outputs.upToDateWhen` is discouraged and should be replaced by properly declaring the task's inputs.”“you should not assume that the output of a task can only change if it actually executed. Actually, loading the outputs from the build cache would also change them.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:how_does_it_work` 关键原句：“Note that if a task has an output directory specified, any files added to that directory since the last time it was executed are ignored and will NOT cause the task to be out of date. This is so unrelated tasks may share an output directory without interfering with each other. If this is not the behaviour you want for some reason, consider using `TaskOutputs.upToDateWhen(groovy.lang.Closure)`”
- **对本场景**：可迁移为**约束条件**：6 份生成物应当各有独立、互不重叠的输出位置；不要把 6 份产物混写进同一个目录，否则「新增文件不导致 out-of-date」这一条会让你漏掉同步。迁移代价为零（只是目录规划纪律），但违反它会产生难查的漏同步。

#### 机制名：实现不可追踪时缓存整体失效

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：跟踪任务/动作/嵌套输入的实现时，Gradle 用「类名 + 实现所在 classpath 的标识」。有两种情况无法精确追踪：**未知 classloader**（classloader 非 Gradle 创建，无法确定 classpath）与 **Java lambda**（lambda 类名运行时生成、每次不同）。此时 **Gradle 对该任务关闭一切缓存**：任务永远既不 up-to-date 也不从 build cache 加载。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:how_does_it_work` 关键原句：“For tracking the implementation of tasks, task actions and nested inputs, Gradle uses the class name and an identifier for the classpath which contains the implementation.”“When the implementation of a task, task action or a nested input cannot be tracked precisely, Gradle disables any caching for the task. That means that the task will never be up-to-date or loaded from the build cache.”
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/caching/CachingDisabledReasonCategory.java`（master）枚举项 Javadoc：“`NON_CACHEABLE_INPUTS` — One of the work's inputs is not cacheable. Reasons for non-cacheable inputs: some type used as an input is loaded via an unknown classloader, a Java lambda was used as an input, an input is not tracked.”（并 `@see <a href="https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:how_does_it_work">How fingerprinting works</a>`）
- **对本场景**：迁移时的**隐性代价**。如果生成器逻辑是用闭包/lambda 传给 Gradle（例如在构建脚本里写 `doFirst { … }`），缓存会被静默关闭，退化成「每次都跑」。迁移时生成逻辑要落在**具体的 task 类/插件**里，而不是构建脚本的闭包。

#### 机制名：输入/输出注解不全 → 全量降级

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：运行时校验发现任务类型的注解有问题（注解用在不兼容类型上、注解写在 setter 上、以及**任何没有 input/output 注解的 getter 会被标记**）时，任务会被以**无任何优化**的方式执行：它不能 up-to-date、不能读写 build cache、不能并行、不能增量执行，且执行前会失效 Virtual File System 的内存状态。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_validation`（章节 “Validation at runtime”）关键原句：“Any getter not annotated with an input/output annotation is also flagged.”“Tasks that have a validation warning are executed without any optimizations. Specifically, they never can be: up-to-date, loaded from or stored in the build cache, executed in parallel with other tasks, even if parallel execution is enabled, executed incrementally.”
- **对本场景**：可迁移为**验收项**。迁移后应把「校验零告警」当作发布门禁：只要有一个 getter 忘注解，整个生成器就永久全量跑，看起来"能用"但增量收益全丢。

---

### 5.2 输入输出注解：各自如何影响 snapshot / hash，以及什么不被追踪

#### 机制名：注解总表（`@Input` / `@InputFile` / `@InputDirectory` / `@InputFiles` / `@OutputFile` / `@OutputDirectory` 等）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：官方给出「增量构建属性类型注解」总表，规定每个注解允许的属性类型与语义。要点：`@Input` 接受任何 `Serializable` 类型或依赖解析结果（**简单的值输入**，非文件）；`@InputFile`/`@InputDirectory` 各为单个文件/单个目录；`@InputFiles` 是文件与目录的可迭代集合，**文件顺序不参与 up-to-date 判断**；`@OutputFile`/`@OutputDirectory`/`@OutputFiles`/`@OutputDirectories` 是声明产物位置，**用 file tree 作为输出会关闭该任务的缓存**；`@Internal`、`@Console`、`@ReplacedBy` 表示既不输入也不输出；`@SkipWhenEmpty`、`@Incremental`、`@PathSensitive`、`@IgnoreEmptyDirectories`、`@NormalizeLineEndings`、`@Optional` 是行为修饰。注解必须放在 getter（Groovy 属性）上，**放在 setter 上或没有对应注解 getter 的 Java 字段上会被忽略**。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_output_annotations`（章节 “Declaring inputs and outputs via annotations”，含 “Table 1. Incremental build property type annotations”）关键原句：“Annotations must be placed on getters or on Groovy properties. Annotations placed on setters, or on a Java field without a corresponding annotated getter, are ignored.”；`@InputFiles` 行：“An iterable of input files and directories. The order of the files is not considered for up-to-date checks.”；`@Internal` 行：“Indicates that the property is used internally but is neither an input nor an output.”；`@PathSensitive` 行：“Used with any input file property to tell Gradle to only consider the given part of the file paths as important.”
  - 源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/Input.java`（master）Javadoc：“This will cause the task to be considered out-of-date when the property has changed. This annotation cannot be used on a `File` object.”；`InputFile.java`：“This will cause the task to be considered out-of-date when the file path or contents have changed.”；`InputDirectory.java`：“This will cause the task to be considered out-of-date when the directory location or contents have changed.” + “This annotation implies `@IgnoreEmptyDirectories`.”；`InputFiles.java`：“This will cause the task to be considered out-of-date when the file paths or contents have changed. The order of the files does not affect up-to-date checks.”；`OutputFile.java`：“This will cause the task to be considered out-of-date when the file path or contents are different to when the task was last run.”；`OutputDirectory.java`：“This will cause the task to be considered out-of-date when the directory path or task output to that directory has been modified since the task was last run.”；`OutputFiles.java`：“Task output caching will be disabled if the outputs contain file trees.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_output_runtime_api`（章节 “Declaring inputs and outputs via the runtime API”）：`Task.getInputs()`（`TaskInputs`）、`Task.getOutputs()`（`TaskOutputs`）、`Task.getDestroyables()`；“In fact, the runtime API has almost feature parity with the annotations. It lacks equivalents for `@Nested`、`@Classpath`、`@CompileClasspath`、`@LocalState`、`@ReplacedBy`、`@Internal`”
  - 源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/TaskInputs.java`（master）：`TaskInputFilePropertyBuilder files(@Nullable Object... paths);` / `file(Object path);` / `dir(Object dirPath);`；“Registers an input directory hierarchy. All files found under the given directory are treated as input files for this task.”
  - 源码 `subprojects/core/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/internal/tasks/DefaultTaskInputs.java`（master）关键行 110/127/137/172：`public TaskInputFilePropertyBuilderInternal files(final Object... paths)` / `file(final Object path)` / `dir(final Object dirPath)` / `property(final String name, @Nullable final Object value)` —— 运行时 API 与注解在内部落到同一套属性注册上。
- **对本场景**：可迁移。本场景的 6 份生成物本质上就是 6 个（或更多）`@OutputFile`/`@OutputDirectory`；源文件是 `@InputFiles`，生成参数（模板版本、包名等）是 `@Input`。**注意 `@OutputFiles` 若用 file tree 会关闭缓存**，所以宁可多声明几个显式的 `@OutputFile`，也不要用一个 file tree 一把抓——对本场景的 834 个同步点，显式逐产物声明正好是想要的粒度。代价：需要对每个生成物补一条显式输出声明（一次性工作，但 139 包 × 6 份会放大声明量）。

#### 机制名：`@Classpath` / `@CompileClasspath`（classpath 专用归一化）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：classpath 属性与普通 `@InputFiles` 有两处根本差异：① **条目顺序参与比较**（顺序变化即 out-of-date）；② classpath 上目录与 jar 的**名字与路径被忽略**，jar 内 class/resource 的**时间戳与条目顺序也被忽略**，因此「用不同文件日期重建 jar」不会让任务 out-of-date。`@Classpath` 的 jar 归一化路径为空、内容归一化，且**递归作用于目录内与其他 jar 内嵌套的 jar**；目录作为 classpath 条目时根目录本身被忽略、目录内文件排序后用相对路径。`@CompileClasspath` 更进一步：按 “Java compile avoidance” 抽取 ABI 签名，实现变更、资源/manifest 变更、私有成员变更、调试信息变更、目录变更都被忽略。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_using_classpath_annotations`（章节 “Using the classpath annotations”）关键原句：“As opposed to input properties annotated with `@InputFiles`, for classpath properties the order of the entries in the file collection matter.”“Timestamps and the order of class files and resources inside jar files on a classpath are ignored, too, thus recreating a jar file with different file dates will not make the task out of date.”
  - 同页 Table 1 的 `@CompileClasspath` 行列出被忽略的变化类型：“Changes to timestamps and the order of entries in Jars.”“Changes to resources and Jar manifests…”“Changes to code, such as method bodies, static initializers and field initializers (except for constants).”
  - 源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/Classpath.java`（master）Javadoc：“For jar files, the normalized path is empty. The content of the jar file is normalized so that time stamps and order of the zip entries in the jar file do not matter.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:configure_input_normalization`（章节 “Configure input normalization”）关键原句：“Normalization applies to all zip files on the classpath (e.g. jars, wars, aars, apks, etc). This allows Gradle to recognize when two zip files are functionally the same, even though the zip files themselves might be slightly different due to metadata (such as timestamps or file order).”
- **对本场景**：**部分可迁移且价值高**。本场景的「生成物」若也是 zip/jar 类打包物（`139 包` 听起来像包），归一化能保证「内容相同但时间戳/顺序不同」不触发下游重算——这正是让「改 1 个源文件」只影响真正受影响的包的前提。代价：归一化只覆盖 classpath/zip 语义；非 zip 的生成物若要获得同等待遇，得靠第 4 节的字节可复现或第 2 节的 `@NormalizeLineEndings`。

#### 机制名：`@Nested`（嵌套输入，含实现追踪与命名规则）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：`@Nested` 用于把自定义类型（不必 `Serializable`）的自身属性当作输入/输出纳入快照；Gradle 按**运行时实际类型**发现子属性。嵌套类型自身的**类型与 classpath 也被追踪**（所以嵌套实现变更会让构建 out-of-date），因此可以把用户提供的代码（例如 `Action`）当输入。`Provider` 的取值被当作嵌套输入；`Iterable` 的每个元素是独立嵌套输入，默认命名为 `$` + 索引（实现 `Named` 则用其 name，实际源码为 `name$index` 形式），**元素顺序对可靠性与缓存至关重要**，名字重复不允许；`Map` 的每个 value 是独立嵌套输入、key 作名字。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_nested_inputs`（章节 “Nested inputs”）关键原句：“When analyzing `@Nested` task properties for declared input and output sub-properties Gradle uses the type of the actual value.”“The type and classpath of nested inputs is tracked, too. This ensures that changes to the implementation of a nested input causes the build to be out of date.”“The ordering of the elements in the iterable is crucial for reliable up-to-date checks and caching if not all of the elements implement `Named`.”
  - 源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/Nested.java`（master）Javadoc：“The implementation of the nested bean is tracked as an input, too. This allows tracking behavior such as `Action`s as task inputs.”“As a property name, the index of the element in the iterable prefixed by `$` is used, e.g. `$0`. If the element implements `Named`, then the property name is composed of `Named.getName()` and the index, e.g. `name$1`.”
- **对本场景**：可迁移。139 个包的生成参数（每包的模板、变量、目标路径）适合建模成 `@Nested` 的类型化对象，而不是拼成一个 string。代价：一旦用 `Iterable` 传参数集合，**顺序必须稳定**，否则每次构建都被判定 out-of-date——迁移时要固定遍历顺序。

#### 机制名：`@PathSensitive` / `PathSensitivity`（路径敏感度决定 hash 里包含哪部分路径）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：`PathSensitive` 决定文件路径的哪部分参与 up-to-date 与 build cache key 计算。**未标注时默认 `ABSOLUTE`**。`ABSOLUTE` = 完整路径（官方警告这会让产物**无法跨工作区经 build cache 共享**）；`RELATIVE` = 相对所属层级的路径，根目录下的文件用文件名、根目录下的目录用空串；`NAME_ONLY` = 只看文件名/目录名（**移动文件而不改内容不会 out-of-date**）；`NONE` = 完全忽略路径（且用于 `@Incremental` 输入时，Gradle 可能把 `MODIFIED` 拆成 `ADDED` + `REMOVED` 事件）。内部实现上，路径敏感度被映射为一组 normalizer 枚举。
- **出处**：
  - 源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/PathSensitive.java`（master）Javadoc：“If a `Task` declares a file property without this annotation, the default is `PathSensitivity.ABSOLUTE`.”（方法 `PathSensitivity value();`）
  - 源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/PathSensitivity.java`（master）：`ABSOLUTE` — “Consider the full path of files and directories. This will prevent the task's outputs from being shared across different workspaces via the build cache.”；`RELATIVE` — “For files in the root of the file collection, the file name is used as the normalized path. For directories in the root of the file collection, an empty string is used as normalized path.”；`NAME_ONLY` — “Consider only the name of files and directories.”；`NONE` — “Ignore file paths and directories altogether. When used on an `@Incremental` input, instead of `ChangeType.MODIFIED` events Gradle may produce `ChangeType.ADDED` and `ChangeType.REMOVED` events.”
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/model/InputNormalizer.java`（master）关键行：`public enum InputNormalizer implements FileNormalizer { ABSOLUTE_PATH(false), RELATIVE_PATH(false), NAME_ONLY(false), IGNORE_PATH(true), RUNTIME_CLASSPATH(true), COMPILE_CLASSPATH(true); }` 与 `public static FileNormalizer determineNormalizerForPathSensitivity(PathSensitivity pathSensitivity) { switch (pathSensitivity) { case NONE: return IGNORE_PATH; case NAME_ONLY: return NAME_ONLY; case RELATIVE: return RELATIVE_PATH; case ABSOLUTE: return ABSOLUTE_PATH; …`
  - 源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/TaskInputFilePropertyBuilder.java`（master）：`TaskInputFilePropertyBuilder withPathSensitivity(PathSensitivity sensitivity);` — “Sets which part of the path of files should be considered during up-to-date checks and build cache key calculations.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:task_output_caching_inputs` 关键原句（章节 “Declaring task inputs and outputs”）：“We suggest adding tests to ensure that the task inputs are relocatable, i.e. that the task can be loaded from the cache into a different build directory (see `@PathSensitive`).”
- **对本场景**：**迁移的关键参数**。本场景是「多包多生成物、同一台机器/同一仓库内同步」，路径敏感度直接决定 hash 稳定性：① 若生成物会移动位置或跨工作区复用 → 必须 `RELATIVE`（`@CacheableTask` 的官方示例正是为此显式设置）；② 若只是想「内容变了才重生成」→ `RELATIVE`/`NAME_ONLY` 明显优于默认 `ABSOLUTE`。代价：默认是 `ABSOLUTE`，**不显式声明就会把绝对路径掺进 hash**，导致缓存完全无法跨目录复用；这是一处必须逐属性处理的迁移成本。

#### 机制名：`@Input` 值的快照（非文件输入如何进 hash）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：非文件输入走 `ValueSnapshot`（`ExecutionInputState.getInputProperties()` 返回 `ImmutableSortedMap<String, ValueSnapshot>`），与文件输入的 `FileCollectionFingerprint` 分开存放、一并进快照。`@Input` 的具体类型必须可序列化；官方明确：**`@Input` 不能用在 `java.io.File` 上**（要表达「只关心路径不关心内容」应返回路径字符串；要关心内容则用 `@InputFile`/`@InputDirectory`）。
- **出处**：
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/history/ExecutionInputState.java`（master）：`ImmutableSortedMap<String, ValueSnapshot> getInputProperties();`（注释 “The non-file inputs.”）
  - 源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/Input.java`（master）Javadoc：“This annotation cannot be used on a `java.io.File` object. If you want to refer to the file path, independently of its contents, return a `String` instead which returns the absolute path.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_output_annotations` Table 1：`@Input` — “Any `Serializable` type or dependency resolution result types”
- **对本场景**：可迁移。6 份生成物的生成参数（版本号、开关、目标路径）应当作为 `@Input` 声明而不是藏在脚本变量里。代价：几乎没有，但要警惕「非确定性输入」——若把时间戳/构建号当输入，任务将永久 out-of-date 且缓存失效。

#### 机制名：默认不被追踪的东西（等价于所请求的 “What is not tracked”）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：Gradle 官方的“显式不追踪”机制是注解与 API，而不是一个叫 “What is not tracked” 的章节：① `@Internal` —— 属性不参与 up-to-date；② `@Console` —— 只影响控制台输出；③ `@ReplacedBy` —— 属性已被替代、当输入输出忽略；④ `Task.doNotTrackState()` —— 整个任务不做 up-to-date 检查；⑤ `@UntrackedTask(because = …)` —— 类级声明任务不可追踪（等价于 doNotTrackState），此时 Gradle 不做任何优化：**永远 out-of-date、永不读写 build cache**，且 **`InputChanges` 不能用于 untracked 任务**；⑥ `@IgnoreEmptyDirectories` —— 目录结构变化（增删重命名空目录）不算变更；⑦ `@NormalizeLineEndings` —— 换行符差异被归一化；⑧ 未加任何 input/output 注解的 getter → 触发校验告警并让任务失去全部优化；⑨ `@OutputFiles`/`@OutputDirectories` 用 file tree → 关闭该任务缓存；⑩ `@LocalState` 声明的文件在从缓存加载时会被删除。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:disable-state-tracking`（章节 “Disabling up-to-date checks”）关键原句：“That's where the `doNotTrackState()` method on `Task` comes in.”“If you are writing your own task that always should run, then you can also use the `@UntrackedTask` annotation on the task class instead of calling `Task.doNotTrackState()`.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:untracked_external_tool`（章节 “Integrate an external tool which does its own up-to-date checking”）关键原句：“Sometimes you want to integrate an external tool like Git or Npm, both of which do their own up-to-date checking. In that case it doesn't make much sense for Gradle to also do up-to-date checks. You can disable Gradle's up-to-date checks by using the `@UntrackedTask` annotation on the task wrapping the tool.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/javadoc/org/gradle/api/tasks/UntrackedTask.html`（章节 “Annotation Type UntrackedTask”）关键原句：“If a task is untracked, then Gradle will not try to capture its state. That also means that Gradle does not do any optimizations for running the task. For example, such a task will always be out of date and never stored in or loaded from the build cache.”“`InputChanges` cannot be used for untracked tasks, since incremental tasks need to track the state of their inputs and outputs for them to be correct.”
  - 源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/Internal.java`（master）Javadoc：“Attached to a task property to indicate that the property is not to be taken into account for up-to-date checking.”“This will cause the task *not* to be considered out-of-date when the property has changed.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_output_annotations` Table 1：`@Console` — “Indicates that the property is neither an input nor an output.”；`@ReplacedBy` — “Indicates that the property has been replaced by another and should be ignored as an input or output.”；`@IgnoreEmptyDirectories` — “instruct Gradle to track only changes to the contents of directories and not differences in the directories themselves.”；`@NormalizeLineEndings` — “instruct Gradle to normalize line endings when calculating up-to-date checks or build cache keys.”；`@LocalState` — “Specifies one or more files that represent the local state of the task. These files are removed when the task is loaded from cache.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_validation`：“Any getter not annotated with an input/output annotation is also flagged.”
- **对本场景**：可迁移，且是**排除法工具**。本场景里那些「确实由外部系统负责一致性」的生成物（例如由另一个工具自己维护状态、或有不可读文件、或输出位置不独占）应当显式声明为 `@UntrackedTask`/`doNotTrackState`——那等于承认"这一份同步仍需人工/外部保证"，从而让 Gradle 的增量承诺只覆盖它真能保证的部分。代价：`@UntrackedTask` 会**放弃**该任务的全部优化与 build cache，是永久成本。

#### 机制名（未证实）：「What is not tracked」章节不存在于 Gradle 官方文档

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：**未证实：查过下列全部一手页面/版本，均未出现 “What is not tracked” 或 “not tracked” 作为章节名或正文短语**，唯一命中是 `@UntrackedTask` 注解与 `Task.doNotTrackState()`（见上一条）。因此本文件用上一条的十条「显式不追踪」机制来回答该问题；若该章节名来自 Gradle 之外的构建系统文档，在 Gradle 侧需要以那条机制替代。
- **出处**（均为实际抓取并 grep）：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html`、`…/build_cache.html`、`…/more_about_tasks.html` 在版本 `current`(9.7.1)、`9.0`、`8.7`、`8.0`、`7.6.4`、`7.5` 下 grep `not tracked` 全空；
  - 版本 `7.0.2`、`6.9.4`、`6.0.1`、`5.6.4`、`4.10.3` 的 `incremental_build.html`、`more_about_tasks.html` grep `not tracked` 全空；
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/working_with_files.html`、`…/common_caching_problems.html`、`…/gradle_optimizations.html` grep 亦无。
  - 反向确认：`https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:untracked_external_tool` 存在，章节名是 “Integrate an external tool which does its own up-to-date checking”。
- **对本场景**：不适用（只是取证结论）。若下游汇报需要引用「什么不被追踪」，请引用「显式不追踪的十条机制」那一节，不要引用不存在的章节名。

---

### 5.3 build cache：cache key、开关、命中跳过、污染与重叠输出

#### 机制名：build cache key 由哪些输入算出

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：任务声明了全部输入输出后，Gradle 计算一个 **build cache key** 唯一标识该任务的输出，用 key 去 cache 取/存。官方列出的计入 key 的输入是：**任务类型及其 classpath**、**输出属性的名字**、**有注解的属性名与值**、**通过运行时 `TaskInputs` 添加的属性名与值**、**Gradle 发行版 + `buildSrc` + 插件的 classpath**、**构建脚本中影响该任务执行的内容**。关键推论（官方同章明确）：**任务路径不是 build cache key 的输入**，所以不同 task path 的任务只要执行结果相同就能互相复用输出。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:task_output_caching_details`（章节 “Cacheable tasks”）关键原句：“Since a task describes all of its inputs and outputs, Gradle can compute a build cache key that uniquely defines the task's outputs based on its inputs.”“The following inputs contribute to the build cache key for a task in the same way that they do for up-to-date checks: The task type and its classpath / The names of the output properties / The names and values of properties annotated as described in the section called "Custom task types" / The names and values of properties added by the DSL via `TaskInputs` / The classpath of the Gradle distribution, `buildSrc` and plugins / The content of the build script when it affects execution of the task”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:task_output_caching_inputs`（章节 “Declaring task inputs and outputs”）关键原句：“The task path is not an input to the build cache key. This means that tasks with different task paths can re-use each other's outputs as long as Gradle determines that executing them yields the same result.”
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/caching/CachingStateFactory.java`（master）：`CachingState createCachingState(BeforeExecutionState beforeExecutionState, HashCode cacheKey, ImmutableList<CachingDisabledReason> cachingDisabledReasons);` 与 `HashCode calculateCacheKey(BeforeExecutionState beforeExecutionState);` —— cache key 就是一个 `HashCode`，其计算入口只接收 `BeforeExecutionState`（即上一条的输入快照集合）。
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/caching/CachingState.java`（master）：`public static CachingState enabled(BuildCacheKey key, BeforeExecutionState beforeExecutionState)`、`public BuildCacheKey getKey()`、`private final BuildCacheKey key;`
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/javadoc/org/gradle/caching/BuildCacheKey.html`（章节 “Interface BuildCacheKey”）关键原句：“Cache key identifying an entry in the build cache.”；方法 `String getHashCode();`“Returns the string representation of the cache key.” 与 `byte[] toByteArray();`
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:how_does_it_work`：“The inputs for the task are also used to calculate the build cache key used to load task outputs when enabled.”
- **对本场景**：**可迁移，且是本场景最值钱的机制**。「改 1 个源文件 → 139 个包里只有少数几个真正受影响的包需要重生成」这件事，靠的就是「key 由输入闭包算出」：未受影响的包 key 不变，直接复用旧产物或从 cache 取。代价：key 里含**Gradle/插件/buildSrc 的 classpath 和「影响执行的构建脚本内容」**，因此迁移后会多一个敏感面——构建脚本一改，所有包都可能被认为 key 变了（官方在 “Review usages of doFirst and doLast” 里也正是因此劝阻把逻辑写在构建脚本闭包里）。

#### 机制名：本地 cache 与远程 cache 的启用方式

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：**build cache 默认关闭**。开启方式二选一：命令行 `--build-cache`（仅本次构建生效）；或在 `gradle.properties` 写 `org.gradle.caching=true`（对所有构建生效，除非用 `--no-build-cache` 显式关闭）。开启后输出存在 Gradle User Home。可分别配置 `local` 与 `remote`：两者都启用时**先查本地、未命中再查远程，远程命中后会写回本地**；只有 `BuildCache.isPush()` 为 `true` 的 cache 才会被写入，默认**本地 push 开启、远程 push 关闭**（官方推荐的 CI 用法是 CI 做 push、开发者只读）。本地 cache 由 `DirectoryBuildCache` 实现并在 `settings.gradle` 的 `buildCache { }` 块里配置目录与 `enabled`/`push`；远程用 `HttpBuildCache`，按 `GET/PUT https://…/cache/<cache-key>` 交互。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:build_cache_enable`（章节 “Enable the Build Cache”）关键原句：“By default, the build cache is not enabled.”“Run with `--build-cache` on the command-line… Put `org.gradle.caching=true` in your `gradle.properties`”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:build_cache_configure`（章节 “Configure the Build Cache”）关键原句：“When both build caches are enabled, Gradle tries to load build outputs from the local build cache first, and then tries the remote build cache if no build outputs are found.”“Gradle stores ("pushes") build outputs in any build cache that is enabled and has `BuildCache.isPush()` set to `true`. By default, the local build cache has push enabled, and the remote build cache has push disabled.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:build_cache_configure_remote`（章节 “Remote HTTP build cache”）关键原句：“When attempting to load an entry, a `GET` request is made to `https://example.com:8123/cache/«cache-key»`.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/command_line_interface.html#sec:command_line_performance`（章节 “Performance options”）关键原句：“`--build-cache`, `--no-build-cache` Toggles the Gradle Build Cache. Gradle will try to reuse outputs from previous builds. Default is off.”
- **对本场景**：可迁移但**先只用本地**。834 个同步点在同一台机器/同一仓库内，本地 cache 收益已经很大；远程 cache 的收益主要在 CI 与多机，代价是要部署 HTTP backend（Gradle 提供 Docker 版 cache node）并处理凭证/TLS/重定向。迁移成本分两档：本地 cache = 一个 property；远程 cache = 一套基础设施。

#### 机制名：`@CacheableTask` 与 `@DisableCachingByDefault`（谁有资格被缓存）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：**自定义任务类型默认不可缓存**，必须用类级 `@CacheableTask` 显式 opt-in；该注解**不被子类继承**。内置任务里当前可缓存的只有：Java 工具链（`JavaCompile`、`Javadoc`）、Groovy（`GroovyCompile`、`Groovydoc`）、Scala（`ScalaCompile`、`ScalaDoc`）、Native（`CppCompile`、`CCompile`、`SwiftCompile`）、测试（`Test`）、代码质量（`Checkstyle`、`CodeNarc`、`Pmd`）、JaCoCo（`JacocoReport`），以及 `AntlrTask`/`ValidatePlugins`/`WriteProperties`；**其余内置任务都不可缓存**（`Copy`/`Jar` 这类“只搬文件”的任务通常没必要缓存）。反向声明用 `@DisableCachingByDefault`（可附人类可读原因，且可与 `@CacheableTask` 同用，只用于文档化默认决定），而**构建逻辑可以用运行时 API 覆盖这个决定**：`outputs.cacheIf { … }` 打开、`outputs.doNotCacheIf(…)` 关闭。官方对 `@CacheableTask` 的资格要求写得很直白：“Only tasks that produce reproducible and relocatable output should be marked with `CacheableTask`.”
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:task_output_caching_cacheable_tasks`（章节 “Built-in cacheable tasks”）关键原句：“Currently, the following built-in Gradle tasks are cacheable: … All other built-in tasks are currently not cacheable.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:task_output_caching_details` 关键原句：“Task types need to opt-in to task output caching using the `@CacheableTask` annotation. Note that `@CacheableTask` is not inherited by subclasses. Custom task types are not cacheable by default.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:enable_caching_of_non_cacheable_tasks`（章节 “Enable caching of non-cacheable tasks”）与其子章节 `#using_annotations`、`#using_the_runtime_api` 关键原句：“`@CacheableTask` … This is sufficient to make the task cacheable on your own machine. However, input files are identified by default by their absolute path. So if the cache needs to be shared between several developers or machines using different paths, that won't work as expected. So we also need to set the path sensitivity.”；以及 “For enabling caching for the task you need to use the `TaskOutputs.cacheIf()` method.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:task_output_caching_disabled_by_default`（章节 “Marking tasks as non-cacheable by default”）关键原句：“You can signify that a task is not to be cached by adding the `@DisableCachingByDefault` annotation to it.”“This annotation is only for documenting the reason behind not caching the task by default. Build logic can override this decision via the runtime API”
  - 源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/CacheableTask.java`（master）Javadoc：“Attached to a task type to indicate that task output caching should be enabled by default for tasks of this type. Only tasks that produce reproducible and relocatable output should be marked with `CacheableTask`.”
  - 源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/TaskOutputs.java`（master）：`void cacheIf(Spec<? super Task> spec);` / `void doNotCacheIf(String cachingDisabledReason, Spec<? super Task> spec);`
- **对本场景**：**迁移的第一道门槛**。本场景的 6 份生成物如果是自定义生成器，默认**完全不可缓存**——必须显式打 `@CacheableTask` 并满足「输出可复现且可重定位（relocatable）」这一硬条件。代价：可重定位意味着必须配 `@PathSensitive(RELATIVE)`；可复现意味着第 4 节的字节级可复现工作必须先做完，否则缓存即使命中也可能拿到与本地不一致的产物。

#### 机制名：缓存命中即跳过执行（`FROM-CACHE`）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：`BuildCacheStep` 是执行链里的一步：有可用 cache key 时先尝试 `buildCache.load(cacheKey, …)`，**命中就不再执行任务体**（`Try.successful(Execution.skipped(FROM_CACHE, work))`，用户看到 `FROM-CACHE`），并把加载到的产物写回工作区、清掉该任务的 `@LocalState`；未命中才走 `executeAndStoreInCache`。加载前会先 `fileSystemAccess.invalidate(cacheableLocations)` 失效 VFS 里这些输出位置的内存状态，避免读到自己的旧快照。
- **出处**：
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/steps/BuildCacheStep.java`（master）关键行：第 96 行 `Try<Execution> execution = Try.successful(Execution.skipped(FROM_CACHE, work));`；第 91 行 `LOGGER.info("Loaded cache entry for {} with cache key {}", work.getDisplayName(), cacheKey.getHashCode());`；第 104 行 `.orElseGet(() -> executeAndStoreInCache(cacheableWork, cacheKey, context))`；第 120–123 行 `private Optional<BuildCacheLoadResult> tryLoadingFromCache(BuildCacheKey cacheKey, CacheableWork cacheableWork) { Collection<String> cacheableLocations = cacheableWork.work.getCachedOutputLocationsForInvalidation(cacheableWork.workspace); fileSystemAccess.invalidate(cacheableLocations); return buildCache.load(cacheKey, cacheableWork); }`；第 142 行 `LOGGER.debug("Did not find cache entry for {} with cache key {}, executing instead", …)`
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/Execution.java`（master）：`FROM_CACHE` 的 Javadoc “The outputs of the work have been loaded from the build cache.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/gradle_optimizations.html#sec:task_outcome_labels`：“`FROM-CACHE` — The task was skipped and its outputs were restored from the local or remote build cache.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:task_output_caching_example`（章节 “What does it look like”）关键原句：“Now we see that, instead of executing the `:compileJava` task, the outputs of the task have been loaded from the build cache.”
- **对本场景**：可迁移。这直接消灭「同一份内容重复生成 6 遍」里的重复工作：只要输入闭包相同，第二次以后是 `FROM-CACHE`/`UP-TO-DATE` 而不是重新执行生成脚本。代价：需要 `clean` 之后仍能正确重建（官方前置条件原文见下一条），也就是生成器不能依赖上一次执行留下的中间态。

#### 机制名：前置条件——必须先满足增量构建语义

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：官方把「连续跑两次、所有有输出的任务都应 `UP-TO-DATE`」列为**启用 task output caching 的硬前置**，否则既不能期待更快、也不能期待正确。
- **出处**：`https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:task_output_caching`（章节 “Task Output Caching”）关键原句：“For your build to play well with task output caching it must work well with the incremental build feature. For example, when running your build twice in a row all tasks with outputs should be `UP-TO-DATE`. You cannot expect faster builds or correct builds when enabling task output caching when this prerequisite is not met.”
- **对本场景**：可迁移为**迁移验收门禁**。本场景的验收动作应当是：连续两次跑同一目标，输出侧除第一次外应全 `UP-TO-DATE`；若某个生成器永远不是 `UP-TO-DATE`，说明它有未声明的输入或非确定性输出，必须先修好再谈缓存收益。

#### 机制名：缓存污染（缺输入 = 错误命中）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：缓存正确性完全取决于「输入输出声明是否完整」。官方列出三类后果：**漏声明输入 → 错误命中（incorrect cache hits）**，不同结果因为共用同一 key 而被当成同一份；**漏声明输出 → 构建失败**（Gradle 未完整捕获输出）；**错误声明输入（含易变数据或绝对路径）→ 缓存未命中**。并给出两条防污染手段：用集成测试（例如 TestKit）验证「同输入产出同输出」且「输出文件被完整捕获」，以及保证输入可重定位。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:task_output_caching_inputs`（章节 “Declaring task inputs and outputs”）关键原句：“It is very important that a cacheable task has a complete picture of its inputs and outputs, so that the results from one build can be safely re-used somewhere else. Missing task inputs can cause incorrect cache hits, where different results are treated as identical because the same cache key is used by both executions. Missing task outputs can cause build failures if Gradle does not completely capture all outputs for a given task. Wrongly declared task inputs can lead to cache misses especially when containing volatile data or absolute paths.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/common_caching_problems.html`（章节首段）关键原句：“With cacheable tasks incorrect results are stored permanently, and can come back to haunt you later; re-running with `clean` won't help in this situation either. When using a shared cache, these problems even cross machine boundaries.”
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/caching/CachingDisabledReasonCategory.java` 枚举 Javadoc：“`NON_CACHEABLE_OUTPUT` — Work has declared output that is not cacheable. Reasons for non-cacheable outputs: an output contains a file tree, an output is not tracked.”
- **对本场景**：**这是迁移后最危险的新失败模式**。手工同步时漏同步是"看得见"的（文件不对），缓存污染是"看不见"的（拿到另一份输入产出的缓存结果）。对 834 个同步点，迁移必须先为每个生成器做一次输入闭包盘点；建议按官方建议补集成测试，把「同一输入两次产出字节相同」写成断言。

#### 机制名：overlapping outputs（重叠输出）的处理

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：多个任务的输出位置重叠时，Gradle 会**关闭后执行那个任务的 task output caching**，以免把别的任务的输出当成自己的产物收进错误的 cache key；**后续构建中若检测到输出重叠，还会阻止从 cache 加载会删掉别的任务既有产物的输出**。诊断方式是 `--info` 日志级别，或看 Build Scan/Develocity 的 build timeline 与 task input comparison。官方给出的解决模式是：**加一个 `Sync` 任务把合并后的输出同步到目标目录，而原始任务保持可缓存**。内部实现上，`OverlappingOutputDetector` 通过比对上一次与当前的输出根快照来定位重叠，报告形如 “output property '%s' with path '%s'”；禁用缓存的分类原因里就有 `OVERLAPPING_OUTPUTS`（“Work's outputs overlap with other work's.”）。另外 up-to-date 侧对输出目录放宽：目录里新增的文件不算 out-of-date（为了让不相关任务共享目录）。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/4.0.1/release-notes.html`（章节 “Detecting overlapping task outputs”）关键原句：“When two tasks write into the same directory, Gradle will now disable task output caching for the second task to execute. This prevents issues where task outputs for a different task are captured for the wrong build cache key. On subsequent builds, if overlapping outputs are detected, Gradle will also prevent you from loading task outputs from the cache if it would remove existing outputs from another task.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/common_caching_problems.html#overlapping_outputs`（章节 “Overlapping outputs”）关键原句：“You already saw that overlapping outputs are a problem for task output caching. When you add new tasks to your build or re-configure built-in tasks make sure you do not create overlapping outputs for cacheable tasks. If you must you can add a `Sync` task which then would sync the merged outputs into the target directory while the original tasks remain cacheable.”
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/caching/CachingDisabledReasonCategory.java`：`OVERLAPPING_OUTPUTS,` 且 Javadoc “Work's outputs overlap with other work's.”
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/history/impl/DefaultOverlappingOutputDetector.java`（master）关键行：第 47–50 行 `// If the root hashes are the same there are no overlapping outputs` + `if (getRootHashes(previousSnapshot).equals(getRootHashes(currentSnapshot))) { continue; }`；第 113 行 `// Created since last execution, possibly by another task`；第 117 行 `return !snapshot.isContentUpToDate(previousSnapshot);`
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/history/OverlappingOutputs.java`（master）：`public String toString() { return String.format("output property '%s' with path '%s'", propertyName, overlappedFilePath); }`
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/history/OverlappingOutputDetector.java`：`OverlappingOutputs detect(ImmutableSortedMap<String, FileSystemSnapshot> previous, ImmutableSortedMap<String, FileSystemSnapshot> current);`
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_output_parallelism`（章节 “Task parallelism”）关键原句：“Gradle will inspect the outputs of tasks when selecting the next task to run and will avoid concurrent execution of tasks that write to the same output directory.”
- **对本场景**：**可迁移为硬约束**。6 份生成物如果都往同一个目录里写，后写的那个任务的缓存会被自动关闭（收益丢失），且共享目录里"新增文件不算变更"会让漏同步更难发现。迁移必须做到**每份生成物有独立、互不重叠的输出位置**。代价：需要重新规划 834 个同步点的目标路径；如果业务上确实必须合并，官方模式是额外加 `Sync` 任务——这会给 139 个包各加一层任务。

#### 机制名：`--rerun-tasks` / `--rerun`（强制忽略 up-to-date）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：`--rerun-tasks` 让**所有**任务忽略 up-to-date 检查强制执行，并且会**连同被调任务的依赖任务一起**强制执行，效果类似 `gradle clean test` 但**不删除已生成输出**；`--rerun` 是内置 task option，只强制**指定任务**重跑。两者是「怀疑缓存/up-to-date 结果不对」时的排障与强制重建手段。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/command_line_interface.html#sec:rerun_tasks`（章节 “Forcing tasks to execute”）关键原句：“You can force Gradle to execute all tasks ignoring up-to-date checks using the `--rerun-tasks` option”“This will force `test` and all task dependencies of `test` to execute. It is similar to running `gradle clean test`, but without the build's generated output being deleted. Alternatively, you can tell Gradle to rerun a specific task using the `--rerun` built-in task option.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/command_line_interface.html#sec:builtin_task_options`（章节 “Built-in task options”）关键原句：“`--rerun` Causes the task to be rerun even if up-to-date. Similar to `--rerun-tasks`, but for a specific task.”
- **对本场景**：可迁移，作为**逃生舱与验收工具**。其价值恰恰在于本场景最容易出错的地方：当怀疑某份生成物被错误跳过（例如生成脚本改了但输入没声明全），`--rerun-tasks` 可以无视判定强制全量重生成一遍，用来和增量结果做对账（「增量结果 vs 全量结果是否字节一致」）。代价：这是 O(全量)，只能按需用，不能进日常流水线。

---

### 5.4 依赖校验与可复现归档：让「产物字节」稳定到可以当哈希用

#### 机制名：依赖校验的启用与 `verification-metadata.xml`

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：依赖校验有两条互补机制：**checksum** 验证完整性（内容没被改），**signature(PGP)** 验证来源（谁发布的）。只要文件存在于固定位置 `$PROJECT_ROOT/gradle/verification-metadata.xml`，**校验就自动对所有构建生效**（当前唯一的元数据来源就是这个 XML）。配置是**全局的**：同一个文件作用于所有子项目、根项目与 `buildSrc`；包含构建（included build）会使用当前构建的配置、**忽略自己那份**。校验范围覆盖依赖管理引擎下载的一切（jar/zip、POM/Ivy/Gradle Module Metadata、settings 与 project 插件、经高级解析 API 得到的产物），但**不校验 changing dependency（`SNAPSHOT`）和构建内本地生成的产物**。最小配置形如 `<configuration><verify-metadata>true</verify-metadata><verify-signatures>false</verify-signatures></configuration>`；官方提醒：只放最小配置会让有外部依赖的项目**立刻失败**，因为还没有任何校验和。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#understanding_verification_methods`（章节 “Understanding Verification Methods”）关键原句：“Checksums – Verify the integrity of the artifact (that file contents haven't changed)”“Signatures – Verify the provenance of the artifact (who published it)”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#sub:enabling-verification`（章节 “Enabling Dependency Verification”）：“To enable dependency verification, you need to create a verification metadata file that tells Gradle which checksums and signatures to verify. Once this file exists, Gradle automatically verifies all dependencies during every build.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#sub:verification-metadata`（章节 “The Verification Metadata File”）关键原句：“Dependency verification is automatically enabled as soon as the verification metadata file is detected. The file must be located at `$PROJECT_ROOT/gradle/verification-metadata.xml`.”“Gradle does not verify changing dependencies (specifically `SNAPSHOT` versions) or locally produced artifacts (such as JARs generated during the build itself).”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#sec:verification-scope`（章节 “Scope of Dependency Verification”）：“The dependency verification configuration is global; a single file is used to verify the entire build. Specifically, the same file applies to all subprojects, the root project, and `buildSrc`.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#sec:checksum-verification`（章节 “Checksums”）：“You record the expected checksum (for example, `SHA-256`) of a library or plugin in the `gradle/verification-metadata.xml` file.”“If the computed checksum doesn't match the expected value, the build fails instead of using the tampered file.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#trusted-keys`（章节 “Trusted Keys and Key Servers”）：“Gradle records trusted public key IDs in `verification-metadata.xml` on a group-level basis”
- **对本场景**：可迁移，且**锁定的是"外部输入"这一半**。本场景 139 包如果要保证「同一输入永远同一产物」，除了自己包的源码，还包括第三方依赖的字节：`verification-metadata.xml` 正是把这个锁进 VCS（`gradle/` 目录可 diff）。代价：文件会很大（官方提示 metadata 校验会显著增大配置文件，可用 `<verify-metadata>false</verify-metadata>` 关闭），且每次升级依赖都会因新校验和缺失而**构建失败**——需要把「跑 `--write-verification-metadata` 并 review」纳入升级流程。

#### 机制名：`--write-verification-metadata`（引导生成）与 `--dependency-verification=strict`（模式）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：用 `--write-verification-metadata sha256` 让 Gradle 解析所有可解析配置（根项目、所有子项目、`buildSrc`、included builds、插件用到的配置）、下载全部产物、算出校验和并写到 `gradle/verification-metadata.xml`；`sha256,pgp` 则同时生成签名信息。官方给出**安全警告**：引导生成会信任当前仓库里的一切，如果恶意依赖已经进入构建，Gradle 会把被污染的校验和记下来，因此**必须 review 生成的文件**。模式和校验和取值：三种校验模式 `strict`（**默认**，尽早失败）、`lenient`（有失败仍继续，只显示错误）、`off`（完全忽略），可通过 `--dependency-verification`、`-Dorg.gradle.dependency.verification` 或 `gradle.properties` 的 `org.gradle.dependency.verification` 指定；支持的校验和算法为 `MD5`、`SHA1`、`SHA-256`、`SHA-512`，但**只有 `SHA-256`/`SHA-512` 现代可认为安全**（`SHA-1` 已知碰撞、`MD5` 不应使用）。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#generating_checksums_only`（章节 “Generating Checksums Only”）：“`$ ./gradlew --write-verification-metadata sha256`”“Gradle supports `MD5`, `SHA1`, `SHA-256`, and `SHA-512` checksums. However, only `SHA-256` and `SHA-512` are considered secure for modern use. `SHA-1` has known collisions and `MD5` should not be used.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#generating_checksums_and_signatures`（章节 “Generating Checksums and Signatures”）：“`$ ./gradlew --write-verification-metadata sha256,pgp`”；“Executing this command will cause Gradle to: Resolve all resolvable configurations, which includes: Configurations from the root project / Configurations from all subprojects / Configurations from `buildSrc` / Included builds configurations / Configurations used by plugins”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#sec:bootstrapping-verification`（章节 “Generating and Bootstrapping Verification Metadata”）关键原句：“However, bootstrapping has a critical security limitation: it trusts whatever is currently in your repositories. If a malicious dependency has already been introduced into your build (or into a repository you use), Gradle will record the compromised artifact's checksum or signature. This is why you must review the generated verification file.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#sec:disabling-verification`（章节 “Verification Modes”）：“`strict` (default) … Verification fails as early as possible to avoid using compromised dependencies during the build”；“`$ ./gradlew --dependency-verification lenient build`”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/command_line_interface.html#sec:dependency_verification_options`（章节 “Dependency verification options”）：“`-F=(strict,lenient,off)`, `--dependency-verification=(strict,lenient,off)` Configures the dependency verification mode. The default mode is `strict`.”“`-M`, `--write-verification-metadata` Generates checksums for dependencies used in the project (comma-separated list) for dependency verification.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#example_verification_metadata_file`（章节 “Example Verification Metadata File”）给出 `<components><component group="com.google.guava" name="failureaccess" version="1.0.3"><artifact name="failureaccess-1.0.3.jar"><pgp value="…"/><sha256 value="…" origin="Verified" reason="Added manually to fix CI"/></artifact></component></components>` 结构。
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html#sec:verification-dry-mode`（章节 “Dry-Run Mode”）：“`$ ./gradlew --write-verification-metadata sha256 help --dry-run`”“Instead of generating the `verification-metadata.xml` file, Gradle will create a new file called `verification-metadata.dryrun.xml` that you can review.”
- **对本场景**：可迁移，且是**「内容哈希稳定」的前置条件之一**（哈希稳定的前提是输入字节稳定，输入包括依赖）。取舍：`strict` 是默认值，所以真正的动作只是「生成并提交 `verification-metadata.xml`」；若把 `--dependency-verification` 放宽到 `lenient` 只为图省事，就等于放弃这条防线。代价：一次性引导生成 + 每次依赖变更后的 review，配置文件的体积与维护成本（可用 `--dry-run` 先生成 `.dryrun.xml` 预审，减少误提交）。

#### 机制名：`reproducibleFileOrder` / `preserveFileTimestamps`（`AbstractArchiveTask` 的字节可复现开关）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：`AbstractArchiveTask`（`Zip`/`Jar`/`Tar`/`War`/`Ear` 的父类）把「文件顺序」与「文件时间戳」做成**任务属性**：`isReproducibleFileOrder()` 为真时按可复现顺序遍历目录（与文件系统/操作系统无关），`isPreserveFileTimestamps()` 为假时归档条目使用同一时间（与机器、Java 版本、操作系统无关）。**两者都带 `@Input` 注解**——也就是说它们本身就是 up-to-date / cache key 的输入，会改变任务的指纹语义。**从 Gradle 9.0.0 起两者默认已是可复现值**：`reproducibleFileOrder` 默认 `true`、`preserveFileTimestamps` 默认 `false`，因此 `AbstractArchiveTask` 默认产出可复现归档；用户文档进一步列举 Gradle 9 起的默认：文件顺序确定、文件时间戳固定（具体值依归档类型）、目录权限 `0755`、文件权限 `0644`。可分别回退某个方面（`preserveFileTimestamps = true` / `reproducibleFileOrder = false` / `useFileSystemPermissions()`）。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/javadoc/org/gradle/api/tasks/bundling/AbstractArchiveTask.html#isReproducibleFileOrder()` 关键原句：“@Input public boolean isReproducibleFileOrder() — Specifies whether to enforce a reproducible file order when reading files from directories. Gradle will then walk the directories on disk which are part of this archive in a reproducible order independent of file systems and operating systems. This helps Gradle reliably produce byte-for-byte reproducible archives. Gradle defaults to `true` if not set explictly starting Gradle 9.0.0”
  - 同页 `#isPreserveFileTimestamps()`：“@Input public boolean isPreserveFileTimestamps() — Specifies whether file timestamps should be preserved in the archive. If `false` this ensures that archive entries have the same time for builds between different machines, Java versions and operating systems.”
  - 同页 `#useFileSystemPermissions()`：“Sets the directory and file permissions for archived files to be read from the file system.”
  - 源码 `subprojects/core/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/bundling/AbstractArchiveTask.java`（master）关键行：第 281–283 行 `@Input` + `@ToBeReplacedByLazyProperty` + `public boolean isPreserveFileTimestamps()`；第 317–319 行 `@Input` + `@ToBeReplacedByLazyProperty` + `public boolean isReproducibleFileOrder()`
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/working_with_files.html#sec:reproducible_archives`（章节 “Reproducible archives”）关键原句：“Starting with Gradle 9, archives are reproducible by default. It's desirable to recreate archives exactly the same, byte for byte, on different machines.”“Starting with Gradle 9.0.0, all `AbstractArchiveTask` (e.g. `Jar`, `Zip`) tasks produce reproducible archives by default: File order in the archive is deterministic / Files have fixed timestamps (exact values depend on the archive type) / Directories have fixed permissions set to `0755` / Files have fixed permissions set to `0644`”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/working_with_files.html#sec:revert_reproducible_archives`（章节 “Preserve file system-defined aspects”）关键原句：“`isPreserveFileTimestamps = true` (1) // Use file timestamps from the file system”“`isReproducibleFileOrder = false` (2) // Make file order based on the file system”
- **对本场景**：**直接可迁移，而且是把"内容哈希稳定"落到字节层面的那一环**。若 6 份生成物里含 zip/jar 类归档，可复现归档保证「同一输入 → 逐字节相同」；只有逐字节相同，第 3 节的 cache key 与下游任务的输入指纹才真正稳定（否则时间戳一变，下游整条链都 miss）。迁移代价：Gradle 9+ 已默认开启，若仍在旧版本需要显式设置这两个属性；另外要接受「归档内权限被固定为 0755/0644」，若业务依赖可执行位需要通过 `filePermissions` 或 `useFileSystemPermissions()` 显式恢复（后者可用 `org.gradle.archives.use-file-system-permissions=true` 全局开）。

#### 机制名：`reproducibleFileTimestamp` 与 `SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH`（自定义可复现时间戳）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：`reproducibleFileTimestamp`（Incubating）允许给归档内每个条目指定自定义时间戳（毫秒 unix epoch）；使用前提是 `isPreserveFileTimestamps` 为 `false`（默认值），否则**构建失败**。典型用法是把 `SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH` 环境变量设为最近一次 git 提交的时间（`git log -1 --format=%ct`）再喂给该属性，从而得到「有意义且可验证」的固定时间戳。各归档格式的时间戳分辨率/范围不同，最小值会被抬升：**ZIP 为 1980-02-01 00:00，TAR 为 1970-01-02T00:00:00Z**；ZIP 最大值 2097-11-01T00:00:00Z，超过则构建失败。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/working_with_files.html#sec:reproducible_timestamp`（章节 “Timestamp for files inside archives (Incubating)”）关键原句：“The `reproducibleFileTimestamp` property allows you to set a custom timestamp for every entry in the archive. Note that the `isPreserveFileTimestamps` property must be `false` (which is the default value) to use a custom timestamp, otherwise the build fails.”“the `SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH` environment variable can be set to the Unix timestamp from the most recent git commit using `git log -1 --format=%ct`”“Each format also has a minimum supported timestamp: 1980-02-01 00:00 for ZIP archives and 1970-01-02T00:00:00Z for TAR archives. Smaller values are raised to the minimum. ZIP archives also have a maximum supported timestamp of 2097-11-01T00:00:00Z; the build fails for larger values.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/javadoc/org/gradle/api/tasks/bundling/AbstractArchiveTask.html#setReproducibleFileTimestamp(...)` 相关方法说明：“If set, the `preserveFileTimestamps` property must be `false` (the default value), otherwise the task fails. Each archive format has a minimum supported timestamp, and smaller values are raised to that minimum: 1980-02-01 00:00 for ZIP archives and 1970-01-02T00:00:00Z for TAR archives.”（Since 9.7.0）
- **对本场景**：可迁移但**非必需**。默认固定时间戳已足够稳定；只有在审计/溯源要求「时间戳要可解释」时才用它。代价：引入环境变量作为输入，若 CI 与本地取值不同会引入新的缓存 miss 面（需要把它声明为 `@Input` 或保证一致）。

#### 机制名：非可复现输出如何破坏下游缓存（为什么要做到字节可复现）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：官方把「同输入不同输出」列为 `Non-repeatable task outputs`：如果这种输出被别的任务当输入，**任何依赖它的任务都无法从 cache 加载**——例如同一份内容但 mtime 不同的 jar 被重建，jar 文件本身就不同，下游任务的输入指纹随之变化，产生难以定位的 cache miss。对策有两条：让输出可复现（归档默认已可复现），或对易变数据做输入归一化（`normalize`/`@Classpath`）；官方还提醒：**用「让非可复现任务也开缓存」来绕过是有副作用的**，会带来更多 miss 甚至并行构建竞争写同一 cache key 的问题。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/common_caching_problems.html#volatile_outputs`（章节 “Non-repeatable task outputs”）关键原句：“Non-repeatable task outputs lead to non-stable task inputs as soon as another task depends on the non-repeatable output. For example, re-creating a jar file from the files with the same contents but different modification times yields a different jar file. Any other task depending on this jar file as an input file cannot be loaded from the cache when the jar file is rebuilt locally.”“This can lead to hard-to-diagnose cache misses when the consuming build is not a clean build or when a cacheable task depends on the output of a non-cacheable task.”
  - 同章续段：“Tasks that produce archives (`tar`, `zip`) are reproducible by default, starting with Gradle 9.0.0.”；“Another way to make the outputs repeatable is to activate caching for a task with non-repeatable outputs… Moreover, race conditions between different builds trying to store the same outputs in the build cache in parallel can lead to hard-to-diagnose cache misses. If possible, you should avoid going down that route.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/common_caching_problems.html#volatile_inputs`（章节 “Volatile task inputs”）：“If you use a volatile input like a timestamp as an input property for a task, then there is nothing Gradle can do to make the task cacheable.”“You can do this by splitting the task into two tasks - the first task doing the expensive work which is cacheable and the second task adding the volatile data to the output.”
- **对本场景**：**这是「产物字节可复现 → 内容哈希稳定」在 Gradle 里的闭环论证**，也正是本场景 6 份生成物能否共享缓存的关键。迁移时必须先验证「同一输入两次生成的产出字节一致」；不一致的生成物（例如注入了构建时间/构建号/随机 id）要按官方模式拆成「昂贵且可缓存的主产物 + 后置注入易变数据的便宜任务」。代价：需要改造生成器（把时间戳/构建号从主产物中剥离），这是迁移里最实际的一项工作量。

---

### 5.5 增量影响集：Gradle 如何确定「哪些任务需要重算」

#### 机制名：task graph 由声明的 inputs/outputs 推导（inferred task dependencies）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：Gradle 不是靠扫描文件依赖关系，而是靠**声明的输入输出**推导任务依赖：如果一个任务的输入被设为另一个任务的输出，Gradle **自动**建立依赖边（inferred task dependency），不需要写 `dependsOn`。`from(producerTask)` 之所以有效，是因为 `from()` 内部用 `project.files()` 包装参数，从而暴露该任务的 formal outputs。要连接「A 的 `@OutputDirectory`」到「B 的 `@InputFiles`」，官方给了三种写法：`tasks.x.map { it.outputs.files }`、`layout.files(producerTask)`（`files()`/`layout.files()`/`objects.fileCollection()` 能接受 task 参数而 `fileTree()` 不能）、或在 `fileTree(...) { builtBy(producerTask) }` 里显式声明 `builtBy`。并且做校验：**若一个任务在某位置产出、另一任务把该位置当输入，Gradle 会检查消费者依赖了生产者**；当生产与消费同时执行时构建直接失败以避免捕获错误状态。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:inferred_task_dependencies`（章节 “Inferred task dependencies”）关键原句：“Gradle will automatically make `packageFiles` depend on `processTemplates`. It can do this because it's aware that one of the inputs of packageFiles requires the output of the processTemplates task. We call this an inferred task dependency.”“Behind the scenes, `from()` uses the `project.files()` method to wrap the argument, which in turn exposes the task's formal outputs as a file collection.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:link_output_dir_to_input_files`（章节 “Linking an `@OutputDirectory` to an `@InputFiles`”）关键原句：“Remember that `files()`, `layout.files()` and `objects.fileCollection()` can take tasks as arguments, whereas `fileTree()` cannot.”“if you have to link just one output property among several, then you need to explicitly tell Gradle which task generates the input files using the `builtBy` method”；反例输出：“> Task :badInstrumentClasses NO-SOURCE” + “The use of `fileTree()` means that Gradle can't infer the task dependency itself.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_output_validation`（章节 “Input and output validation”）关键原句：“If one task produces an output in a location and another task consumes that location by referring to it as an input, then Gradle checks that the consumer task depends on the producer task. When the producer and the consumer tasks are executing at the same time, the build fails to avoid capturing an incorrect state.”
- **对本场景**：**这是直接消灭「手工同步」的机制本体**。834 个同步点之所以存在，是因为「A 变了要记得去改 B」这条规则只存在于人的脑子里；把它变成「B 的输入指向 A 的 declared output」，规则就变成 task graph 上的一条边，由 Gradle 校验、排序、并在 A 变更后自动把 B 列为待重算。迁移代价集中于一处：**必须先给 6 类生成物各写一个任务并声明其输出**（否则没有边可连），这是无法绕过的前置投入；之后每个包的同步点变成一行声明式连线。

#### 机制名：受影响集合的产生（inputs changed → out of date → 沿 graph 传播）

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：Gradle 的「受影响集合」不是预先算出来的文件集合，而是**执行期逐任务判定的结果**：执行前重建每个任务自己的输入/输出指纹，与历史比对得出「变更内容」，再按任务是否在本次执行图里决定跑或跳（第 1 节 `SkipUpToDateStep`/`ResolveChangesStep`）。**下游传播靠数据本身**：上游任务重跑后写出新的输出字节，下游任务的输入指纹随即与历史不同 → 下游被判 out-of-date。因此传播范围天然等于「输出真的变了的任务的下游闭包」，而不是「源码变更文件的静态下游」。官方另外给了两条与影响集相关的行为：输出目录里**新增的文件不会**让任务 out-of-date（保护共享目录场景，也意味着上游"多产出了文件"不会自动触发下游）；`@Destroys` 声明的破坏性文件会被纳入并行调度，避免「删除」与「消费/创建」并发。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:how_does_it_work`：“Each time after that, before the task is executed, Gradle takes a new fingerprint of the inputs and outputs. If the new fingerprints are the same as the previous fingerprints, Gradle assumes that the outputs are up to date and skips the task. If they are not the same, Gradle executes the task.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_output_side_effects`（章节 “Benefits of declaring task inputs and outputs”）：“Once you declare a task's formal inputs and outputs, Gradle can then infer things about those properties. For example, if an input of one task is set to the output of another, that means the first task depends on the second, right? Gradle knows this and can act upon it.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_output_parallelism`（章节 “Task parallelism”）关键原句：“Gradle will inspect the outputs of tasks when selecting the next task to run and will avoid concurrent execution of tasks that write to the same output directory. Similarly, Gradle will use the information about what files a task destroys (e.g. specified by the `Destroys` annotation) and avoid running a task that removes a set of files while another task is running that consumes or creates those same files (and vice versa).”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_output_continuous_build`（章节 “Continuous build”）：“Since Gradle knows what files a task depends on, it can automatically run a task again if any of its inputs change.”
  - 源码 `platforms/core-execution/execution/src/main/java/org/gradle/internal/execution/steps/ResolveChangesStep.java`：`changeDetector.detectChanges(work, previousExecution, beforeExecution, incrementalInputProperties, context.getDetectedOverlappingOutputs().isPresent())` —— 变更判定的输入正是「上一次状态」与「本次执行前状态」两份快照。
- **对本场景**：可迁移，并给出一个重要的期望管理：**收益不是「1 个源文件 → 只重算 1 个包」，而是「→ 只重算输出真的变了的包的下游闭包」**。若生成器把时间戳写进产物，「1 个源文件」会传染成全部 139 包（第 4 节的可复现问题）。迁移代价：把「传播」从人工规则换成数据驱动后，"改一处要连带改几处"这件事必须靠第 4 节的字节稳定性来收窄；否则会从「手工漏同步」变成「全量重算」。

#### 机制名：Configuration Cache 与增量执行的分工

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：官方把两个缓存的分工讲得很清楚并给了对照表：**Build Cache 缓存「任务输出与 artifact transform 输出」，key 是「每个任务声明的输入（源码、classpath、动作实现）」，作用是「通过复用先前输出跳过任务执行」**；**Configuration Cache 缓存「配置阶段的产物：task graph、每个任务的已配置状态、依赖信息」，key 是「构建逻辑与环境（构建脚本、init 脚本、配置期读取的环境变量与属性等）」，作用是「命中时完全跳过配置阶段」**。二者是不同层次的工作规避：Configuration Cache **不替代** up-to-date 与 task output caching——它跳过的是「计算要跑哪些任务」，而任务是否真的执行仍由 inputs/outputs 判定（其生命周期表里 Execution 阶段在两种模式下都「Runs the scheduled tasks against the (deserialized) task graph」）。Configuration Cache 命中判定的做法是：用请求的任务、其参数与一些环境信息作 key 找候选条目，再对比「当前构建的配置输入」与条目里记录的指纹（不匹配即 miss）。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/configuration_cache.html#config_cache:intro`（章节 “Configuration Cache Basics”，含 Build Cache / Configuration Cache 对照表）关键原句：“Build Cache | Task outputs and artifact transform outputs | Each task's declared inputs (sources, classpath, action implementation) | Skips task execution by reusing previous outputs”；“Configuration Cache | The result of the configuration phase: the task graph, each task's configured state, and dependency information | Build logic and environment (build scripts, init scripts, environment variables and properties read at configuration time, and more) | Skips the configuration phase entirely on a cache hit”；“Gradle's Build Cache has long optimized the execution phase by reusing previously built outputs and parallelizing as much work as possible. The Configuration Cache builds on this idea of work avoidance and parallelization.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/configuration_cache.html#config_cache:intro:lifecycle`（章节 “The Build Lifecycle”）表：Execution 阶段 无 CC 时 “Runs the scheduled tasks against the just-computed task graph.”，有 CC 时 “Runs the scheduled tasks against the deserialized task graph.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/configuration_cache.html#the_hit_or_miss_decision`（章节 “The Hit-or-Miss Decision”）：“At the start of every invocation, Gradle uses the requested tasks, their arguments, and some other environmental information as a cache key, then checks whether there is a stored entry matching the key.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/command_line_interface.html#sec:command_line_performance`：“`--configuration-cache`, `--no-configuration-cache` Toggles the Configuration Cache. Gradle will try to reuse the build configuration from previous builds. Default is off.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/common_caching_problems.html#custom_actions`：“Note that configuring a task from other task is not supported when using the configuration cache.”
- **对本场景**：可迁移但**优先级低于前几节**。本场景的痛点是「单个源文件变更要手工同步 6 份生成物」，这属于**执行期**问题，解决它的是 declared outputs + up-to-date + build cache；Configuration Cache 优化的是「139 包的生成任务图每次都要重新配置脚本」的开销。是否值得迁移取决于 834 个同步点是否被建模成大量任务（任务越多，配置阶段成本越高，CC 收益越大）。代价：CC 有兼容性约束（不能在别的任务里配置另一个任务、构建逻辑需满足其要求），官方默认关闭，属于"第二阶段"优化。

---

### 5.6 与本场景的可迁移性（改 1 个源文件 → 手工同步 6 份生成物、139 包 / 834 同步点）

#### 机制名：用 declared outputs + task 依赖消灭手工同步

- **项目**：Gradle
- **机制**：Gradle 的整套增量能力都建立在「任务**声明**它的输入与输出」这一前提上：官方把结论写得很直白——**增量构建要求任务至少有一个输出**，构建作者要做的事就是「告诉 Gradle 哪些属性是输入、哪些是输出」；如果某个属性影响输出却没登记为输入，任务会在不该 up-to-date 时被判 up-to-date；反之登记了不影响输出的属性会让任务不必要地执行；**非确定性任务（同输入不同输出）不应配置增量构建，因为 up-to-date 检查不会生效**。声明之后，Gradle 自动获得：跳过执行（UP-TO-DATE）、从 cache 复用（FROM-CACHE）、推导任务依赖（inferred task dependencies）、连续构建（`--continuous`）、并行安全调度，以及（配合第 4 节）字节级可复现的产物。
- **出处**：
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_inputs_outputs`（章节 “Task inputs and outputs”）关键原句：“Also note that incremental build won't work unless a task has at least one task output, although tasks usually have at least one input as well.”“What this means for build authors is simple: you need to tell Gradle which task properties are inputs and which are outputs. If a task property affects the output, be sure to register it as an input, otherwise the task will be considered up to date when it's not.”“Also be careful of non-deterministic tasks that may generate different output for exactly the same inputs: these should not be configured for incremental build as the up-to-date checks won't work.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/incremental_build.html#sec:task_input_output_side_effects`（章节 “Benefits of declaring task inputs and outputs”）
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/build_cache.html#sec:task_output_caching_inputs`：“It is very important that a cacheable task has a complete picture of its inputs and outputs, so that the results from one build can be safely re-used somewhere else.”
  - `https://docs.gradle.org/current/javadoc/org/gradle/api/tasks/CacheableTask.html`（对应源码 `subprojects/core-api/src/main/java/org/gradle/api/tasks/CacheableTask.java`）：“Only tasks that produce reproducible and relocatable output should be marked with `CacheableTask`.”
- **对本场景**：
  - **可迁移**：把「6 份生成物」的定义从脚本/人脑里的约定，升级为 6 个（或按包展开成 6×N 个）任务的 `@OutputFile`/`@OutputDirectory`；源文件是生产者任务的 `@InputFiles`，各生成物之间以及与源之间用 `from(producerTask)` / `layout.files(producerTask)` / `files(producerTask)` 连线。**834 个同步点于是不再是一条条需要记住的规则，而是 task graph 上的边**：改 1 个源文件后，哪些生成物要重做由 Gradle 逐任务比对指纹决定，未被影响的任务直接 `UP-TO-DATE`（几乎零成本），跨机器/跨构建的重复工作则由 build cache 以 `FROM-CACHE` 复用。这条路径同时把「同步点是否漏了」这件事从人工检查变成可校验的构建事实（第 5 节的 producer/consumer 校验会在不该并行时直接让构建失败）。
  - **代价（按投入顺序）**：
    1. **输入闭包盘点（最贵、无法跳过）**：必须为 6 类生成物逐个确认「哪些输入影响输出」。官方明确了漏声明的后果——增量构建下会「在不该 up-to-date 时被判 up-to-date」，build cache 下会产生「incorrect cache hits」。对 834 个同步点，这意味着需要一次系统性盘点，而不是试错。
    2. **非确定性改造**：同输入不同输出的生成器要么改成确定性（第 4 节的归档可复现 + 剥离时间戳/构建号到后置任务），要么用 `@UntrackedTask`/`doNotTrackState()` 显式退出追踪（代价是永久 out-of-date、永不读写 cache）。这一步决定缓存能否真正命中。
    3. **路径敏感度与重定位**：`PathSensitive` 默认是 `ABSOLUTE`，官方警告这会让输出无法跨工作区经 cache 共享；每个文件输入都要显式声明 `RELATIVE`/`NAME_ONLY` 才能获得跨目录/跨机器的复用。
    4. **输出位置不重叠**：多个任务写同一目录会让后者的缓存被自动关闭（`OVERLAPPING_OUTPUTS`），且输出目录里新增文件不触发 out-of-date；要么给 834 个同步点各自规划独立输出位置，要么按官方模式加 `Sync` 任务合并（对 139 包会额外加一层任务）。
    5. **依赖字节锁定**：若要把「同一输入 → 同一产物」扩到第三方依赖，需要生成并提交 `gradle/verification-metadata.xml`，并把依赖升级流程改成「更新 metadata + review（可先用 `--dry-run` 生成 `.dryrun.xml`）」；`strict` 是默认模式，但配置文件会显著变大（可用 `<verify-metadata>false</verify-metadata>` 收敛）。
    6. **基础设施与生态成本**：本地 build cache 只需 `org.gradle.caching=true`；远程 cache 需要部署 HTTP backend（Gradle 提供 Docker 版 cache node）并处理凭证/TLS/重定向，收益主要在 CI 与多机。Configuration Cache 属于第二阶段（能省配置阶段，但要求构建逻辑满足其约束，例如不能在别的任务里配置任务、`doFirst` 里改输入不会被检测）。
    7. **组织/流程成本**：官方校验告警与 `--info` 的 “is not up-to-date because:” 原因列表需要成为日常排障手段；重复两遍必须全 `UP-TO-DATE` 应作为验收门禁，否则缓存收益与正确性都不可信。
  - **一句话结论**：Gradle 用「declared outputs + task 依赖 + 内容指纹」把本场景从**人盯 834 个同步点**变成**机器算一个受影响闭包**，机制上完全对口；真正的迁移成本不在 Gradle 侧，而在把 6 类生成器的输入闭包与确定性一次性梳理干净——这两项做不完，增量与缓存会退化成"看起来启用、实际上全量重算或错误复用"。
