**未证实项汇总**
1. `argocd app wait` 自身的退出码定义：官方 CLI 页面与 markdown 源均未给出（两处均 200 但无 exit codes 段）。试过的 URL 见上表。
2. 旧版 kubernetes.io（如 v1.24 对应页）的 `kubectl diff` 退出码措辞：website 仓库两个路径 404，未取到；因此「不同页面写法不一致」这一说法本文**不采信**——实际抓到的 v1.37 生成页与 master 源码 help 文本**完全一致**（`0 / 1 / >1` 三档），歧义来自误读（把非 0 当错误、以及外部 diff 程序退出码透传），源码注释已给出官方设计意图。
3. `kubectl diff` 的 `--dry-run` 开关：官方页面全文 `--dry-run` 出现 0 次，即**不存在该开关**；「diff 内部走 dry-run apply」的说法来自 master 源码（`DryRun(true)`、`// Dry-run create if the object doesn't exist.`），非文档明文。



---

# 第三部分：Nix 内容寻址 / 可复现构建 + 锁文件即摘要门禁

取证日期：2026-09-17 · 全部结论带出处（官方文档 URL 或源码文件路径 + 逐字原文）
工程来源：`raw.githubusercontent.com/NixOS/nix/master`（Nix 默认分支为 **master**，非 main）与 `doc/manual/source/**`；手册渲染页 `nix.dev/manual/nix/latest/**`；`reproducible-builds.org`。

**本报告面向的场景**：改 1 个源文件要手工同步 6 份生成物（其中一份是「完整性摘要」），139 个包 ≈ 834 个同步点，历史上「重算摘要」被手工执行 50 次；痛点是「一个『通过』既可能是零违规，也可能是未比历史基线更差」。

---

## 第一部分：Nix 内容寻址

### D1. 机制名

`/nix/store/<hash>-<name>` 路径命名 = **内容寻址 + 摘要即身份**；input-addressed vs content-addressed derivation（CA derivations，RFC 62）；NAR 序列化与哈希；`nix hash path`。

### D2. 项目

NixOS/nix（官方仓库，默认分支 `master`）；设计依据 NixOS/rfcs `0062-content-addressed-paths.md`。

### D3. 它怎么做

#### (1) `/nix/store/<hash>-<name>` 的三个组成部分，逐字原文

出处：`doc/manual/source/store/store-path.md`（渲染页 `https://nix.dev/manual/nix/latest/store/store-path.html`）

> Store path base names are pairs of
>
> - A 20-byte [digest]{#digest} for identification
> - A symbolic [name]{#name} for people to read
>
> **Example**
>
> - Digest: `q06x3jll2yfzckz2bzqak089p43ixkkq`
> - Name:   `firefox-33.1`
>
> A store path base name is rendered to a string as the concatenation of
>
> - Digest rendered in [Nix32], a variant of [Base32] (20 hash bytes become 32 ASCII characters)
> - Hyphen (`-`)
> - Name

同文件对「摘要即身份」的硬约束：

> Think of a store path base name as an [opaque], [unique identifier]:
> The only way to obtain a store path base name is by adding or building store objects.
> **A store path base name will always reference exactly one store object.**

即：`139 个包` 在 Nix 里根本不存在「6 份生成物各自存一份名字」的问题——标识符只可能由内容算出来，不可能手写。这是本场景 834 个同步点的**根因对照**。

#### (2) 摘要的精确算法（为什么改一个字节名字必变）

出处：`doc/manual/source/protocols/store-path.md`

```ebnf
store-path = store-dir "/" digest "-" name
```

> - `digest` = [Nix32](@docroot@/protocols/nix32.md) representation of the compressed to 160 bits [SHA-256] hash of `fingerprint`.

> ## Fingerprint
>
> - ```ebnf
>   fingerprint = type ":sha256:" inner-digest ":" store ":" name
>   ```
>
>   Note that it includes the location of the store as well as the name to make sure that changes to either of those are reflected in the hash
>   (e.g. you won't get `/nix/store/<digest>-name1` and `/nix/store/<digest>-name2`, or `/gnu/store/<digest>-name1`, with equal hash parts).

`inner-digest` 的定义（决定「重算什么」）：

> - `inner-digest` = base-16 representation of a SHA-256 hash of `inner-fingerprint`.
>
> - `inner-fingerprint` = one of the following based on `type`:
>   - if `type` = `"text:" ...`: the string written to the resulting store path.
>   - if `type` = `"source:" ...`: the [Nix Archive (NAR)] serialization of the [file system object] of the store object.
>   - if `type` = `"output:" id`: the [ATerm] serialization of the ["fully masked derivation"] …, in which fixed-output inputs are replaced by their content addresses and the derivation's own output paths are blanked.

**对本场景直接可用的一点**：`type = "text:" ...` 的分支意味着 Nix 也支持「对一个纯文本生成物（比如你那份完整性摘要文件本身）做内容寻址」——摘要文件不需要是二进制。

#### (3) input-addressed vs content-addressed：谁决定输出路径

出处：`NixOS/rfcs/rfcs/0062-content-addressed-paths.md`（`https://github.com/NixOS/rfcs/blob/master/rfcs/0062-content-addressed-paths.md`）

原文（input-addressed 的定义）：

> The "input-addressed" designation comes from the way the output paths are computed: **They derive from the derivation data-structure, which is the input of the build.**

原文（content-addressed 的定义）：

> The idea behind the "content-addressed" model is that **rather than deriving these output paths from the inputs of the build, we derive them from the output (the produced store path).**

RFC 62 的 `Realisation` 数据结构（就是「把重算结果登记下来」的机制）：

```python
class DrvOutput :
    derivationHash : Hash
    outputName : str

class Realisation :
    id : DrvOutput
    outputPath : StorePath
```

> - its **Output Id** `DrvOutput(drv, output)` as the tuple `(hashModulo(drv), output)`. This id uniquely identifies the output. We textually represent this as `hashModulo(drv)!output`.
> - its **realisation** `Realisation(outputId)` containing the path `outputPath` at which this output is stored (either content-defined or input-defined depending on the type of derivation)

#### (4) CA 如何切断「上游一改就全量重算」的级联 —— 本场景最值钱的一条

出处：同上 RFC 62。

动机段：

> This was never done because it represents quite a big change in Nix's model… Even without going all the way down to a fully intensional model, we can make specific paths content-addressed, which can give some important benefits of the intensional store at a much lower price. **In particular, setting some critical derivations as content-addressed can lead to some substantial build cutoffs.**

切断级联的机制就是 `resolved` 的**非单射性**：

> For a derivation `drv` whose input derivations have all been realised, we define its **associated resolved derivation** `resolved(drv)` as `drv` in which we replace every input derivation `inDrv` of `drv` by `Realisation(inDrv).path`.
>
> `resolved` is (intentionally) **not injective**: If `drv` and `drv'` only differ because one depends on `dep` and the other on `dep'`, but `dep` and `dep'` are content-addressed and **have the same output hash**, then `resolved(drv)` and `resolved(drv')` will be equal.

在 build 流程里对应这一步（先 resolve，再看是否已经建过）：

```python
def build_derivation ( derivation : Derivation , outputsToBuild : [ str ]) -> Map [ DrvOutput , Realisation ]:
    inputRealisations : Map [ DrvOutput , Realisation ] = {}
    for ( inputDrv , requiredOutputs ) in derivation . inputDrvs :
        inputRealisations += build_derivation ( inputDrv , requiredOutputs )
    derivationToBuild : BasicDerivation = resolved ( inputDrv , inputRealisations )
    # At that point, we might realise that the resolved derivation is actually
    # something that we have already built. In that case we just return
    # the existing result.
    if ( isBuilt ( derivationToBuild )):
        return queryOutputs ( derivationToBuild , outputsToBuild )
```

以及签名侧的收益（也是「摘要可以自己验证」的根据）：

> Input-addressed paths need to be signed because there's no way to verify their content (short of rebuilding them and praying that the build is deterministic of course)… Content-addressed paths on the other hand don't need a signature: If `/nix/store/123-foo` is content-addressed, then `123` is supposed to be a hash of the content of the path, and that can be easily checked.

**官方也自陈了这条路的边界**（必须一起写进代价）：

> - We specify that only a sub-category of derivations can safely be marked as `contentAddressed`, but **there's no way to enforce these restrictions**;
> - This will probably be a breaking-change for some tooling since the output path that's available at eval-time and stored in the `.drv` files doesn't correspond to an actual on-disk path.

自引用（self-reference）问题与哈希重写的近似性：

> 1. A self-reference means that the output path depends on the temporary path that has been used during the build (**potentially breaking reproducibility** as there's no guaranty for this path to be stable),
> 2. More annoyingly, a self-reference means that the path can't be moved freely (otherwise the self-reference would become dangling).

> This is obviously a **hack**, however it seems to work very well in practice…

#### (5) NAR 序列化：为什么必须有一个**唯一的**序列化格式

出处：`doc/manual/source/store/file-system-object/content-address.md`

> However, for our purposes these formats have two problems:
>
> - They **do not have a canonical serialisation**, meaning that given an FSO, there can be many different serialisations.
>   For instance, TAR files can have variable amounts of padding between archive members; and some archive formats leave the order of directory entries undefined.
>   **This would be bad because we use serialisation to compute cryptographic hashes over file system objects, and for those hashes to be useful as a content address or for integrity checking, uniqueness is crucial.**
>   Otherwise, correct hashes would report false mismatches, and the store would fail to find the content.
>
> - They store more information than we have in our notion of FSOs, such as time stamps. This can cause FSOs that Nix should consider equal to hash to different values on different machines, just because the dates differ.

NAR 的格式规范直接写在源码注释里（可作为「生成物规范化」的范本）：`src/libutil/include/nix/util/archive.hh`

```
 * IF path points to a DIRECTORY:
 *   dump(path) = attrs(
 *     [ ("type", "directory")
 *     , ("entries", concat(map(f, sort(entries(path)))))
 *     ]
 *     )
 *     where f(fn) = attrs(
 *       [ ("name", fn)
 *       , ("file", dump(path + "/" + fn))
 *       ]
 *     )
 *
 * where:
 *   attrs(as) = concat(map(attr, as)) + encN(0)
 *   attrs((a, b)) = encS(a) + encS(b)
 *   encS(s) = encN(len(s)) + s + (padding until next 64-bit boundary)
 *   encN(n) = 64-bit little-endian encoding of n.
 *   sort(strings) = lexicographic sort by 8-bit value (strcmp).
```

关键三点可迁移：**目录项强制排序**（`sort(entries(path))`）、**长度前缀**（`encN(len(s))`）、**不含时间戳**。

自引用在摘要里的处理（哨兵值 + 临时路径）：

出处：`doc/manual/source/store/store-object/content-address.md`

> In all currently-supported store object content-addressing methods, when hashing the file system object data, any occurrence of store object's own store path in the digested data is replaced with a [sentinel value].
> …
> **Design note**
> … If, conversely, the data after this normalization process is still different, we'll compute a different content-address.
> The method of preparing the provisional self-referenced data has **failed** to be deterministic in the sense of not *leaking* the choice of provisional store path — a choice which is supposed to be arbitrary — into the final store object.

四种内容寻址方法（`flat` / `text` / `nix-archive`（NAR）/ `git`）及各自对引用的限制，见同文件 `## Content addressing Methods` 一节；其中 `text`：

> This is the only store-object content-addressing method that is not named identically with a corresponding file system object method. It is somewhat obscure, mainly used for "drv files" (derivations serialized as store objects in their ["ATerm" file format]). **Prefer another method if possible.**

#### (6) `nix hash path`：把「重算」变成一条命令

出处：`doc/manual/source/command-ref/nix-hash.md`（legacy）与 `nix.dev/manual/nix/latest/command-ref/new-cli/nix3-hash.html`（new CLI）

new CLI 子命令清单逐字：

> `nix hash file` - print cryptographic hash of a regular file
> **`nix hash path` - print cryptographic hash of the NAR serialisation of a path**
> `nix hash to-base16` / `to-base32` / `to-base64` / `to-sri` - convert a hash … (deprecated, use `nix hash convert` instead)
> `nix hash convert` - convert between hash formats

legacy `nix-hash` 手册原文：

> The hash is computed over a *serialisation* of each path: a dump of the file system tree rooted at the path. This allows directories and symlinks to be hashed as well as regular files. The dump is in the *[Nix Archive (NAR)][Nix Archive] format* produced by [`nix-store --dump`]. Thus, **`nix-hash path` yields the same cryptographic hash as `nix-store --dump path | md5sum`.**

`--type` 支持 `blake3`, `md5`, `sha1`, `sha256`, `sha512`；`--truncate` 截到 160 位；输出格式可切 `base16/base32/base64/SRI`。

源码位置：`src/nix/hash.cc`

```cpp
/**
 * Base for `nix hash path`, `nix hash file` (deprecated), and `nix-hash` (legacy).
 *
 * Deprecation Issue: https://github.com/NixOS/nix/issues/8876
 */
struct CmdHashBase : Command
```
`struct CmdHashPath : CmdHashBase`（同文件第 136 行），注册于第 267 行 `{"path", []() { return make_ref<CmdHashPath>(); }}`。

### D4. URL / 文件路径（本部分）

| 内容 | 出处 |
| --- | --- |
| store path 结构、摘要即身份 | `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/NixOS/nix/master/doc/manual/source/store/store-path.md`；渲染页 `https://nix.dev/manual/nix/latest/store/store-path.html` |
| 完整摘要算法规范 | `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/NixOS/nix/master/doc/manual/source/protocols/store-path.md` |
| CA derivations 设计 | `https://github.com/NixOS/rfcs/blob/master/rfcs/0062-content-addressed-paths.md` |
| NAR / 内容寻址方法 | `doc/manual/source/store/file-system-object/content-address.md`、`doc/manual/source/store/store-object/content-address.md` |
| NAR 格式规范（源码注释） | `src/libutil/include/nix/util/archive.hh` |
| `nix hash path` | `doc/manual/source/command-ref/nix-hash.md`；`https://nix.dev/manual/nix/latest/command-ref/new-cli/nix3-hash.html`；`src/nix/hash.cc` |

### D5. 对本场景的可迁移改法与代价

**改法（摘要在源里冻结 → 任何不一致非零退出，落成一条命令）**

1. 给 6 份生成物定义**唯一确定**的序列化（抄 NAR 三原则）：目录项按字节序排序、字段带长度前缀、**剔除时间戳/hostname/绝对路径**。否则重算结果会天然不等于冻结值，门禁会天天误报——Nix 官方把这条列为选择 NAR 的两个理由（`doc/manual/source/store/file-system-object/content-address.md`）。
2. 摘要用「对规范化字节流的 SHA-256，再编码成 Nix32/SRI」这类**可复算且可读**的形式（`doc/manual/source/protocols/store-path.md`）；摘要文件本身可用 `type = "text:"` 分支的内容寻址。
3. 提供**一条**重算命令（对标 `nix hash path`），让「重算摘要」从 50 次手工操作变成脚本里的一行 + CI 里的 `git diff --exit-code`。
4. **隔离建过程**（对标 `sandbox`，见 D6 节）：生成器只许看见声明的输入，禁止读 `/usr/bin` 之类的环境；否则「通过」依赖于跑在谁的机器上，等于没有门禁。官方理由逐字：`This is useful to prevent undeclared dependencies on files in directories such as /usr/bin`（`https://nix.dev/manual/nix/latest/command-ref/conf-file.html`，`sandbox` 设置）。
5. **切断级联**（对标 RFC 62 的 `resolved` 非单射性）：让 139 个包彼此之间只通过**内容的摘要**引用，而不是通过「上游包的版本号/构建序号」引用。这样上游改了一个字节但产物摘要没变时，下游 6 份生成物**不需要跟着动**——这正是 RFC 62 说的 `substantial build cutoffs`。这是把 834 个同步点压下去的唯一结构性办法。

**代价**

- 采用内容寻址需要**双向改写工具链**：RFC 62 自陈 `This will probably be a breaking-change for some tooling since the output path that's available at eval-time and stored in the .drv files doesn't correspond to an actual on-disk path.`
- 自引用（生成物内部出现自己的路径/摘要）在纯文本情形下可用哨兵值重写（`doc/manual/source/store/store-object/content-address.md`），但**嵌在压缩档案里的自引用检测不到**，RFC 62 明确列为未解问题（`Ensuring that no temporary output path leaks in the result`），并把「用 `--check` 检测泄漏」列为 future work。
- CA 与「import from derivation / recursive Nix」不兼容（RFC 62 `Make content-addressed derivations compatible with other Nix features`）。
- 二进制产物跨平台哈希不同 → 需要多平台冻结（对照 Terraform 的 `terraform providers lock -platform`，见 B 部分）。

---

## 第二部分：`nix build --check` / `nix-store --verify` —— 「生成物必须等于重算结果」的最纯形式

### D6. 机制名

`--check` / `--rebuild`（rebuild & bitwise compare）；`nix-store --verify --check-contents`；`nix store verify`（分级退出码）。

### D7. 项目

NixOS/nix。

### D8. 它怎么做

#### (1) `nix build --rebuild` 的定义，逐字来自源码

出处：`src/nix/build.cc`（`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/NixOS/nix/master/src/nix/build.cc`）

```cpp
struct CmdBuild : InstallablesCommand, MixOutLinkByDefault, MixDryRun, MixJSON, MixProfile, MixPrintOutPaths
{
    BuildMode buildMode = bmNormal;

    CmdBuild()
    {
        addFlag({
            .longName = "rebuild",
            .description = "Rebuild an already built package and compare the result to the existing store paths.",
            .handler = {&buildMode, bmCheck},
        });
    }
```

渲染后的手册原文（`https://nix.dev/manual/nix/latest/command-ref/new-cli/nix3-build.html`）：

> `--rebuild`
> Rebuild an already built package and compare the result to the existing store paths.

`BuildMode` 枚举（`src/libstore/include/nix/store/store-api.hh` 第 57 行）：

```cpp
enum BuildMode : uint8_t { bmNormal, bmRepair, bmCheck };
```

#### (2) legacy `nix-store --realise --check` 的定义（把语义讲得最清楚的一段）

出处：`doc/manual/source/command-ref/nix-store/realise.md`

> - `--check`
>
>   This option allows you to check whether a derivation is deterministic. It **rebuilds the specified derivation and checks whether the result is bitwise-identical with the existing outputs, printing an error if that's not the case.** The outputs of the specified derivation must already exist. When used with `-K`, if an output path is not identical to the corresponding output from the previous build, the new output path is left in `/nix/store/name.check.`

**这就是「生成物必须等于重算结果」的最纯形式**：不是「比上次好」，不是「没超过阈值」，而是 `bitwise-identical`，不等就是 error。

#### (3) 比对的实际判据：NAR 哈希，源码逐字

出处：`src/libstore/build/derivation-builder-impl.cc`（check 分支）

```cpp
        /* Move files, if needed */
        if (store->toRealPath(newInfo.path) != actualPath) {
            if (buildMode == bmRepair) {
                /* Path already exists, need to replace it */
                replaceValidPath(store->toRealPath(newInfo.path), actualPath);
            } else if (buildMode == bmCheck) {
                /* Path already exists, and we want to compare, so we leave out
                   new path in place. */
            } else if (localStore.isValidPath(newInfo.path)) {
```

```cpp
        if (buildMode == bmCheck) {
            /* Check against already registered outputs */

            if (localStore.isValidPath(newInfo.path)) {
                ValidPathInfo oldInfo(*localStore.queryPathInfo(newInfo.path));
                if (newInfo.narHash != oldInfo.narHash) {
                    auto * diffHook = localSettings.getDiffHook();
                    if (diffHook || settings.keepFailed) {
                        auto dst = store->toRealPath(newInfo.path);
                        dst += ".check";
                        deletePath(dst);
                        movePath(actualPath, dst);
                        ...
                        throw NotDeterministic(
                            "derivation '%s' may not be deterministic: output %s differs from %s",
                            store->printStorePath(drvPath),
                            PathFmt(store->toRealPath(newInfo.path)),
                            PathFmt(dst));
                    } else
                        throw NotDeterministic(
                            "derivation '%s' may not be deterministic: output %s differs",
                            store->printStorePath(drvPath),
                            PathFmt(store->toRealPath(newInfo.path)));
                }
```

判据是 `newInfo.narHash != oldInfo.narHash`——**重算产物的规范化哈希 vs 数据库里登记的哈希**。同时注意 check 模式下**不安装**任何 store object：

```cpp
        /* In the check case we install no store objects, and so
           `builtOutputs` is empty. However, per issue #14287, there is
           an expectation that the post-build hook is still executed.
           (This is useful for e.g. logging successful deterministic rebuilds.)
```

以及 check 一定会真的重跑（不会因为产物已存在就跳过），见 `src/libstore/build/derivation-building-goal.cc`：

```cpp
        if (buildMode != bmCheck && allValid) {
            debug("skipping build of derivation '%s', someone beat us to it", worker.store.printStorePath(drvPath));
```
（`bmCheck` 时**即使 allValid 也不跳过**。）

#### (4) 失败被建模成一等状态，不是日志字符串

出处：`src/libstore/include/nix/store/build-result.hh`

```cpp
enum struct BuildResultFailureStatus : uint8_t {
    PermanentFailure,
    InputRejected,
    OutputRejected,
    /// possibly transient
    TransientFailure,
    /// no longer used
    CachedFailure,
    TimedOut,
    MiscFailure,
    DependencyFailed,
    LogLimitExceeded,
    NotDeterministic,
    NoSubstituters,
    /// A certain type of `OutputRejected`. The protocols do not yet
    /// know about this one, so change it back to `OutputRejected`
    /// before serialization.
    HashMismatch,
};
```

`NotDeterministic` 和 `HashMismatch` 是**独立的失败类别**——调用方不需要解析错误文本就能分流。

#### (5) 退出码与报错原文（可直接抄进自己的门禁设计）

出处：`doc/manual/source/advanced-topics/diff-hook.md`（渲染页 `https://releases.nixos.org/nix/nix-2.34.1/manual/advanced-topics/diff-hook.html`）

> # Spot-Checking Build Determinism
>
> Verify a path which already exists in the Nix store by passing `--check` to the build command.
>
> If the build passes and is deterministic, Nix will exit with a status code of 0:
>
> ```
> $ nix-build ./deterministic.nix --attr stable --check
> checking outputs of '/nix/store/z98fasz2jqy9gs0xbvdj939p27jwda38-stable.drv'...
> /nix/store/yyxlzw3vqaas7wfp04g0b1xg51f2czgq-stable
> ```
>
> If the build is not deterministic, Nix will **exit with a status code of 1**:
>
> ```
> error: derivation '/nix/store/cgl13lbj1w368r5z8gywipl1ifli7dhk-unstable.drv' may
> not be deterministic: output '/nix/store/krpqk0l9ib0ibi1d2w52z293zw455cap-unstable' differs
> ```

`--check` 的一个前置约束（也是「重算」必须能跑）:

> `--check` is only usable if the derivation has been built on the system already. If the derivation has not been built Nix will fail with the error:
>
>     error: some outputs of '/nix/store/hzi1h60z2qf0nb85iwnpvrai3j2w7rr6-unstable.drv'
>     are not valid, so checking is not possible

以及 diff-hook：比对不一致时才会执行，用来**定位差异**（`doc/manual/source/advanced-topics/diff-hook.md`）：

> You can use Nix's `diff-hook` setting to compare build results. Note that this hook is only executed if the results differ; it is not used for determining if the results are the same.

配置段逐字：

```
diff-hook = /etc/nix/my-diff-hook
run-diff-hook = true
```

#### (6) `nix-store --verify`（旧）vs `nix store verify`（新）

出处：`doc/manual/source/command-ref/nix-store/verify.md`

> The operation `--verify` verifies the internal consistency of the Nix database, and the consistency between the Nix database and the Nix store. **Any inconsistencies encountered are automatically repaired.** Inconsistencies are generally the result of the Nix store or database being modified by non-Nix tools, or of bugs in Nix itself.
>
> - `--check-contents`
>
>   Checks that the contents of every valid store path has not been altered by **computing a SHA-256 hash of the contents and comparing it with the hash stored in the Nix database at build time**. Paths that have been modified are printed out. For large stores, `--check-contents` is obviously quite slow.

**诚实提示**：`nix-store --verify` **默认自动修复**并且在被打断时可能给出部分成功的印象——它不如 `--check` 硬。要做门禁，用 `--check` 而不是 `--verify`。

新 CLI 的 `nix store verify` 语义更严，且**退出码分级求和**（出处：`src/nix/verify.md`，渲染页 `https://nix.dev/manual/nix/latest/command-ref/new-cli/nix3-store-verify.html`）：

> This command verifies the integrity of the store paths *installables*, or, if `--all` is given, the entire Nix store. For each path, it checks that
>
> * its contents match the NAR hash recorded in the Nix database; and
> * it is *trusted*, that is, it is signed by at least one trusted signing key, is content-addressed, or is built locally ("ultimately trusted").
>
> # Exit status
>
> The exit status of this command is the **sum** of the following values:
>
> * **1** if any path is corrupted (i.e. its contents don't match the recorded NAR hash).
> * **2** if any path is untrusted.
> * **4** if any path couldn't be verified for any other reason (such as an I/O error).

**这一条直击本场景的痛点**：「一个『通过』既可能是零违规，也可能是未比基线更差」的根源是**通过只有一档**。Nix 的做法是让退出码本身携带失败类别（求和式位掩码）——门禁的输出不是一个布尔值，而是「哪一类问题、几个」。

#### (7) `outputChecks`：把「不允许变大/不允许引用某些东西」也变成构建期硬失败

出处：`src/libstore/build/derivation-check.cc`

```cpp
void checkCAOutput(
    const StoreDirConfig & store,
    const StorePath & drvPath,
    const DerivationOutput & outputSpec,
    const ValidPathInfo & info,
    const std::string & outputName)
{
    ...
            [&](const DerivationOutput::CAFixed & dof) {
                auto & wanted = dof.ca.hash;
                /* Check wanted hash */
                assert(info.ca);
                auto & got = info.ca->hash;
                if (wanted != got) {
                    throw BuildError(
                        BuildResult::Failure::HashMismatch,
                        "hash mismatch in fixed-output derivation '%s':\n  specified: %s\n     got:    %s",
                        store.printStorePath(drvPath),
                        wanted.to_string(HashFormat::SRI, true),
                        got.to_string(HashFormat::SRI, true));
                }
```

注意报错格式：**`specified:` 与 `got:` 并排打印**。这正是本场景缺的东西——「期望值 vs 实算值」必须一起出现，而不是只说「不一致」。

规模/引用约束（同文件）：

```cpp
            if (checks.maxSize && info.narSize > *checks.maxSize)
                throw BuildError(..., "path '%s' is too large at %d bytes; limit is %d bytes", ...);
            if (checks.maxClosureSize) {
                uint64_t closureSize = getClosure(info.path).second;
                if (closureSize > *checks.maxClosureSize)
                    throw BuildError(..., "closure of path '%s' is too large at %d bytes; limit is %d bytes", ...);
            }
```
以及 `allowedReferences` / `allowedRequisites` / `disallowedReferences` / `disallowedRequisites`：

```cpp
                if (!badPaths.empty()) {
                    throw BuildError(
                        BuildResult::Failure::OutputRejected,
                        "output '%s' is not allowed to refer to the following paths:%s", ...);
                }
            };
            /* Mandatory check: absent whitelist, and present but empty
               whitelist mean very different things. */
```

**「absent whitelist 与 present-but-empty whitelist 含义完全不同」**——把它和自建的「未比基线更差」对照：如果门禁配置缺失时默认放行，就会出现「一个通过既可能是零违规也可能是没检查」。

#### (8) `--check` 的正式入口：`nix store verify` 之外，还有 `nix store make-content-addressed`

出处：`src/nix/make-content-addressed.md`

> Nix store paths are usually *input-addressed*, meaning that the hash part of the store path is computed from the contents of the derivation (i.e., the build-time dependency graph). Input-addressed paths need to be signed by a trusted key if you want to import them into a store, because we need to trust that the contents of the path were actually built by the derivation.
>
> By contrast, in a *content-addressed* path, **the hash part is computed from the contents of the path. This allows the contents of the path to be verified without any additional information such as signatures.**

### D9. URL / 文件路径（本部分）

| 内容 | 出处 |
| --- | --- |
| `--rebuild` 定义 | `src/nix/build.cc`；`https://nix.dev/manual/nix/latest/command-ref/new-cli/nix3-build.html` |
| `--check` 语义（bitwise-identical） | `doc/manual/source/command-ref/nix-store/realise.md` |
| 比对判据 `narHash` + `NotDeterministic` | `src/libstore/build/derivation-builder-impl.cc` |
| bmCheck 不跳过已存在产物 | `src/libstore/build/derivation-building-goal.cc` |
| 失败类别枚举 | `src/libstore/include/nix/store/build-result.hh` |
| 退出码 0/1 与报错原文 | `doc/manual/source/advanced-topics/diff-hook.md` |
| `nix-store --verify --check-contents` | `doc/manual/source/command-ref/nix-store/verify.md` |
| `nix store verify` 求和退出码 | `src/nix/verify.md`；`https://nix.dev/manual/nix/latest/command-ref/new-cli/nix3-store-verify.html` |
| `HashMismatch` / `outputChecks` | `src/libstore/build/derivation-check.cc` |
| CA 免签名 | `src/nix/make-content-addressed.md` |

### D10. 对本场景的可迁移改法与代价

- **把「重算摘要」升级为「重算 + 逐字节比对 + 非零退出」**，一步都不许省：只重算不比对，等于把 Nix 的 `--verify` 当 `--check` 用。
- **失败要分类**：抄 `BuildResultFailureStatus` 与 `nix store verify` 的 1/2/4 求和退出码——「摘要不匹配」与「生成物缺失」应是不同出口，而不是同一个 `echo FAILED`。
- **报错必须打印 `specified:` / `got:` 两个值**（`derivation-check.cc` 的 `hash mismatch ... specified: %s got: %s`）。
- **缺配置时要 fail-closed**：`absent whitelist` 与 `empty whitelist` 必须区分（`derivation-check.cc` 注释）。
- **代价**：`--check` 需要产物已存在才能跑（`some outputs ... are not valid, so checking is not possible`）；每次门禁都真的重跑一遍构建，成本 = 一次全量构建；产物确定性不满足时门禁会稳定变红，必须先解决确定性（见第三部分）才能上这道门禁。

---

## 第三部分：可复现构建

### D11. 机制名

bit-by-bit identical 定义；可复现性「诫律」；`diffoscope` 逐字节比对；Nix 的确定性约束（`sandbox`、`pure-eval`、时间戳/路径泄漏）。

### D12. 项目

reproducible-builds.org（网站 + 规范）；diffoscope（Debian/Reproducible Builds 项目）；NixOS/nix。

### D13. 它怎么做

#### (1) 「可复现」的官方定义（逐字）

出处：`https://reproducible-builds.org/docs/definition/`

> A build is reproducible if given the same source code, build environment and build instructions, **any party can recreate bit-by-bit identical copies of all specified artifacts.**

> The reproducibility of artifacts is verified by **bit-by-bit comparison**. This is usually performed using cryptographically secure hash functions.

> Relevant attributes of the build environment would usually include dependencies and their versions, build configuration flags and environment variables as far as they are used by the build system (eg. the locale). **It is preferable to reduce this set of attributes.**

最后一句是核心：**输入集合越小，门禁越硬**。

#### (2) 可复现构建诫律（逐字，只摘与本场景相关的）

出处：`https://reproducible-builds.org/docs/commandments/`

> Thou shall not record the name of thy maker nor the place of thy making (username, hostname)
> Thou shall not record the date nor time of thy making, unless you respect the holy SDE spec (date+time)
> Thou shall not use memory without initialization or use memory addresses to decide outcomes (ASLR)
> **Thou shall do all your work in order - not use filesystem-readdir-order nor random order of hash elements**
> Thou shall not (gamble and) record random numbers (UUID, private/public key, hash-seed, ASLR)
> Thou shall only do one thing at a time or ensure races do no harm (parallelism)
> Thou shall not look at build machine processor capabilities (CPU)
> Thou shall not look at build machine benchmarks for optimizations
> Thou shall be careful with profile-guided-optimization for it can amplify any sin (non-determinism)
> Thou shall keep your workspace environment clean of timezones, locales and umasks or ensure they do no harm
> **Thou shall allow for offline builds (aka "vendoring" as servers can be down, contents can change)**
> **If Thou publishst binaries, Thou shall take note of your build inputs**

倒数第二条直接对应本场景：**外部依赖内容会变**，所以必须 vendoring/冻结，否则「重算摘要」永远不可复现。

#### (3) `diffoscope`：逐字节比对，且能解释**为什么**不同

官方工具页：`https://reproducible-builds.org/tools/`；项目首页：`https://diffoscope.org/`

> **diffoscope**
> diffoscope will try to get to the bottom of what makes files or directories different. It will **recursively unpack archives of many kinds and transform various binary formats into more human-readable forms for comparison**. It can compare two tarballs, ISO images, or PDFs just as easily.

`https://diffoscope.org/` 首页逐字：

> diffoscope
> In-depth comparison of files, archives, and directories.
> Latest release: 329 (21 Aug 2026)
> …
> diffoscope tries to get to the bottom of what makes files or directories different. It will recursively unpack archives of many kinds and transform various binary formats into more human-readable form to compare them. It can compare two tarballs, ISO images, or PDF just as easily.

Docker 一行试用（官方首页逐字）：

```
echo foo > file1
echo bar > file2
docker pull registry.salsa.debian.org/reproducible-builds/diffoscope
docker run --rm -t -w $(pwd) -v $(pwd):$(pwd):ro \
 registry.salsa.debian.org/reproducible-builds/diffoscope file1 file2
```

**未证实**：任务书给的 `https://reproducible-builds.org/docs/diffoscope/` 返回 **404**（已实测）。diffoscope 的官方入口是 `https://diffoscope.org/` 与 `https://reproducible-builds.org/tools/`。

同页另两个配套工具（对本场景的「不确定性必须被主动检测」很有用）：

> **reprotest** builds the same source code in different environments and then checks the binaries produced by the builds to see if **changing the environment, without changing the source code, changed the generated binaries.**

> **disorderfs** … is an overlay FUSE filesystem that **deliberately introduces non-determinism** into filesystem metadata. For example, it can randomize the order in which directory entries are read.

#### (4) Nix 的确定性约束

**(a) `sandbox`** 出处：`https://nix.dev/manual/nix/latest/command-ref/conf-file.html`（`sandbox` 设置）

> If set to `true`, builds are performed in a sandboxed environment, i.e., they're isolated from the normal file system hierarchy and **only see their dependencies in the Nix store**, the temporary build directory, private versions of `/proc`, `/dev`, `/dev/shm` and `/dev/pts` (on Linux), and the paths configured with the `sandbox-paths` option. **This is useful to prevent undeclared dependencies on files in directories such as `/usr/bin`.** In addition, on Linux, builds run in private PID, mount, network, IPC and UTS namespaces to isolate them from other processes in the system (except that fixed-output derivations do not run in private network namespace to ensure they can access the network).
>
> If this option is set to `relaxed`, then fixed-output derivations and derivations that have the `__noChroot` attribute set to `true` do not run in sandboxes.
>
> **The default is `true` on Linux and `false` on all other platforms.**
> Default: `true`
> Deprecated alias: `build-use-chroot`, `build-use-sandbox`

三个命令行开关（`src/libstore/globals.cc`，第 347/354/361 行）：

```cpp
        .description = "Enable sandboxing.",
        ...
        .description = "Disable sandboxing.",
        ...
        .description = "Enable sandboxing, but allow builds to disable it.",
```
即 `--sandbox` / `--no-sandbox` / `--relaxed-sandbox`。

**(b) `pure-eval`**（把「结果只由显式声明的输入决定」也变成开关）同出处：

> **pure-eval**
> Pure evaluation mode ensures that the result of Nix expressions is **fully determined by explicitly declared inputs, and not influenced by external state**:
>
> - Restrict file system and network access to files specified by cryptographic hash
> - Disable impure constants:
>   - `builtins.currentSystem`
>   - `builtins.currentTime`
>   - `builtins.nixPath`
>   - `builtins.storePath`
>
> Default: `false`

**(c) 时间戳/顺序/自引用泄漏**——见本报告 D3(5)：NAR 之所以不用 TAR/ZIP，第一条官方理由就是「非规范序列化 + 记录时间戳会导致本该相等的对象得到不同哈希」。自引用处理用哨兵值，官方自陈是 `hack`，且明确「泄漏临时路径 = 不确定性」（`failed to be deterministic ... leaking the choice of provisional store path`）。

**(d) 构建输入必须可离线**（对应诫律最后两条）——RFC 62 的 `Volatile inputs can disappear` 类问题在 Nix 里的解法是 fixed-output derivation 必须**提前给出哈希**：

出处：`NixOS/rfcs/rfcs/0062-content-addressed-paths.md`

> Nix already supports a special-case of content-addressed derivations with the so-called "fixed-output" derivations. These are derivations that are content-addressed, but **whose output hash has to be specified in advance**, and are used in particular to fetch data from the internet (as the constraint that the hash has to be specified in advance means that we can relax the sandbox for these derivations).

### D14. URL / 文件路径（本部分）

| 内容 | URL |
| --- | --- |
| 「可复现」定义（bit-by-bit identical） | `https://reproducible-builds.org/docs/definition/` |
| 诫律（12 条） | `https://reproducible-builds.org/docs/commandments/` |
| diffoscope 官方入口 | `https://diffoscope.org/` |
| 工具总览（diffoscope / reprotest / disorderfs） | `https://reproducible-builds.org/tools/` |
| `SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH` 规范 | `https://reproducible-builds.org/specs/source-date-epoch/`（页面可访问，实测 200） |
| Nix `sandbox` / `pure-eval` | `https://nix.dev/manual/nix/latest/command-ref/conf-file.html`；源码 `src/libstore/globals.cc` |
| NAR 规范化理由 | `doc/manual/source/store/file-system-object/content-address.md` |
| fixed-output 的「哈希必须提前给」 | `https://github.com/NixOS/rfcs/blob/master/rfcs/0062-content-addressed-paths.md` |

### D15. 对本场景的可迁移改法与代价

- **先把 6 份生成物的生成器变成确定性的**，再谈门禁。确定性不满足时 `--check` 型门禁会永久红。抄诫律的排序/时间戳/随机数三条。
- **18 条诫律里的「work in order」对应本场景最直接的 bug 源**：`filesystem-readdir-order`。生成物里任何「按目录遍历顺序拼接」的字段（包括摘要自身的输入）都可能在不同机器上不同 → 摘要不稳 → 门禁误报。
- **比对失败时用 diffoscope 同族思路输出「为什么不同」**，而不是只说 mismatch。Nix 的等价物是 `diff-hook`（只在结果不同时执行）。
- **配置 `sandbox`（或等价隔离）与 `pure-eval`（或等价「只依赖显式声明输入」）**，否则「通过」依赖于跑在谁的机器上。
- **代价**：`sandbox` 默认在 Linux 为 true、**其他平台为 false**——本机 macOS 上默认**不隔离**，需要显式配置，否则拿不到 Nix 在 Linux 上的同等保证。`diffoscope` 依赖大量解包器（官方首页列了上百种格式），CI 里装齐成本不低。

---

## 第四部分：锁文件即摘要门禁（跨生态，逐个取证）

本部分由三份子取证合并而成，每节格式统一为 **机制名 / 项目 / 它怎么做（含出处原文）/ URL 或文件路径 / 对本场景的可迁移改法与代价**。

> **总览：7 个生态的「冻结摘要 → 不一致非零退出」对照**
>
> | 生态 | 冻结载体 | 被冻结的是什么 | 不一致时的行为 |
> | --- | --- | --- | --- |
> | Cargo | `Cargo.lock` | 精确版本 + checksum | `--locked` 直接拒绝改锁文件 |
> | Go | `go.mod` + `go.sum` | 模块版本 + `h1:` 内容哈希 | `go mod tidy -diff` 非零退出且不改文件 |
> | npm | `package-lock.json` | `integrity`（SRI 字符串） | `npm ci` EINTEGRITY / EUSAGE 退出 1 |
> | Bazel | `MODULE.bazel.lock` | 模块解析结果 + 扩展结果 | `--lockfile_mode=error` 直接失败 |
> | Terraform | `.terraform.lock.hcl` | 提供者包内容哈希 `h1:` | 哈希不符则拒绝安装并提供 |
> | Gradle | `gradle/verification-metadata.xml` | 每个依赖的 checksum | `--dependency-verification=strict` 失败构建 |
> | Dhall | import 后的 `sha256:…` 语义哈希 | 规范化后的语义（β-normal form） | `dhall freeze --check` 逐字节比对文本 |

---

### D16. 机制 1 — Cargo `--locked` / `Cargo.lock`

**机制名**：`--locked`（三个子命令共用的门禁开关）
**项目**：Rust / Cargo

**它怎么做（出处原文逐字）**

官方手册 `https://doc.rust-lang.org/cargo/commands/cargo-build.html`（Manifest Options → `--locked`）：

> `--locked`
>
> Asserts that the exact same dependencies and versions are used as when the existing Cargo.lock file was originally generated. Cargo will exit with an error when either of the following scenarios arises:
>
> - The lock file is missing.
> - Cargo attempted to change the lock file due to a different dependency resolution.
>
> It may be used in environments where deterministic builds are desired, such as in CI pipelines.

同一段定义在 `cargo install --locked`（`https://doc.rust-lang.org/cargo/commands/cargo-install.html`）与 `cargo update --locked`（`.../cargo-update.html`）里**逐字相同** —— 即 `--locked` 是全局门禁语义。

`cargo install` 手册「Dealing with the Lockfile」一节：

> By default, the Cargo.lock file that is included with the package will be ignored. This means that Cargo will recompute which versions of dependencies to use, possibly using newer versions that have been released since the package was published. **The --locked flag can be used to force Cargo to use the packaged Cargo.lock file if it is available.** This may be useful for ensuring reproducible builds, to use the exact same set of dependencies that were available when the package was published.

命令行 `--help` 文本出自源码 `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/rust-lang/cargo/master/src/bin/cargo/cli.rs` 第 680 行（`flag("locked")`）：

> Assert that `Cargo.lock` will remain unchanged

**URL / 文件路径**

- `https://doc.rust-lang.org/cargo/commands/cargo-build.html`
- `https://doc.rust-lang.org/cargo/commands/cargo-install.html`
- `https://doc.rust-lang.org/cargo/commands/cargo-update.html`
- `https://doc.rust-lang.org/cargo/guide/cargo-toml-vs-cargo-lock.html`
- 源码：`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/rust-lang/cargo/master/src/bin/cargo/cli.rs`（默认分支 **master**，不是 main）

**已核实的偏差**：任务书预期的措辞「Assert that Cargo.lock is up to date」**不在官方手册正文里**；手册用「Asserts that the exact same dependencies and versions are used…」，`up to date` 类措辞只出现在 `--help` 文本（cli.rs）。**`src/bin/cargo/commands/build.rs` 不含 `--locked` 定义**（实测）。

**对本场景的可迁移改法与代价**

- 可迁移：`--locked` 的价值在于**把「是否可以改摘要」这件事变成一个开关，而开关的默认方向是拒绝**。对本场景：生成物摘要的重写命令必须存在，但**只能被人显式调用**；CI 里跑的永远是「不许改、改了就不一致就红」的那条路径。
- 代价：`--locked` 只保证「锁文件没被改」，不保证「锁文件里的内容是对的」—— 首次生成锁文件那一刻没有历史可比。这与 Terraform 官方自陈的 TOFU 是同一个洞（见 D18）。本场景里表现就是那句「一个『通过』既可能是零违规，也可能是未比基线更差」。

---

### D17. 机制 2 — Go `go mod tidy -diff` / `go mod verify`

**机制名**：`-diff`（只算不改 + 非零退出）+ `go mod verify`（落地层校验）
**项目**：Go

**它怎么做（出处原文逐字）**

`go mod tidy -diff`，出处 `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/golang/website/master/_content/ref/mod.md`（对应网页 `https://go.dev/ref/mod#go-mod-tidy`）：

> `-diff` flag causes `go mod tidy` not to modify go.mod or go.sum but instead print the necessary changes as a unified diff. **It exits with a non-zero code if the diff is not empty.**

Go 1.23 release notes（`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/golang/website/master/_content/doc/go1.23.md`）：

> The new `go` `mod` `tidy` `-diff` flag causes the command not to modify the files but instead print the necessary changes as a unified diff. **It exits with a non-zero code if updates are needed.**

`go mod verify`（`https://go.dev/ref/mod#go-mod-verify`）：

> `go mod verify` checks that dependencies of the main module stored in the module cache have not been modified since they were downloaded. To perform this check, **`go mod verify` hashes each downloaded module `.zip` file and extracted directory, then compares those hashes with a hash recorded when the module was first downloaded.** … If all the modules are unmodified, `go mod verify` prints "all modules verified". Otherwise, it reports which modules have been changed and **exits with a non-zero status**.

官方还显式划出**两条门禁的边界**（对本场景极重要）：

> Note that all module-aware commands verify that hashes in the main module's `go.sum` file match hashes recorded for modules downloaded into the module cache. …
>
> In contrast, `go mod verify` checks that module `.zip` files and their extracted directories have hashes that match hashes recorded in the module cache when they were first downloaded. This is useful for **detecting changes to files in the module cache *after* a module has been downloaded and verified**.

**URL / 文件路径**

- `https://go.dev/ref/mod#go-mod-tidy`
- `https://go.dev/doc/go1.23`
- `https://go.dev/ref/mod#go-mod-verify`
- 官方 Markdown 源：`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/golang/website/master/_content/ref/mod.md`、`.../_content/doc/go1.23.md`
- 源码 help 文本：`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/golang/go/master/src/cmd/go/alldocs.go`
- **未证实**：`raw.githubusercontent.com/golang/go/master/doc/go1.23.md` 返回 404（release notes 托管在 golang/website）

**对本场景的可迁移改法与代价**

- **`-diff` 是本场景最该直接抄的一条**：它同时做到「打印必要变更」「不改文件」「有变更就非零退出」。这正是把「重算摘要 50 次」变成一次 CI 调用所需的全部。
- **两层门禁要分清**（官方用 `In contrast` 点明）：`go.sum`/声明层校验 ≠ 落地层校验。本场景的 834 个同步点里，「源文件 → 6 份生成物」是落地层，「生成物 → 摘要文件」是声明层，两层的失败必须能分别定位。
- 代价：`-diff` 只能回答「当前磁盘与重算结果是否一致」，无法回答「这次的不一致比上次更糟吗」——**基线维度需要自己在外面加**（见第五部分）。

---

### D18. 机制 3 — `npm ci` + `package-lock.json` 的 `integrity`

**机制名**：`npm ci`（锁文件与清单必须一致，否则退出 1）+ `integrity` 字段（SRI 内容哈希）
**项目**：npm / Node.js

**它怎么做（出处原文逐字）**

`https://docs.npmjs.com/cli/v10/commands/npm-ci`：

> The `npm ci` command … is meant to be used in automated environments such as test platforms, continuous integration, and deployment — or any situation where you want to make sure you're doing a clean install of your dependencies.
>
> It will exit with an error if `package-lock.json` does not match `package.json`, or if `node_modules` is already present.

`https://docs.npmjs.com/cli/v11/configuring-npm/package-lock-json`（`integrity` 字段）：

> `integrity`: This is **a `sha512` or `sha1` Standard Subresource Integrity string** for the artifact that was unpacked in this location.

**已核实的偏差**：任务书预期的「a SHA-512 hash of the tarball」**不是官方原文**；官方写的是「a `sha512` or `sha1` Standard Subresource Integrity string for the artifact that was unpacked in this location」（SRI 规范：`https://w3c.github.io/webappsec/specs/subresourceintegrity/`）。

官方对「不要手工编辑锁文件」的约束（同页）：

> `package-lock.json` … is **never intended to be published** … and is **not to be edited by hand**.

**URL / 文件路径**

- `https://docs.npmjs.com/cli/v10/commands/npm-ci`
- `https://docs.npmjs.com/cli/v11/configuring-npm/package-lock-json`
- 源码文档源：`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/npm/cli/latest/docs/lib/content/commands/npm-ci.md`、`.../configuring-npm/package-lock-json.md`

**对本场景的可迁移改法与代价**

- `npm ci` 的语义是「**要么完全按锁文件装，要么整体失败**」—— 没有「部分不符但继续」这个中间态。本场景的 6 份生成物同步可以用同样的原子性：**6 份里任何 1 份不符就整体红**，而不是「打了 5 个勾、1 个警告」。
- `integrity` 用的是 SRI 字符串（带算法前缀），不是裸哈希 —— 好处是**算法可升级、可并存**。本场景的摘要字段建议采用同款带前缀形式（`sha256-…`），避免将来换算法时无法同时表达新旧值。
- 代价：`npm ci` 每次都清空 `node_modules` 重装，代价是时间；本场景类比 = 每次都全量重算 6 份生成物（139×6），必须能并行化，否则门禁会慢到没人用。

---

### D19. 机制 4 — Bazel `--lockfile_mode=error` / `MODULE.bazel.lock`

**机制名**：`--lockfile_mode=error`（信息过期即 fail）
**项目**：Bazel（bzlmod）

**它怎么做（出处原文逐字）**

官方 flag 文档 `https://bazel.build/reference/command-line-reference`：

> `--lockfile_mode off, update, refresh or error default: "update"`
>
> Specifies how and whether or not to use the lockfile. Valid values are `update` to use the lockfile and update it if there are changes, `refresh` to additionally refresh mutable information (yanked versions and previously missing modules) from remote registries from time to time, **`error` to use the lockfile but throw an error if needed information is not up-to-date** (superfluous entries that the current resolution does not consult are ignored, not reported), or `off` to neither read from or write to the lockfile.

官方「Lockfile Usage」一节 `https://bazel.build/external/lockfile`：

> **`error`: Like `update`, but if any information consulted during resolution is missing or out-of-date, Bazel will fail with an error. This mode never changes the lockfile or performs network requests during resolution.** … Note that superfluous information is ignored, not reported, so a lockfile that passes in this mode may nevertheless be rewritten (pruned) by `update`.

真正抛错的那句英文（源码逐字），`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bazelbuild/bazel/master/src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/bazel/bzlmod/SingleExtensionEvalFunction.java` 第 504–512 行：

```java
  private static SingleExtensionEvalFunctionException createOutdatedLockfileException(
      String reason) {
    return new SingleExtensionEvalFunctionException(
        ExternalDepsException.withMessage(
            Code.BAD_LOCKFILE,
            "MODULE.bazel.lock is no longer up-to-date because %s. Please run `bazel mod deps"
                + " --lockfile_mode=update` to update your lockfile.",
            reason));
  }
```

flag 定义与默认值（源码 `.../bazel/repository/RepositoryOptions.java` 第 419–432 行）：`defaultValue = "update"` —— **门禁默认不开，必须显式传 `--lockfile_mode=error`**。

**URL / 文件路径**

- `https://bazel.build/reference/command-line-reference`
- `https://bazel.build/external/lockfile`
- `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bazelbuild/bazel/master/src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/bazel/repository/RepositoryOptions.java`
- `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bazelbuild/bazel/master/src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/bazel/bzlmod/SingleExtensionEvalFunction.java`
- `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bazelbuild/bazel/master/src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/bazel/bzlmod/BazelLockFileModule.java`

**未证实**：`https://bazel.build/versions/8.0.0/external/lockfile` 返回 404（无版本化文档 URL）；`LockfileMode.java` 作为独立文件在 master 不存在（枚举实际在 `RepositoryOptions.java`，第 502–507 行）；该枚举内 `UPDATE`/`ERROR` 两行的**注释本身在上游就是截断的**（`Update the lockfile wh` / `Throw an error when it mismatc`），三份来源（master / tag 8.0.0 / jsDelivr 镜像）一致 —— **不可引用这两行注释**，权威定义只有 flag help 串。

**对本场景的可迁移改法与代价**

- `error` 模式的两条附加强约束值得照抄：**「never changes the lockfile」**（门禁路径绝不允许有写权限）与**「never performs network requests during resolution」**（门禁路径绝不允许依赖外网）。本场景的 834 个同步点门禁也必须满足这两条，否则「通过」依赖于网络与写权限时序。
- 官方自己点出的盲区（`superfluous information is ignored, not reported`）：**锁文件能通过 `error` 但仍是脏的**。本场景的对应风险是「摘要文件自洽但与生成物已无对应关系」。
- 代价：`--lockfile_mode=error` 与 Bazel 版本强绑定（锁文件格式与 Bazel 版本耦合），升级 Bazel 需要重生成锁文件 —— 门禁的稳定性依赖于工具版本冻结。

---

### D20. 机制 5 — Terraform `.terraform.lock.hcl` 的 `h1:` 与 `terraform providers lock`

**机制名**：`h1:`（内容哈希）+ `zh:`（旧 zip 哈希）+ `terraform providers lock`（显式重算入口）
**项目**：Terraform / HashiCorp

**它怎么做（出处原文逐字）**

出处 `https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/language/files/dependency-lock`（mdx 源：`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hashicorp/web-unified-docs/main/content/terraform/v1.16.x/docs/language/files/dependency-lock.mdx`）：

> The `h1:` and `zh:` prefixes on these values represent different hashing schemes, each of which represents calculating a checksum using a different algorithm.
>
> - `zh:`: a mnemonic for "zip hash", this is a legacy hash format which is part of the Terraform provider registry protocol … This hashing scheme captures a SHA256 hash of each of the official `.zip` packages indexed in the origin registry.
> - **`h1:`: a mnemonic for "hash scheme 1", which is the current preferred hashing scheme. Hash scheme 1 is also a SHA256 hash, but is one computed from the *contents* of the provider distribution package, rather than of the `.zip` archive it's contained within.** This scheme therefore has the advantage that it can be calculated for an official `.zip` file, an unpacked directory with the same contents, or a recompressed `.zip` file which contains the same files but potentially different metadata or compression schemes.

`hashes` 字段语义（章节 `Understanding Lock File Changes`）：

> - `hashes`: a number of checksums that are all considered to be valid for packages implementing the selected version of this provider on different platforms.

门禁判据（章节 `Checksum verification`）：

> Terraform will also verify that each package it installs **matches at least one of the checksums it previously recorded in the lock file**, if any, returning an error if none of the checksums match:
>
> ```
> Error: Failed to install provider
>
> Error while installing hashicorp/azurerm v2.1.0: the current package for
> registry.terraform.io/hashicorp/azurerm 2.1.0 doesn't match any of the
> checksums previously recorded in the dependency lock file.
> ```
>
> This checksum verification is intended to represent a *trust on first use* approach.

锁文件的自增长规则（弱化门禁的一条）：

> Terraform will add a new hash to an existing provider **only if the hash is calculated from a package that *also* matches one of the existing hashes.**

重算入口（`https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/cli/commands/providers/lock`）：`terraform providers lock`，支持 `-platform`、`-fs-mirror`、`-net-mirror`，用途是在 CI 之前把多平台 `h1:` 一次写全。

**URL / 文件路径**

- `https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/language/files/dependency-lock`
- `https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/cli/commands/providers/lock`
- `https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/internals/provider-registry-protocol`
- `https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/internals/provider-network-mirror-protocol`
- mdx 源：`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hashicorp/web-unified-docs/main/content/terraform/v1.16.x/docs/language/files/dependency-lock.mdx`

**未证实**：`h1:` 的**精确算法**与 Terraform 源码侧实现（如 `internal/getproviders/hash.go`）未取证 —— 只取到官方文档级别的「contents 而非 zip 容器」描述。

**对本场景的可迁移改法与代价**

- **`h1:` vs `zh:` 的取舍直接对应本场景的假阳性问题**：哈希「容器」会在重新打包/换压缩元数据时失配；哈希「内容」不会。本场景的 6 份生成物摘要必须选**内容**而非**文件字节**，否则每次格式化/换行符/LF-CR 都会假红。
- **一个版本对应多个 `h1:`，判定是「至少命中一个」** —— 多平台/多目标产物要用同一模式：摘要文件里存一组可接受值，门禁判「命中其一」。
- **`terraform providers lock` 是「显式重算入口」的范式**：重算摘要必须是一条**独立、可批量、可 diff** 的官方命令，而不是散落的手工步骤。这正是把「重算摘要 50 次」变成「一条命令 + `git diff --exit-code`」。
- 代价（必须写清）：**TOFU** —— 首次记录那一刻没有历史可比，「通过」只代表「当时我信任了抓到的包」。锁文件还会在日常 `init` 中**自己长出新的 `h1:`**（`opportunistically add … as it learns of them`），所以「锁文件没变」≠「没人动过」。这两条正是本场景「一个通过既可能是零违规，也可能是未比基线更差」的同构案例。

---

### D21. 机制 6 — Gradle `--dependency-verification=strict` + `gradle/verification-metadata.xml`

**机制名**：依赖校验严格模式 + 内容摘要清单
**项目**：Gradle

**它怎么做（出处原文逐字）**

出处 `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html`

> Dependency verification is automatically enabled as soon as the verification metadata file is detected.
> The file must be located at `$PROJECT_ROOT/gradle/verification-metadata.xml`.

因果链：

> You record the expected checksum (for example, SHA-256) of a library or plugin in the `gradle/verification-metadata.xml` file.
> When the build downloads that artifact, it computes the checksum of the downloaded bytes.
> **If the computed checksum doesn't match the expected value, the build fails instead of using the tampered file.**

三档模式（官方表格）：

| Mode | Description |
|---|---|
| `strict` (default) | `Verification fails as early as possible to avoid using compromised dependencies during the build` |
| `lenient` | `Runs the build even if there are verification failures. Verification errors are displayed without causing build failure` |
| `off` | `Verification is completely ignored` |

CLI 参考页（`https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/command_line_interface.html`）：

> `-F=(strict,lenient,off), --dependency-verification=(strict,lenient,off)`
> Configures the dependency verification mode.
> **The default mode is strict.**

生成命令与默认路径：

> `$ ./gradlew --write-verification-metadata sha256`
> - It scans all the dependencies in your project.
> - It calculates the SHA-256 checksum for each.
> - It creates a file located at `gradle/verification-metadata.xml`.

**官方自己承认的 TOFU 漏洞**（与本场景痛点同构，可直接引用）：

> Gradle can automatically generate a dependency verification file by downloading all your dependencies and recording their checksums or signatures. This is called bootstrapping.
> However, bootstrapping has a critical security limitation: **it trusts whatever is currently in your repositories.** If a malicious dependency has already been introduced into your build (or into a repository you use), Gradle will record the compromised artifact's checksum or signature.
> This is why you must review the generated verification file.

**把「谁审过」编码进锁文件本身**（直接对症「通过的含义不明确」）：

> Gradle-generated checksums will have an `origin` attribute starting with `"Generated by Gradle"` to indicate **they need review**.
> …
> - Update all "Generated by Gradle" origins
> - **Change `origin` to `"Verified"`** — After verifying the checksum matches, edit the origin attribute to `"Verified"` to indicate you've confirmed it.

**URL / 文件路径**

- `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html`
- `https://docs.gradle.org/8.5/userguide/dependency_verification.html`
- `https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/command_line_interface.html`

**已核实的偏差**：任务书预期的原文「fails the build on any verification failure」**在官方文档中不存在**；实际措辞是 `Verification fails as early as possible to avoid using compromised dependencies during the build`。**Gradle 仓库的 adoc 源码 5 条候选路径全部 404** —— 本节的 Gradle 引用均来自官方渲染页，非仓库 `.adoc` 源。`DependencyVerificationMode` 源码类的 3 条路径亦全部 404。本机无 gradle，未实测运行。

**对本场景的可迁移改法与代价**

- **`origin="Generated by Gradle"` → `origin="Verified"` 是本报告里最值得照抄的一条**：它把「这个摘要是机器生成的、还没人审」与「这个摘要有人确认过」编码进锁文件本身。本场景的「通过」之所以含糊，正是因为**没有人审过这个标记** —— 加上这一位状态，一个 `通过` 就能区分「零违规」与「机器重算过一次」。
- **strict 是默认值**（Gradle）—— 对比 Bazel 的 `update` 默认值与 npm/cargo 的显式 `--locked`，**默认方向的选择本身就是门禁强度的一部分**。
- 代价：校验元数据文件会随依赖数量膨胀，且每加一个依赖都要重生成 + 人工复核 diff；官方明确 `bootstrapping … is not a substitute for manual verification of critical dependencies`。本场景 139 个包 × 6 份生成物的人工复核成本必须靠分级（关键项人工 + 一般项自动）来控。

---

### D22. 机制 7 — Dhall `dhall freeze` / `--check` / `lint` / `format --check`

**机制名**：语义哈希（sha256 of αβ-normal form）冻结 + 幂等性断言（`--check`）
**项目**：Dhall（dhall-lang / dhall-haskell）

**它怎么做（出处原文逐字）**

哈希字段的定义（`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dhall-lang/dhall-haskell/master/dhall/src/Dhall/Import.hs`）：

```haskell
-- | Hash a fully resolved, αβ-normal expression
--
-- Note that the argument must be already normalized: this function hashes the
-- αβ-normal form.  Hashing a typechecked-but-unreduced tree (for example the
-- result of 'load' of an unhashed @Code@ import) produces a value that is
-- __not__ a valid integrity hash.
hashExpression :: Expr Void Void -> Dhall.Crypto.SHA256Digest
hashExpression = Dhall.Crypto.sha256Hash . encodeExpression
```

```haskell
hashExpressionToCode :: Expr Void Void -> Text
hashExpressionToCode expr =
    "sha256:" <> Text.pack (show (hashExpression expr))
```

`dhall freeze` 是**无条件重算**（`dhall/src/Dhall/Freeze.hs`）：

```haskell
freezeImportWithSettings settings directory import_ = do
    let unprotectedImport =
            import_
                { importHashed =
                    (importHashed import_)
                        { hash = Nothing
                        }
                }
    ...
    let normalizedExpression = Core.alphaNormalize (Core.normalizeWith (view Dhall.normalizer settings) expression)
    ...
    let expressionHash = Dhall.Import.hashExpression normalizedExpression
    let newImportHashed = (importHashed import_) { hash = Just expressionHash }
```

源码注释解释这个选择：

```haskell
                {- Here we could actually compare the `_expectedHash` and
                   `_actualHash` to see if they differ, but we choose not to do
                   so and instead automatically accept the `_actualHash`.  This
                   is done for the same reason that the `freeze*` functions
                   ignore hash mismatches: the user intention when using `dhall
                   freeze` is to update the hash, which they expect to possibly
                   change.
                -}
```

`--check` 的实现 —— **判定的是「重新生成的文本是否与磁盘逐字节相同」**：

```haskell
            Check ->
                return $
                    if originalText == modifiedText
                        then Right ()
                        else Left CheckFailed{..}
```

（`dhall format --check` 用同一模式，见 `dhall/src/Dhall/Format.hs`。）

失败即非零退出（`dhall/src/Dhall/Util.hs`）：

```haskell
handleMultipleChecksFailed command modified f xs = post =<< mapM f xs
  where
    post results =
        case lefts (toList results) of
            [] -> pure ()
            cf:cfs -> Control.Exception.throwIO $ MultipleCheckFailed
                { command
                , modified
                , inputs = fmap input (cf:|cfs)
                }
```

报错文案：

```haskell
        _ERROR <> ": ❰dhall " <> command_ <> " --check❱ failed on:\n\
        \\n" <> files <>
        "\n\
        \You ran ❰dhall " <> command_ <> " --check❱, but the input appears to have not\n\
        \been " <> modified_ <> " before, or was changed since the last time the input\n\
        \was " <> modified_ <> ".\n"
```

`Secure` vs `Cache` 两种意图（源码枚举，官方注释逐字）：

```haskell
data Intent
    = Secure
    -- ^ Protect imports with an integrity check without a fallback so that
    --   import resolution fails if the import changes
    | Cache
    -- ^ Protect imports with an integrity check and also add a fallback import
    --   import without an integrity check.
```

**URL / 文件路径**

- `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dhall-lang/dhall-haskell/master/dhall/src/Dhall/Freeze.hs`
- `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dhall-lang/dhall-haskell/master/dhall/src/Dhall/Import.hs`
- `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dhall-lang/dhall-haskell/master/dhall/src/Dhall/Lint.hs`
- `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dhall-lang/dhall-haskell/master/dhall/src/Dhall/Format.hs`
- `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dhall-lang/dhall-haskell/master/dhall/src/Dhall/Main.hs`
- `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dhall-lang/dhall-haskell/master/dhall/src/Dhall/Util.hs`
- `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dhall-lang/dhall-lang/master/standard/imports.md`
- `https://docs.dhall-lang.org/discussions/Safety-guarantees.html`

**已核实的偏差（3 条，必须照实写）**

1. `dhall freeze --check` 比的是**重新生成的文本与磁盘文本逐字节相同**，**不是**运行时的哈希校验（后者属 `Dhall.Import`）。任务书把两者混为一谈了。
2. 任务书给的 `https://docs.dhall-lang.org/howtos/How-to-freeze-remote-imports.html` 返回 **404**（该页不存在）。
3. `dhall-lang` 的两份 `README.md` 中**没有任何** freeze/lint/hash 正文。

本机无 dhall，未实测运行。

**对本场景的可迁移改法与代价**

- **「哈希 of 语义（αβ-normal form）而非字节」** 是本报告第二条最重要的可迁移点（第一条是 RFC 62 的 `resolved` 非单射性）。对本场景：如果 6 份生成物只是同一语义的不同表示（换行、缩进、字段顺序），摘要应当对**语义**算，不该对字节算 —— 否则门禁会淹没在格式化噪音里。Dhall 的做法就是先把表达式规范化到 αβ-normal form 再哈希。
- **`dhall freeze` 无条件重算 + 丢掉旧 hash（`hash = Nothing`）** 意味着重算是一次干净的重建，不受旧值影响 —— 本场景的「重算摘要」也必须这样实现（先清空再算），否则容易写成「比对旧值后决定要不要改」的循环依赖。
- **`Secure` vs `Cache` 两种意图**：同一个哈希字段可以有两种语义（改了就必须失败 / 改了可回退）。本场景的摘要也应显式声明属于哪种，而不是让读者猜。
- **`--check` 是幂等性断言**，配合 CI 使用：`dhall freeze --check` / `dhall lint --check` / `dhall format --check` 三连 —— 本场景等价于「重算摘要 / 规范生成物 / 格式化」三条各自独立的 `--check`，每条都能单独红，定位不需要看日志。
- 代价：语义哈希要求有一个**权威的规范化器**（Dhall 有 `Dhall.normalizer`）。本场景若自建，规范化器本身会成为新的单点——规范化器一改，所有历史摘要全失效。必须把规范化器版本一起冻结进摘要（或用带版本前缀的摘要格式）。

---

## 第五部分：共同模式提炼 —— 为什么「摘要在源里冻结 → 任何不一致直接非零退出」比「比历史基线更好就算通过」更硬

### D23. 共同模式的五个要素（每一条都有前面 7 个生态的原文支撑）

**① 摘要写在源里，与源同一个版本控制单元**

- Cargo：`Cargo.lock` 与 `Cargo.toml` 同仓
- Go：`go.sum` 与 `go.mod` 同仓
- npm：`package-lock.json` 与 `package.json` 同仓
- Bazel：`MODULE.bazel.lock` 与 `MODULE.bazel` 同仓
- Terraform：`.terraform.lock.hcl` 与 `.tf` 同仓
- Gradle：`gradle/verification-metadata.xml` 在 `$PROJECT_ROOT` 下
- Dhall：`sha256:…` 直接写在 import 语句行内

**关键推论**：摘要的变更会出现在 **同一个 diff** 里。review 时「摘要变了」和「源变了」是**同一屏**，不存在「摘要在别处悄悄漂移」。本场景「改 1 个文件要手工同步 6 份」的根因是这 6 份不在同一决策单元里。

**② 判定是「相等/不相等」，不是「更好/更差」**

- `nix-store --realise --check`：`checks whether the result is bitwise-identical with the existing outputs, printing an error if that's not the case`
- `nix build --rebuild`：`Rebuild an already built package and compare the result to the existing store paths`
- `go mod tidy -diff`：`It exits with a non-zero code if the diff is not empty`
- `dhall freeze --check`：`if originalText == modifiedText then Right () else Left CheckFailed{..}`
- npm `npm ci`：`exit with an error if package-lock.json does not match package.json`
- Bazel `error`：`throw an error if needed information is not up-to-date`
- Gradle：`If the computed checksum doesn't match the expected value, the build fails`
- Terraform：`returning an error if none of the checksums match`

**八处一致地都用了二元判定。没有一个用「比上次好」。**

**③ 「更好/更差」判定为什么结构性地更弱**

这一段是本报告的核心论证。**前 7 个生态中没有任何一个采用「比历史基线更好」作为门禁判据**，这一点本身就是证据。原因在原文里可以逐条对上：

- **基线的可信度上限 = 首次记录的那一刻**。Terraform 官方：`This checksum verification is intended to represent a *trust on first use* approach`；Gradle 官方：`bootstrapping has a critical security limitation: it trusts whatever is currently in your repositories`。
- **基线可以被「合法地」改写**。Terraform 官方：`Terraform will add a new hash to an existing provider only if the hash is calculated from a package that also matches one of the existing hashes` —— 锁文件会自己长大。Bazel 官方：`superfluous information is ignored, not reported, so a lockfile that passes in this mode may nevertheless be rewritten (pruned) by update` —— 能通过但仍脏。
- **一旦判据含「更好」，比对双方就必须都可信**：如果基线本身是脏的，「比基线好」这个结论没有任何信息量。这正是本场景「一个『通过』既可能是零违规，也可能是未比历史基线更差」—— **问题不在判据松，而在判据的输出只有一档**。

**④ 失败必须是分类的、带两个值的、机器可读的**

- Nix：`BuildResultFailureStatus` 里 `NotDeterministic` 与 `HashMismatch` 是独立类别；`nix store verify` 退出码 **1/2/4 求和**（corrupted / untrusted / 其他）
- Nix 报错格式：`hash mismatch in fixed-output derivation '…':\n  specified: %s\n     got:    %s` —— **期望值与实算值并排**
- npm：`EINTEGRITY` 与 `EUSAGE` 是两个不同退出原因，`EINTEGRITY` 同框打印 wanted vs got
- Bazel：`Code.BAD_LOCKFILE` + `MODULE.bazel.lock is no longer up-to-date because %s`
- Go：`go mod verify` `reports which modules have been changed`（不是只报「失败」）
- Gradle：`origin="Generated by Gradle"` vs `origin="Verified"` —— **把「是否人工审过」也编码进判据**

本场景要修的正是这里：**让「通过」带上来源和类别**。「摘要与重算一致」与「摘要被人工确认过」必须是两个可分别查询的状态（抄 Gradle 的 origin 位）。

**⑤ 门禁必须有一条显式的「重算入口」，且默认方向是拒绝**

- Cargo：`--locked` 是显式开关，默认不加
- Go：`-diff` 是显式开关，且**只算不改**
- Bazel：`defaultValue = "update"`（**门禁默认不开**），必须显式 `--lockfile_mode=error`
- Gradle：**默认 `strict`**（默认拒绝）
- Nix：`--check` / `--rebuild` 是显式开关
- Dhall：`dhall freeze` 显式触发重算，`--check` 显式触发校验

对照本场景的 50 次手工「重算摘要」：这 50 次之所以是手工的，是因为**没有一条被命名、被版本化、可批量调用的重算命令**。Terraform 的 `terraform providers lock` 与 Gradle 的 `--write-verification-metadata sha256` 是正面样板。

### D24. 可直接落地的五条改法（按投入排序）

1. **加一条重算命令**，行为对标 `go mod tidy -diff`：算、打印 diff、**不改文件**、有差异非零退出。CI 里跑它即可。成本：低。
2. **摘要改为对「内容/语义」而非「容器字节」**（抄 Terraform `h1:` 的取舍 + Dhall 的 αβ-normal form）。成本：中，需要定义规范化器。（对应 Nix 的 NAR 三原则：目录项排序、长度前缀、剔除时间戳。）
3. **加「是否人工确认」位**（抄 Gradle 的 `origin="Generated by Gradle"` → `"Verified"`）。这一步直接消灭「一个通过既可能是零违规」的歧义。成本：低。
4. **失败分类 + 期望值/实算值并排打印**（抄 `BuildResultFailureStatus` + `specified:/got:`）。成本：低。
5. **切断级联**（抄 RFC 62 的 `resolved` 非单射性）：让生成物之间只通过内容的摘要互相引用，而不是通过版本号/构建序号。这样上游改了但产物摘要没变时，下游 6 份**不需要重算**——这是把 834 个同步点压下去的唯一结构性办法。成本：高，需要改引用模型。

### 1. Cargo `--locked`

#### 机制名
`--locked`：宣告「现有 `Cargo.lock` 必须保持不变」，把锁文件当作**不可变更的输入**而非可刷新产物。锁文件一旦需要被改写，Cargo 直接以非零码退出。

#### 项目
Cargo（Rust 官方包管理器）

#### 它怎么做

##### 出处原文 A：`cargo build` 手册 `--locked`

URL：https://doc.rust-lang.org/cargo/commands/cargo-build.html
HTML 缓存：`/tmp/eng_research/raw/cargo-build.html`

逐字原文（Manifest Options → `--locked`）：

> `--locked`
>
> Asserts that the exact same dependencies and versions are used as when the
> existing Cargo.lock file was originally generated. Cargo will exit with an
> error when either of the following scenarios arises:
>
> - The lock file is missing.
> - Cargo attempted to change the lock file due to a different dependency resolution.
>
> It may be used in environments where deterministic builds are desired,
> such as in CI pipelines.

注意：**任务书预期的措辞 "Assert that Cargo.lock is up to date / does not need to be updated" 并未出现在现行官方手册中**。手册正文用的是上面这段 "Asserts that the exact same dependencies and versions are used..."。真正使用 `up to date` 类措辞的是**命令行 `--help` 文本**（见出处 C）。这一点已核实，不是印象描述。

##### 出处原文 B：`cargo install` 手册 — 「Dealing with the Lockfile」

URL：https://doc.rust-lang.org/cargo/commands/cargo-install.html
HTML 缓存：`/tmp/eng_research/raw/cargo-install.html`

逐字原文（Dealing with the Lockfile 一节）：

> By default, the Cargo.lock file that is included with the package will be
> ignored. This means that Cargo will recompute which versions of dependencies
> to use, possibly using newer versions that have been released since the
> package was published. The --locked flag can be used to force Cargo to use
> the packaged Cargo.lock file if it is available. This may be useful for
> ensuring reproducible builds, to use the exact same set of dependencies that
> were available when the package was published. It may also be useful if a
> newer version of a dependency is published that no longer builds on your
> system, or has other problems. The downside to using --locked is that you
> will not receive any fixes or updates to any dependency. Note that Cargo did
> not start publishing Cargo.lock files until version 1.37, which means
> packages published with prior versions will not have a Cargo.lock file
> available.

同一份手册的 Manifest Options 段落里，`cargo install --locked` 的描述与 `cargo build --locked` **完全一致**（同一段 "Asserts that the exact same dependencies and versions are used..."）。
`cargo update --locked` 的描述也逐字相同（https://doc.rust-lang.org/cargo/commands/cargo-update.html ，缓存 `cargo-update.html`）——即 `--locked` 是**全局门禁语义**，三个子命令共用同一段定义。

##### 出处原文 C：源码中的 help 文本（一手源码路径）

源码文件：https://raw.githubusercontent.com/rust-lang/cargo/master/src/bin/cargo/cli.rs
本地缓存：`/tmp/eng_research/raw/c2_src_bin_cargo_cli.rs`（30805 字节，master 分支，HTTP 200）

第 680 行逐字原文：

```rust
        .arg(
            flag("locked", "Assert that `Cargo.lock` will remain unchanged")
                .help_heading(heading::MANIFEST_OPTIONS)
                .global(true),
        )
```

第 690 行 `--frozen`：

```rust
        .arg(
            flag("frozen", "Equivalent to specifying both --locked and --offline")
                .help_heading(heading::MANIFEST_OPTIONS)
                .global(true),
        )
```

第 438-440 行显示 `locked` 被合成为全局上下文：

```rust
    let frozen = args.flag("frozen") || global_args.frozen;
    let locked = args.flag("locked") || global_args.locked;
    let offline = args.flag("offline") || global_args.offline;
```

本机 `cargo build --help` 实测输出（与源码逐字一致）：

```
      --locked                Assert that `Cargo.lock` will remain unchanged
      --offline               Run without accessing the network
      --frozen                Equivalent to specifying both --locked and --offline
```

`src/bin/cargo/commands/build.rs`（https://raw.githubusercontent.com/rust-lang/cargo/master/src/bin/cargo/commands/build.rs ，master，HTTP 200）中 **build.rs 自身不含 `Args::locked` 字面量**——`--locked` 由全局 `cli.rs` 注入，`build.rs` 只调用 `args.compile_options(gctx, UserIntent::Build, Some(&ws), ProfileChecking::Custom)?`。这解释了为什么 `--locked` 是全局选项而非 build 独有。
（main 分支该文件返回 404，仓库默认分支为 master。）

##### 出处原文 D：`Cargo.lock` 的角色

URL：https://doc.rust-lang.org/cargo/guide/cargo-toml-vs-cargo-lock.html
HTML 缓存：`/tmp/eng_research/raw/cargo-toml-vs-lock.html`

逐字原文：

> Cargo.toml vs Cargo.lock
>
> Cargo.toml and Cargo.lock serve two different purposes. Before we talk
> about them, here's a summary:
>
> - Cargo.toml is about describing your dependencies in a broad sense, and is written by you.
> - Cargo.lock contains exact information about your dependencies. It is maintained by Cargo and should not be manually edited.
>
> When in doubt, check Cargo.lock into the version control system (e.g. Git).

> Enter the Cargo.lock. Because of its existence, you don't need to manually
> keep track of the exact revisions: Cargo will do it for you.

> Now when you give your package to someone else,
> they'll use the exact same SHA, even though you didn't specify it in your
> Cargo.toml.

##### 本机实跑复现（真实终端输出）

目录：`/tmp/eng_research/demo/cargo_demo`

**场景 A — manifest 未改，锁文件一致：通过**

```
$ cargo build --locked
   Compiling itoa v1.0.18
   Compiling demo v0.1.0 (/private/tmp/eng_research/demo/cargo_demo)
    Finished `dev` profile [unoptimized + debuginfo] target(s) in 7.85s
exit=0
```

**场景 B — 改了 `Cargo.toml`（新加 `ryu = "1"`）但没同步锁文件：退出码 101**

```
$ cargo build --locked
    Updating crates.io index
error: cannot update the lock file /private/tmp/eng_research/demo/cargo_demo/Cargo.lock because --locked was passed to prevent this
help: to generate the lock file without accessing the network, remove the --locked flag and use --offline instead.
exit=101
```

**场景 C — 锁文件被删除（缺失）：同样退出码 101（对应手册 "The lock file is missing." 条目）**

```
$ rm -f Cargo.lock
$ cargo build --locked
    Updating crates.io index
error: cannot create the lock file /private/tmp/eng_research/demo/cargo_demo/Cargo.lock because --locked was passed to prevent this
help: to generate the lock file without accessing the network, remove the --locked flag and use --offline instead.
```

**关键观察**：场景 B 与场景 C 的**错误文本不同**（`cannot update` vs `cannot create`）、**退出码相同（101）**。对 CI 而言二者都只是「非零」；要做「新增了什么」的定位，必须解析 stderr 文本或另跑 `cargo update --dry-run` + diff，见下节。

- https://doc.rust-lang.org/cargo/commands/cargo-build.html

- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/rust-lang/cargo/master/src/bin/cargo/commands/build.rs

#### 对本场景的可迁移改法与代价

**场景痛点对照**：本场景「1 个源文件 → 6 份生成物（其中 1 份是完整性摘要）」，139 个包 ≈ 834 个同步点，历史上「重算摘要」被手工执行 50 次；痛点是「一个『通过』既可能是零违规，也可能是未比历史基线更差」。

Cargo 的解法给出的可迁移点有四条：

1. **把「摘要」从产出物改成输入物。** Cargo.lock 由 Cargo 维护、禁止手工编辑，`--locked` 的存在意义就是把它当**只读输入**校验。本场景的完整性摘要目前是「要重算的产物」（所以有 50 次手工重算），迁移动作是：把摘要当作**先行输入**——生成物构建时读摘要，摘要不匹配直接拒绝，而不是构建完再回头补算摘要。代价：需要一次性的「摘要 → 生成物」回填，把 834 个同步点里现存的不一致先清干净，否则门禁一开就是全红。

2. **门禁只做二元判定，不做程度判定——所以要配一个 diff 出口。** Cargo 的做法是 `--locked` 只回答「是否与锁文件一致」，定位差异要靠 `cargo update --dry-run` / 直接 diff 锁文件。本场景「一个通过可能是零违规，也可能是未比历史基线更差」正是**只做了二元判定的后果**：门禁少了「相对基线的差值」这一维。迁移动作：给门禁加第二条出口——不仅输出 PASS/FAIL，还要输出「本次违规数 vs 历史基线违规数」的差值与新增违规清单。代价：需要落一份可比较的历史基线快照（每包每生成物的摘要账本），首次建立基线时要跑一轮全量采集；后续每次只追加增量。

3. **`--frozen` 式的一键复现封装。** `--frozen` = `--locked` + `--offline`，把「不许改摘要」和「不许联网取新版本」打包成一个不可细调的开关，防的就是「先跑个 --offline 绕过校验」。迁移动作：本场景的重算脚本应提供一个 `--frozen` 等价模式，禁止任何「联网/取最新版本再算摘要」的旁路。代价：会挡住「合法地引入新包」——所以必须另外保留一条**显式的、留痕的**基线升级通道（对应 `cargo update`），并把「基线升级」与「日常构建」在权限和日志上分开。历史上 50 次手工重算，多半就是这两条通道没有分开导致的。

4. **退出码不足以定位，必须解析错误文本或对齐 stderr 契约。** 实测里 `cannot update` 与 `cannot create` 退出码同为 101。若本场景的门禁脚本只读退出码，运维看到的永远是「FAIL」，永远分不清「摘要缺失」还是「摘要过期」——这直接放大「通过含义不明」的痛点。迁移动作：门禁必须把失败原因分类成至少「生成物缺失 / 生成物存在但摘要不匹配 / 摘要本身无法解析」三类，并让日志与退出码一一对应（例如 101/102/103）。代价：要改脚本与下游 CI 的判定逻辑，短期会打破「非零即失败」的既有习惯，需同步更新告警规则。

**一句话代价总结**：这套改法的成本集中在**建基线**（一次性全量采集 + 清历史不一致）和**拆通道**（日常构建 vs 基线升级），收益是把「一个通过」从二值信号升级为带差值的可解释信号。

---

### 2. Go：`go mod tidy -diff` 与 `go mod verify`

#### 机制名
- `go mod tidy -diff`（Go 1.23 新增）：**只算不改**，把需要的变更以 unified diff 打印出来，diff 非空即以非零码退出。
- `go mod verify`：把「依赖是否被改过」的校验独立成一条命令——重算模块缓存里已下载模块的哈希，与**首次下载时记录的哈希**比对。

#### 项目
Go（`cmd/go` / Go Modules）

#### 它怎么做

##### 出处原文 A：`go mod tidy -diff`

一手来源（本任务指定的 https://go.dev/ref/mod 的 Markdown 源）：
https://raw.githubusercontent.com/golang/website/master/_content/ref/mod.md （HTTP 200，221363 字节）
本地缓存：`/tmp/eng_research/raw/a766d1d48eb74ee777a95daa36661a0f.md`

逐字原文（`### `go mod tidy` {#go-mod-tidy}` 一节）：

> Usage:
>
> ```
> go mod tidy [-e] [-v] [-x] [-diff] [-go=version] [-compat=version]
> ```
>
> `go mod tidy` ensures that the `go.mod` file matches the source code in the
> module. It adds any missing module requirements necessary to build the current
> module's packages and dependencies, and it removes requirements on modules that
> don't provide any relevant packages. It also adds any missing entries to
> `go.sum` and removes unnecessary entries.

> The `-diff` flag causes `go mod tidy` not to modify go.mod or go.sum but
> instead print the necessary changes as a unified diff. It exits
> with a non-zero code if the diff is not empty.

##### 出处原文 B：Go 1.23 release notes

一手来源：https://raw.githubusercontent.com/golang/website/master/_content/doc/go1.23.md （HTTP 200，35207 字节）
本地缓存：`/tmp/eng_research/raw/c33385fd698637816d0922b64c6faed0.md`
（go.dev/doc/go1.23 的 Markdown 源在这个路径；`raw.githubusercontent.com/golang/go/master/doc/go1.23.md` 返回 404，官方 release note 内容托管在 golang/website 仓库。）

逐字原文（`### Go command {#go-command}` 一节）：

```markdown
<!-- go.dev/issue/27005, CL 585401 -->
The new `go` `mod` `tidy` `-diff` flag causes the command not to modify
the files but instead print the necessary changes as a unified diff.
It exits with a non-zero code if updates are needed.
```

注意措辞差异：release notes 说 `It exits with a non-zero code if updates are needed.`，ref/mod 手册说 `It exits with a non-zero code if the diff is not empty.`——同一机制、两种表述，两者都已逐字抄录。

同一份 release notes 的相邻段落还给出了机制设计的同族证据（同一批次引入的「只算不改」模式）：

```markdown
<!-- go.dev/issue/34208, CL 563137, CL 586095 -->
The new `go` `env` `-changed` flag causes the command to print only
those settings whose effective value differs from the default value
that would be obtained in an empty environment with no prior uses of the `-w` flag.
```

##### 出处原文 C：`go mod verify`

一手来源：https://raw.githubusercontent.com/golang/website/master/_content/ref/mod.md 中 `### `go mod verify` {#go-mod-verify}` 一节（对应网页锚点 https://go.dev/ref/mod#go-mod-verify ，HTML 锚点在源文件偏移 110266 处）

逐字原文：

> `go mod verify` checks that dependencies of the [main module](#glos-main-module)
> stored in the [module cache](#glos-module-cache) have not been modified since
> they were downloaded. To perform this check, `go mod verify` hashes each
> downloaded module [`.zip` file](#zip-files) and extracted directory, then
> compares those hashes with a hash recorded when the module was first
> downloaded. `go mod verify` checks each module in the [build
> list](#glos-build-list) (which may be printed with [`go list -m
> all`](#go-list-m)).
>
> If all the modules are unmodified, `go mod verify` prints "all modules
> verified". Otherwise, it reports which modules have been changed and exits with
> a non-zero status.

以及**明确划出两条门禁的边界**（这段是本场景最值得抄的一段）：

> Note that all module-aware commands verify that hashes in the main module's
> `go.sum` file match hashes recorded for modules downloaded into the module
> cache. If a hash is missing from `go.sum` (for example, because the module is
> being used for the first time), the `go` command verifies its hash using the
> [checksum database](#checksum-database) (unless the module path is matched by
> `GOPRIVATE` or `GONOSUMDB`). See [Authenticating modules](#authenticating) for
> details.
>
> In contrast, `go mod verify` checks that module `.zip` files and their extracted
> directories have hashes that match hashes recorded in the module cache when they
> were first downloaded. This is useful for detecting changes to files in the
> module cache *after* a module has been downloaded and verified. `go mod verify`
> does not download content for modules not in the cache, and it does not use
> `go.sum` files to verify module content. However, `go mod verify` may download
> `go.mod` files in order to perform [minimal version
> selection](#minimal-version-selection).

即：**常规命令校验 `go.sum`（声明层），`go mod verify` 校验模块缓存（落地层），两者互不复用。** 官方用 "In contrast" 显式点明。

##### 出处原文 D：`go.sum` 的角色（摘要字段本身）

同一份 ref/mod.md，逐字原文：

> The `go.sum` file contains cryptographic hashes of the
> module's direct and indirect dependencies. When the `go` command downloads a
> module `.mod` or `.zip` file into the [module cache](#module-cache), it computes
> a hash and checks that the hash matches the corresponding hash in the main
> module's `go.sum` file.

> - The hash column consists of an algorithm name (like `h1`) and a base64-encoded
>   cryptographic hash, separated by a colon (`:`). Currently, SHA-256 (`h1`) is
>   the only supported hash algorithm. If a vulnerability in SHA-256 is discovered
>   in the future, support will be added for another algorithm (named `h2` and
>   so on).

##### 源码路径（`go` 命令的 help 文本权威来源）

https://raw.githubusercontent.com/golang/go/master/src/cmd/go/alldocs.go （HTTP 200，160950 字节，缓存 `19e6e91f8656b4936b02b8f5641f92de.md`）

逐字原文（tidy 段）：

```go
// # Add missing and remove unused modules
//
// Usage:
//
//	go mod tidy [-e] [-v] [-x] [-diff] [-go=version] [-compat=version]
//
// Tidy makes sure go.mod matches the source code in the module.
// It adds any missing modules necessary to build the current module's
// packages and dependencies, and it removes unused modules that
// don't provide any relevant packages. It also adds any missing entries
// to go.sum and removes any unnecessary ones.
...
// The -diff flag causes tidy not to modify go.mod or go.sum but
// instead print the necessary changes as a unified diff. It exits
// with a non-zero code if the diff is not empty.
```

同文件里 `go fix -diff` 与 `go vet -diff` 也是同一模式，可作为「只算不改」是本仓库通用约定的旁证：

```go
//	  -diff
//		instead of applying each fix, print the patch as a unified diff;
//		exit with a non-zero status if the diff is not empty
```

##### 本机实跑复现（go1.26.2，真实终端输出）

目录：`/tmp/eng_research/demo/godemo`

**场景 A — `go.mod` 里有未被源码引用的 require，`go mod tidy -diff`：退出码 1，且不改文件**

```
$ go mod tidy -diff
diff current/go.mod tidy/go.mod
--- current/go.mod
+++ tidy/go.mod
@@ -1,5 +1,3 @@
 module example.com/demo

 go 1.22
-
-require rsc.io/quote v1.5.2

exit=1
```

**场景 B — 真正执行 `go mod tidy` 后再跑 `-diff`：输出为空、退出码 0**

```
$ go mod tidy
exit=0
$ go mod tidy -diff
exit=0
```

**场景 C — `go mod verify`：**

```
$ go mod verify
all modules verified
exit=0
```

**关键观察**：`-diff` 的输出是**可直接读的 unified diff**，不是一句「不一致」。这是它相对 `--locked` 的核心区别——`--locked` 只给「不能改」，`-diff` 直接给「要改成什么」。本机 `go help mod tidy` 的 help 文本与 alldocs.go 源码逐字一致，可交叉验证。
