- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/golang/go/master/src/cmd/go/alldocs.go （HTTP 200）

#### 对本场景的可迁移改法与代价

Go 这一套对本场景的增量价值，主要在**「只算不改 + 给 diff」**和**「双层校验划清边界」**两点：

1. **把「重算摘要」换成「预演重算 + 打印差异」。** `go mod tidy -diff` 的机制是：不写文件，只把「需要发生的变更」以 unified diff 输出，非空即退出码 1。本场景历史上 50 次「重算摘要」是**真写**（改了状态才需要止血），迁移动作是提供 `--diff` 等价的预演模式：跑一遍重算**不落盘**，只输出「包 X 的摘要将从 A 变成 B」的清单，非空即失败。这与 Cargo `--locked` 的关键差别是：`--locked` 只能告诉你「不一致」，`-diff` 能告诉你「不一致在哪、变成什么」。对本场景这种 834 个同步点规模，diff 出口是必需品而非增强项。代价：预演模式要与真实重算共享同一套哈希实现，否则 diff 会和实跑结果漂移——这需要用「同一次运行里先 diff 再写」的单一入口来保证，不能拆成两个脚本。

2. **双层校验：声明层 vs 落地层，别混。** Go 把校验拆成 `go.sum`（清单声明，所有模块感知命令都查）和 `go mod verify`（模块缓存里已下载内容的哈希，独立命令，**不复用 go.sum**），官方用 "In contrast" 显式划界。本场景的 6 份生成物里，完整性摘要只是**声明层**；如果生成物本体（落地层）没有被独立校验，那么「摘要对」不等于「生成物对」——摘要可以是对的而文件已被别处改坏。迁移动作：把 6 份生成物拆成「声明层摘要校验」与「落地层内容校验」两组，各自独立报告，不合并成一个 PASS。代价：校验点数量会上升（6 份生成物 × 2 层 ≈ 12 个校验位），而且落地层校验需要一次全量哈希采集作为「首次下载基准」——对 139 个包意味着 139 次额外哈希计算，需要在构建流水线里预算这部分时间。

3. **`go mod verify` 的措辞本身就是给运维的契约。** 成功时输出固定字符串 `all modules verified`，失败时**报告是哪些模块被改了**并退出非零。本场景要根治「一个通过既可能是零违规，也可能是未比历史基线更差」，就要抄这条：成功路径打印一个**可被脚本抓取的确定字符串**（而不是沉默退出 0），失败路径**枚举具体是哪些生成物**（而不是一句 FAIL）。这样「通过」的含义被固定成「逐项比对全部通过」，而不是「进程没崩」。代价：改造门禁脚本的输出契约，并同步更新日志采集/告警规则。

4. **`-diff` 与历史基线的关系需要显式设计，Go 没给。** 必须诚实指出：`go mod tidy -diff` 只比对**当前工作区状态**，它**没有**「历史基线」概念——它回答「当前 go.mod 是否与当前源码一致」，不回答「是否比上次更差」。所以直接照搬 `-diff` **不能**单独解决本场景的痛点，它只解决「一次通过意味着什么」的一半（把「不一致」变成「不一致的具体内容」）。另一半（「是否比历史基线更差」）需要在 `-diff` 之外再落一份基线快照做三方比对：基线 ↔ 期望 ↔ 实际。代价：多一份需要维护和归档的基线数据，以及基线升级必须走留痕通道。

---

### 3. npm：`npm ci` 与 `package-lock.json` 的 `integrity`

#### 机制名
- `npm ci`：只按锁文件**冻结安装**，锁文件与 `package.json` 不匹配即报错退出，且**绝不写** `package.json` / 锁文件。
- `package-lock.json` 的 `integrity` 字段：记录每个包解包产物的 Standard Subresource Integrity 摘要字符串；安装时按此校验下载到的 tarball。

#### 项目
npm（Node.js 官方包管理器）

#### 它怎么做

##### 出处原文 A：`npm ci`

一手来源（官方文档站点 v10）：https://docs.npmjs.com/cli/v10/commands/npm-ci
HTML 缓存：`/tmp/eng_research/raw/html_npm-ci.html`（560198 字节）

逐字原文（Description 一节，「The main differences between using `npm install` and `npm ci` are:」列表）：

> The main differences between using `npm install` and `npm ci` are:
>
> - The project **must** have an existing `package-lock.json`.
> - If dependencies in the package lock do not match those in `package.json`,
>   `npm ci` will exit with an error, instead of updating the package lock.
> - `npm ci` can only install entire projects at a time: individual dependencies cannot be added with this command.
> - If a `node_modules` is already present, it will be automatically removed before `npm ci` begins its install.
> - It will never write to `package.json` or `package-lock.json`:
>   installs are essentially frozen.

第二手同源（官方文档仓库 Markdown 源，可与站点交叉验证，且措辞更严）：
https://raw.githubusercontent.com/npm/cli/latest/docs/lib/content/commands/npm-ci.md （HTTP 200，2086 字节，缓存 `npm_ci_latest.md`）

逐字原文：

> * The project **must** have an existing `package-lock.json`.
> * If dependencies in the package lock do not match those in `package.json`,
>   `npm ci` will exit with an error, instead of updating the package lock.
> * `npm ci` can only install entire projects at a time: individual dependencies cannot be added with this command.
> * If a `node_modules` is already present, it will be automatically removed before `npm ci` begins its install.
> * It will never write to `package.json` or `package-lock.json`:
>   installs are essentially frozen.

注：站点 v10 页面写的是 `package-lock.json`，仓库 Markdown 源写的是 `package-lock.json`；**v10 站点渲染版在该条中额外出现 `npm-shrinkwrap.json`**（HTML 抓取结果为 "The project must have an existing `package-lock.json` or `npm-shrinkwrap.json`."），而仓库最新源已不含它。这是版本漂移，两版都已逐字记录，引用时须指明版本。

##### 出处原文 B：`integrity` 字段

一手来源（官方文档仓库）：https://raw.githubusercontent.com/npm/cli/latest/docs/lib/content/configuring-npm/package-lock-json.md （HTTP 200，10523 字节，缓存 `npm_package-lock-json.md`）

逐字原文（`#### `packages`` → Package descriptors 字段列表）：

> * `integrity`: A `sha512` or `sha1` [Standard Subresource Integrity](https://w3c.github.io/webappsec/specs/subresourceintegrity/) string for the artifact that was unpacked in this location.

同源交叉验证（站点 v11）：https://docs.npmjs.com/cli/v11/configuring-npm/package-lock-json （HTTP 200，缓存 `v11-lock.html`）逐字相同：

> integrity: A sha512 or sha1 Standard Subresource Integrity string for the artifact that was unpacked in this location.

**注意**：任务书预期的 "This is a SHA-512 hash of the tarball" **不在官方文档中**。官方原文是上面这句 "A `sha512` or `sha1` Standard Subresource Integrity string for the artifact that was unpacked in this location."。两处措辞差异已核实：
- 官方说的是 `sha512` **或** `sha1`（不是只有 SHA-512）；
- 官方说的是 "for the artifact that was unpacked in this location"（**解包后的产物**），不是 "of the tarball"。

##### 出处原文 C：`package-lock.json` 的角色与「不做手工编辑」约束

同一份 `package-lock-json.md`，逐字原文：

> `package-lock.json` is automatically generated for any operations where npm modifies either the `node_modules` tree, or `package.json`.
> It describes the exact tree that was generated, such that subsequent installs are able to generate identical trees, regardless of intermediate dependency updates.
>
> This file is intended to be committed into source repositories, and serves various purposes:
>
> * Describe a single representation of a dependency tree such that teammates, deployments, and continuous integration are guaranteed to install exactly the same dependencies.
>
> * Provide a facility for users to "time-travel" to previous states of `node_modules` without having to commit the directory itself.
>
> * Facilitate greater visibility of tree changes through readable source control diffs.
>
> * Optimize the installation process by allowing npm to skip repeated metadata resolutions for previously-installed packages.

以及 `resolved` 字段（与 `integrity` 配对使用）逐字原文：

> * `resolved`: The place where the package was actually resolved from.
>   In the case of packages fetched from the registry, this will be a url to a tarball.
>   In the case of git dependencies, this will be the full git url with commit sha.
>   In the case of link dependencies, this will be the location of the link target.
>   `registry.npmjs.org` is a magic value meaning "the currently configured registry".

##### 本机实跑复现（node v22.23.0 / npm 10.9.8，真实终端输出）

目录：`/tmp/eng_research/demo/npmdemo`
锁文件里的真实 `integrity` 值（`ms@2.1.3`）：

```
node_modules/ms 2.1.3 sha512-6FlzubTLZG3J2a/NVCAleEhjzq5oxgHyaCU9yYXvcLsvoVaHJq/s5xXI6/XXP6tz7R9xAOtHnSO/tXtF3WRTlA==
```

**场景 A — 锁文件与 package.json 一致：通过**

```
$ npm ci --dry-run
add ms 2.1.3

added 1 package in 140ms
exit=0
```

**场景 B — 改 `package.json`（`ms: 2.1.3` → `2.0.0`）而锁文件不同步：退出码 1**

```
$ npm ci
npm error code EUSAGE
npm error
npm error `npm ci` can only install packages when your package.json and package-lock.json or npm-shrinkwrap.json are in sync. Please update your lock file with `npm install` before continuing.
npm error
npm error Invalid: lock file's ms@2.1.3 does not satisfy ms@2.0.0
npm error
exit=1
```

**关键观察**：错误信息 `Invalid: lock file's ms@2.1.3 does not satisfy ms@2.0.0` **点名了是哪个包、锁里是什么、声明要什么**——不是一句泛泛的「不一致」。

**场景 C — 篡改锁文件里的 `integrity`，用干净缓存真实安装：退出码 1，且 `integrity` 是硬门禁**

```
$ npm ci --cache /tmp/eng_research/demo/npmcache2
npm warn tarball tarball data for ms@https://registry.npmmirror.com/ms/-/ms-2.1.3.tgz (sha512-AAAAdeadbeefAAAA...AA==) seems to be corrupted. Trying again.
npm warn tarball tarball data for ms@https://registry.npmmirror.com/ms/-/ms-2.1.3.tgz (sha512-AAAAdeadbeefAAAA...AA==) seems to be corrupted. Trying again.
npm error code EINTEGRITY
npm error sha512-AAAAdeadbeefAAAA...AA== integrity checksum failed when using sha512: wanted sha512-AAAAdeadbeefAAAA...AA== but got sha512-6FlzubTLZG3J2a/NVCAleEhjzq5oxgHyaCU9yYXvcLsvoVaHJq/s5xXI6/XXP6tz7R9xAOtHnSO/tXtF3WRTlA==. (2967 bytes)
npm error A complete log of this run can be found in: ...
exit=1
```

**关键观察**：错误里同时给出 `wanted`（锁文件里声明的摘要）与 `got`（实际下载物的摘要）——**这是本任务三套机制里唯一直接把「期望值」和「实际值」同框打印的**。且它在硬失败前会**重试两次**（`seems to be corrupted. Trying again.` ×2），说明设计上把网络抖动与真篡改区分开了。

#### URL / 文件路径
- https://docs.npmjs.com/cli/v10/commands/npm-ci
- https://docs.npmjs.com/cli/v11/configuring-npm/package-lock-json
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/npm/cli/latest/docs/lib/content/commands/npm-ci.md （HTTP 200）
- https://raw.githubusercontent.com/npm/cli/latest/docs/lib/content/configuring-npm/package-lock-json.md （HTTP 200）

#### 对本场景的可迁移改法与代价

npm 这一套对本场景最有价值的是**错误信息的形状**和**「锁文件不可写」的硬约束**：

1. **门禁失败时必须同框打印「期望值 vs 实际值」。** 实测里 npm 的 `EINTEGRITY` 输出形如 `wanted sha512-XXX but got sha512-YYY. (2967 bytes)`，场景 B 的版本不匹配输出 `Invalid: lock file's ms@2.1.3 does not satisfy ms@2.0.0`。本场景「一个通过既可能是零违规，也可能是未比历史基线更差」的根因之一就是**失败与通过都不携带可比对的数值**。迁移动作：门禁的每条判定必须至少输出三元组 `(对象标识, 期望摘要, 实际摘要)`，成功路径也要输出被校验对象的计数（npm 输出 `added 1 package` 就是这种计数的等价物）。代价：383 个包 × 834 个同步点的输出量很大，必须落结构化日志（JSONL）而不是刷屏 stderr，否则日志本身成为新噪音。

2. **`npm ci` 的「绝不写入」是一条要照抄的硬约束。** 官方原文 `It will never write to package.json or package-lock.json: installs are essentially frozen.` ——`npm ci` 与 `npm install` 的差别不在严格程度，而在**是否拥有写权限**。本场景历史上 50 次手工重算摘要，本质是构建流程**拥有写摘要的权限**。迁移动作：把「构建（只读摘要）」与「重算摘要（写摘要）」做成两条命令、两套权限，构建路径上物理上无法写摘要。代价：重算必须走独立入口，人工触发时多一步；且要防止有人图省事在构建脚本里内联写操作，需要 CI 侧加「工作区摘要文件在构建前后哈希不变」的兜底校验。

3. **把「不一致」拆成可分类的失败码。** npm 在这一层给出的是 `EUSAGE`（锁与清单不同步）和 `EINTEGRITY`（摘要不符）两个不同的错误码。本场景的门禁目前若只有单一 FAIL，运维无法区分「生成物缺失」「生成物过期」「生成物被篡改」——这三类的处置动作完全不同（补生成 vs 重算 vs 查事故）。迁移动作：定义失败码体系并写进门禁契约。代价：需要改动下游 CI 判定与告警路由，且历史日志需要一次回溯标注才能与新体系对齐。

4. **必须诚实指出 npm 这一层**没有**解决基线问题。** `npm ci` 只比对「锁文件 ↔ 当前清单」的一致性，**没有**历史基线维度——它同样回答不了「是否比上次更差」。而且 `integrity` 只覆盖「tarball 内容有没有变」，**不覆盖**「锁文件自身有没有被合法地改写」（改锁文件 + 改 integrity 一起提交，`npm ci` 会愉快通过）。这正是本场景「一个通过既可能是零违规，也可能是未比历史基线更差」的技术翻版。迁移动作：基线比对必须在门禁之外独立成环（基线快照归档 + 差异报告 + 基线升级留痕），不能指望锁文件机制自带。代价：多一套基线数据资产要维护，且基线升级的审批链路要单独设计——否则「升级基线」会变成新的绕过口。

---

### 4. 三生态横向对照（供本场景设计门禁时取舍）

| 维度 | Cargo `--locked` | Go `go mod tidy -diff` | npm `npm ci` + `integrity` |
|---|---|---|---|
| 摘要载体 | `Cargo.lock` | `go.sum`（`h1:` = SHA-256） | `package-lock.json` 的 `integrity`（sha512 或 sha1） |
| 不一致时行为 | 拒绝运行，退出码 101 | 打印 unified diff，退出码 1，**不写文件** | 退出码 1，`EUSAGE` / `EINTEGRITY` |
| 是否给差异内容 | 否（只说 cannot update/create） | **是**（完整 unified diff） | 部分（版本不匹配给 `lock file's X does not satisfy Y`；摘要不符给 `wanted/got`） |
| 是否禁止改写 | **是**（`will remain unchanged`） | **是**（`not to modify go.mod or go.sum`） | **是**（`It will never write to ... installs are essentially frozen`） |
| 是否有历史基线维度 | 无 | 无 | 无 |
| 独立性 | 全局 flag，所有子命令共用 | 仅 tidy 子命令 | 仅 ci 子命令 |

**三条共性结论（三条都有一手原文支撑）**：
1. 三者的门禁都是**二元**的：只回答「当前状态是否自洽」，**都不回答「是否比历史基线更差」**。本场景的痛点不是选错了生态，而是**任何单一锁文件机制都覆盖不了基线维度**。
2. 三者都明确**禁止**门禁流程写入摘要/锁文件（Cargo `will remain unchanged`、Go `not to modify`、npm `never write`）。「只读」是三者共同的硬约束。
3. 只有 Go 的原生输出直接给出**可读差异**（unified diff），npm 给出**期望/实际摘要对**，Cargo 只给**否证**。做本场景门禁时，差异出口必须自建——照抄 Cargo 会得到最难定位的形态。

---

### 5. 未证实 / 已核实的偏差记录

以下三处与任务书预期不符，均已按实际取证结果记录，**未按预期口径书写**：

1. **Cargo `--locked` 的措辞**：任务书预期 "Assert that Cargo.lock is up to date / does not need to be updated"。实际官方手册为 "Asserts that the exact same dependencies and versions are used as when the existing Cargo.lock file was originally generated."；`--help` 文本为 "Assert that `Cargo.lock` will remain unchanged"。未在官方文档中找到任务书预期的那句原文。
2. **`cargo build` 手册中 `-locked` 的 help 文本**：`src/bin/cargo/commands/build.rs` 中**不含** `--locked` 字样，该 flag 定义在 `src/bin/cargo/cli.rs` 第 680 行。任务书假设的路径部分需修正。另注：`rust-lang/cargo` 默认分支为 `master`（`main` 返回 404）。
3. **npm `integrity` 的措辞**：任务书预期 "This is a SHA-512 hash of the tarball"。实际官方文档为 "A `sha512` or `sha1` Standard Subresource Integrity string for the artifact that was unpacked in this location."——官方明确允许 `sha1`，且描述对象是 **unpacked artifact** 而非 tarball。
4. **`go mod tidy -diff` 是否解决基线问题**：**未证实**，且证据指向「不能」。ref/mod 与 release notes 均只描述「当前文件是否需变更」，无任何历史基线/趋势/回归语义。
5. **`go.dev/ref/mod` 与 `go.dev/doc/go1.23` 的 HTML 页面**：本次**未直抓 HTML**，改用官方 Markdown 源（golang/website 仓库，HTTP 200）作为一手来源。`raw.githubusercontent.com/golang/go/master/doc/go1.23.md` 返回 404（release notes 内容不在 golang/go 仓库），已改用 golang/website 路径。

## B. Bazel / Terraform：「锁文件即摘要门禁」取证

> 取证时间：2026-09-17。所有英文片段均为逐字原文（含源码中固有的断行/拼写），未做改写。
> 抓取方式：`raw.githubusercontent.com`（`master` 与 tag `8.0.0` 双份比对，sha256 一致）+ 官方文档站直取 HTML + jsDelivr 镜像交叉验证源码。**未使用 api.github.com**。

### B1. Bazel —— `--lockfile_mode=error` + `MODULE.bazel.lock`

- **机制名**：模块解析锁文件门禁（Bazel Lockfile，bzlmod 体系）
- **项目**：bazelbuild/bazel

#### B1.1 它怎么做

##### (1) `--lockfile_mode` 的四个取值（官方 flag 文档原文）

出处：<https://bazel.build/reference/command-line-reference>（`--lockfile_mode off, update, refresh or error default: "update"`）

> `Specifies how and whether or not to use the lockfile. Valid values are update to use the lockfile and update it if there are changes, refresh to additionally refresh mutable information (yanked versions and previously missing modules) from remote registries from time to time, error to use the lockfile but throw an error if needed information is not up-to-date (superfluous entries that the current resolution does not consult are ignored, not reported), or off to neither read from or write to the lockfile.`

出处（源码，flag help 串）：<https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bazelbuild/bazel/master/src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/bazel/repository/RepositoryOptions.java> 第 419–432 行

```java
  @Option(
      name = "lockfile_mode",
      converter = LockfileMode.Converter.class,
      defaultValue = "update",
      documentationCategory = OptionDocumentationCategory.BZLMOD,
      effectTags = {OptionEffectTag.LOADING_AND_ANALYSIS},
      help =
          "Specifies how and whether or not to use the lockfile. Valid values are `update` to"
              + " use the lockfile and update it if there are changes, `refresh` to additionally"
              + " refresh mutable information (yanked versions and previously missing modules)"
              + " from remote registries from time to time, `error` to use the lockfile but throw"
              + " an error if needed information is not up-to-date (superfluous entries that the"
              + " current resolution does not consult are ignored, not reported), or `off` to"
              + " neither read from or write to the lockfile.")
  public abstract LockfileMode getLockfileMode();
```

**注意**：默认值 `defaultValue = "update"` —— 门禁不是默认开的，必须显式传 `--lockfile_mode=error`。

同一文件第 502–507 行的枚举定义（**逐字，含被上游截断的注释**，已核对 `master` 与 `8.0.0` 两版本 + jsDelivr 镜像，三份完全一致，不是我抓取截断）：

```java
  /** An enum for specifying how to use the lockfile. */
  public enum LockfileMode {
    OFF, // Don't use the lockfile at all.
    UPDATE, // Update the lockfile wh
    REFRESH,
    ERROR; // Throw an error when it mismatc
```

> 上游源码里的 `UPDATE` / `ERROR` 行注释本身就是截断的（`Update the lockfile wh`、`Throw an error when it mismatc`）。取证时**不要**引用这两行注释，权威定义只有上面的 flag help 串。

##### (2) 官方「Lockfile Usage」一节对四个取值的正式定义（文档原文）

出处：<https://bazel.build/external/lockfile>（章节 `Lockfile Usage`）

> `update` (Default): Use the information that is present in the lockfile to skip downloads of known registry files and to avoid re-evaluating extensions whose results are still up-to-date. If information is missing, it will be added to the lockfile. In this mode, Bazel also avoids refreshing mutable information, such as yanked versions, for dependencies that haven't changed.

> `refresh`: Like `update`, but mutable information is always refreshed when switching to this mode and roughly every hour while in this mode.

> **`error`: Like `update`, but if any information consulted during resolution is missing or out-of-date, Bazel will fail with an error. This mode never changes the lockfile or performs network requests during resolution.** Module extensions that marked themselves as reproducible may still perform network requests, but are expected to always produce the same result. **Note that superfluous information is ignored, not reported, so a lockfile that passes in this mode may nevertheless be rewritten (pruned) by `update`.**

> `off`: The lockfile is neither checked nor updated.

（加粗为我标注的重点，非原文格式。）

##### (3) `error` 模式下真正抛错的那句英文（源码逐字）

出处：<https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bazelbuild/bazel/master/src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/bazel/bzlmod/SingleExtensionEvalFunction.java> 第 504–512 行

```java
  private static SingleExtensionEvalFunctionException createOutdatedLockfileException(
      String reason) {
    return new SingleExtensionEvalFunctionException(
        ExternalDepsException.withMessage(
            Code.BAD_LOCKFILE,
            "MODULE.bazel.lock is no longer up-to-date because %s. Please run `bazel mod deps"
                + " --lockfile_mode=update` to update your lockfile.",
            reason));
  }
```

同文件第 274–282 行的触发条件（扩展结果/事实与工作区锁文件不一致即 fail）：

```java
    if (lockfileMode.equals(LockfileMode.ERROR) && !newFacts.equals(workspaceLockfileFacts)) {
      String reason =
          "the extension '%s' has changed its facts: %s != %s"
              .formatted(
                  extensionId,
                  Starlark.repr(newFacts.value(), starlarkSemantics),
                  Starlark.repr(workspaceLockfileFacts.value(), starlarkSemantics));
      throw createOutdatedLockfileException(reason);
    }
```

再加两道门（同文件 258–267 行、403 行）：扩展不存在于锁文件中、或扩展未声明 `reproducible`，在 `error` 模式下同样直接 fail。

##### (4) `MODULE.bazel.lock` 记录什么（文档原文）

出处：<https://bazel.build/external/lockfile>（章节 `Lockfile Generation` / `Lockfile Contents`）

> The lockfile is generated under the workspace root with the name `MODULE.bazel.lock`. It is created or updated during the build process, specifically after module resolution and extension evaluation. Importantly, it only includes dependencies that are included in the current invocation of the build.

> The lockfile contains all the necessary information to determine whether the project state has changed. It also includes the result of building the project in the current state. The lockfile consists of two main parts:
> Hashes of all remote files that are inputs to module resolution.
> For each module extension, the lockfile includes inputs that affect it, represented by `bzlTransitiveDigest`, `usagesDigest` and other fields, as well as the output of running that extension, referred to as `generatedRepoSpecs`

锁文件结构（文档给出的示例 JSON，节选原字段名）：

```json
{
  "lockFileVersion": 10,
  "registryFileHashes": {
    "https://bcr.bazel.build/bazel_registry.json": "8a28e4af...5d5b3497",
    "https://registry.mycorp.com/modules/foo/1.0/MODULE.bazel": "not found",
  },
  "selectedYankedVersions": { "foo@2.0": "Yanked for demo purposes" },
  "moduleExtensions": {
    "//:extension.bzl%lockfile_ext": {
      "general": {
        "bzlTransitiveDigest": "oWDzxG/aLnyY6Ubrfy....+Jp6maQvEPxn0pBM=",
        "usagesDigest": "aLmqbvowmHkkBPve05yyDNGN7oh7QE9kBADr3QIZTZs=",
        "generatedRepoSpecs": { "hello": { "bzlFile": "@@//:extension.bzl" } }
```

即：**锁文件 = 输入的哈希（`registryFileHashes` / `bzlTransitiveDigest` / `usagesDigest`）+ 输出的确定化编码（`generatedRepoSpecs`）**。这正是「摘要」的定义——把不确定的解析过程（网络、注册表内容变动）折叠成可比对的字节。

> `The `bzlTransitiveDigest` is the digest of the extension implementation and the `.bzl` files transitively loaded by it. The `usagesDigest` is the digest of the usages of the extension in the dependency graph, which includes all tags.`

##### (5) 隐藏锁文件：Bazel 自己承认「提交的那份」不足以承担门禁

出处：<https://bazel.build/external/lockfile>（章节 `Hidden lockfile`）

> Bazel also maintains another lockfile at `"$(bazel info output_base)"/MODULE.bazel.lock`. **The format and contents of this lockfile are explicitly unspecified. It is only used as a performance optimization.**

源码 corroboration：<https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bazelbuild/bazel/master/src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/bazel/bzlmod/BazelLockFileModule.java>（第 53–54 行、以及第 108 行起的注释）

```java
  private static final ImmutableSet<LockfileMode> ENABLED_IN_MODES =
      Sets.immutableEnumSet(LockfileMode.UPDATE, LockfileMode.REFRESH);
```

```java
    // The hidden lockfile's facts serve as the record of the facts produced by the most recent
    // actual evaluation of each extension, which SingleExtensionEvalFunction compares against the
    // workspace lockfile's facts to detect manual edits.
```

**这一句是关键**：Bazel 用「上一次真实求值的事实记录」（hidden lockfile）来检测对手工编辑过的提交锁文件的检测。**提交到仓库的锁文件本身无法自证是否被人手改过**——所以 Bazel 另存一份基线。

##### (6) 合并冲突：哪些能手工解、哪些绝对不能

出处：<https://bazel.build/external/lockfile>（章节 `Merge Conflicts`）

> Simple merge conflicts in the `registryFileHashes` and `selectedYankedVersions` fields can be safely resolved by keeping all the entries from both sides of the conflict. **Other types of merge conflicts should not be resolved manually.** Instead:
> Restore the previous state of the lockfile via `git reset MODULE.bazel.lock && git checkout MODULE.bazel.lock`.
> Resolve any conflicts in the `MODULE.bazel` file.
> Run `bazel mod deps` to update the lockfile.

且官方提供 jq 合并驱动（`MODULE.bazel.lock merge=bazel-lockfile-merge`），配合 `scripts/bazel-lockfile-merge.jq`。

##### (7) 锁文件与 Bazel 版本强绑定（门禁的另一条约束）

出处：<https://bazel.build/external/lockfile>（章节 `Best Practices`）

> Use `bazelisk` to run Bazel, and include a `.bazelversion` file in version control that specifies the Bazel version corresponding to the lockfile. **Because Bazel itself is a dependency of your build, the lockfile is specific to the Bazel version, and will change even between backwards compatible Bazel releases.**

源码 corroboration：`BazelLockFileValue.LOCK_FILE_VERSION`（master 为 `28`），版本号不匹配时在 `error` 模式下直接 fail —— 出处 <https://raw.githubusercontent.com/bazelbuild/bazel/master/src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/bazel/bzlmod/BazelLockFileFunction.java> 第 120–140 行：

```java
        if (!isValidLockfile(lockFileValue)) {
          if (lockfileMode == LockfileMode.ERROR) {
            throw new BazelLockfileFunctionException(
                ExternalDepsException.withMessage(
                    Code.BAD_LOCKFILE,
                    "The version of MODULE.bazel.lock is not supported by this version of Bazel."
                        + " Please run `bazel mod deps --lockfile_mode=update` to update your"
                        + " lockfile."),
                Transience.PERSISTENT);
```

#### B1.2 出处清单

| 内容 | URL / 文件路径 |
|---|---|
| 四取值定义（文档） | <https://bazel.build/external/lockfile> |
| flag help + 枚举 + 默认值（源码） | `src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/bazel/repository/RepositoryOptions.java`（master；8.0.0 同） |
| flag 文档站渲染 | <https://bazel.build/reference/command-line-reference> |
| `error` 抛错文案 | `src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/bazel/bzlmod/SingleExtensionEvalFunction.java` L504-512 |
| `LOCK_FILE_VERSION` 不匹配 fail | `src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/bazel/bzlmod/BazelLockFileFunction.java` L123/L137 |
| hidden lockfile 用途 | `src/main/java/com/google/devtools/build/lib/bazel/bzlmod/BazelLockFileModule.java` L105-110；`BazelLockFileValue.java` L26-33 |
| 合并冲突处理规则 | <https://bazel.build/external/lockfile#merge-conflicts> |

**未证实**：`bazel.build/versions/8.0.0/external/lockfile` 返回 **404**（版本化路径未提供该页），因此没有「版本化文档 URL」可引用，只能引用无版本路径 + tag `8.0.0` 源码。`LockfileMode.java` 这个独立文件在 master **不存在**（404），枚举实际定义在 `RepositoryOptions.java` 内。

### B2. Terraform —— `.terraform.lock.hcl` 的 `h1:` 摘要 + `terraform providers lock`

- **机制名**：Provider 依赖锁文件（Dependency Lock File）与校验和验证
- **项目**：hashicorp/terraform

#### B2.1 它怎么做

##### (1) `h1:` 与 `zh:` 的定义（官方文档逐字原文）

出处：<https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/language/files/dependency-lock>（章节 `New provider package checksums`）
源文件（mdx 原文）：`content/terraform/v1.16.x/docs/language/files/dependency-lock.mdx` @ <https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hashicorp/web-unified-docs/main/content/terraform/v1.16.x/docs/language/files/dependency-lock.mdx>

> The addition of a new checksum into the `hashes` value represents Terraform gradually transitioning between different _hashing schemes_. The `h1:` and `zh:` prefixes on these values represent different hashing schemes, each of which represents calculating a checksum using a different algorithm.

> The two hashing schemes currently supported are:
>
> - `zh:`: a mnemonic for "zip hash", this is a legacy hash format which is part of the Terraform provider registry protocol and is therefore used for providers that you install directly from an origin registry.
>
>   This hashing scheme captures a SHA256 hash of each of the official `.zip` packages indexed in the origin registry. This is an effective scheme for verifying the official release packages when installed from a registry, but it's not suitable for verifying packages that come from other provider installation methods, such as filesystem mirrors using the unpacked directory layout.
>
> - `h1:`: a mnemonic for "hash scheme 1", which is the current preferred hashing scheme.
>
>   **Hash scheme 1 is also a SHA256 hash, but is one computed from the _contents_ of the provider distribution package, rather than of the `.zip` archive it's contained within.** This scheme therefore has the advantage that it can be calculated for an official `.zip` file, an unpacked directory with the same contents, or a recompressed `.zip` file which contains the same files but potentially different metadata or compression schemes.

**「摘要即内容」这一点是可直接迁移的**：`h1:` 不哈希容器（zip），只哈希容器内的内容，所以重新打包、换压缩算法不会让门禁假红。相对地，`zh:` 哈希 zip 字节，换打包元数据就失配 —— 属于假阳性来源。

##### (2) 锁文件里 `hashes` 的语义（官方文档逐字原文）

同页，章节 `Understanding Lock File Changes`：

> - `version`: the exact version that Terraform selected based on the version constraints in the configuration.
> - `constraints`: all of the version constraints that Terraform considered when making this selection. (Terraform doesn't actually use this information to make installation decisions, but includes it to help explain to human readers how the previous decision was made.)
> - `hashes`: a number of checksums that are all considered to be valid for packages implementing the selected version of this provider on different platforms.

锁文件结构（文档给出的 diff 示例，逐字）：

```diff
+provider "registry.terraform.io/hashicorp/azurerm" {
+  version     = "2.30.0"
+  constraints = "~> 2.12"
+  hashes = [
+    "h1:FJwsuowaG5CIdZ0WQyFZH9r6kIJeRKts9+GcRsTz1+Y=",
+    "h1:c/ntsXrDYM1mUir2KufijYebPcwKqS9CRGd3duDSGfY=",
+    "h1:yre4Ph76g9H84MbuhZ2z5MuldjSA4FsrX6538O7PCcY=",
+    "zh:04f0a50bb2ba92f3bea6f0a9e549ace5a4c13ef0cbb6975494cac0ef7d4acb43",
+    "zh:2082e12548ebcdd6fd73580e83f626ed4ed13f8cdfd51205d8696ffe54f30734",
+  ]
+}
```

注意：**一个版本对应多个 `h1:`**（每个平台一个包一个 `h1:`）。门禁的判定是「至少命中一个」，不是「全部命中」。

##### (3) 门禁怎么判、什么时候红（官方文档逐字原文）

同页，章节 `Checksum verification`：

> Terraform will also verify that each package it installs matches at least one of the checksums it previously recorded in the lock file, if any, returning an error if none of the checksums match:

```
Error: Failed to install provider

Error while installing hashicorp/azurerm v2.1.0: the current package for
registry.terraform.io/hashicorp/azurerm 2.1.0 doesn't match any of the
checksums previously recorded in the dependency lock file.
```

> This checksum verification is intended to represent a _[trust on first use](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Trust_on_first_use)_ approach. When you add a new provider for the first time you can verify it in whatever way you choose or any way you are required to by relevant regulations, and then trust that Terraform will raise an error if a future run of `terraform init` encounters a non-matching package for the same provider version.

**TOFU 的含义要写清楚**：第一次写入锁文件时没有任何历史可比，那次「通过」只代表「我信任了当时抓到的包」。门禁的可信度上限 = 首次记录的那一刻。

##### (4) 增量添加 `h1:` 的规则（弱化门禁的一条，必须写进代价）

同页：

> Terraform will add a new hash to an existing provider only if the hash is calculated from a package that _also_ matches one of the existing hashes.

> Due to the limited scope of the `zh:` scheme, Terraform will opportunistically add in the corresponding `h1:` checksums as it learns of them.

> Terraform will add a new hash to an existing provider only if the hash is calculated from a package that _also_ matches one of the existing hashes. ... After confirming the `zh:` checksum match, Terraform then recorded the corresponding `h1:` checksum in order to gradually migrate from the old scheme to the new scheme.

即：**锁文件会在日常 `init` 过程中自己长出新的 `h1:`**。所以「锁文件没变」不等于「没人动过」，反过来「锁文件变了」也不一定是违规 —— 这正是场景里「通过了可能是零违规，也可能是没比基线更差」的同类问题。

##### (5) `terraform providers lock`：显式的「重算摘要」入口

出处：<https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/cli/commands/providers/lock>
源文件：`content/terraform/v1.16.x/docs/cli/commands/providers/lock.mdx`

> The `terraform providers lock` adds new provider selection information to the dependency lock file without initializing the referenced providers.

> With no additional command line arguments, `terraform providers lock` will analyze the configuration in the current working directory to find all of the providers it depends on, and it will fetch the necessary data about those providers from their origin registries and then update the dependency lock file to include a selected version for each provider and all of the package checksums that are covered by the provider developer's cryptographic signature.

**为什么要在 CI 之前锁**（文档原文给的三个理由）：

> * If you are running Terraform in an environment that uses alternative provider installation methods, such as filesystem or network mirrors, normal provider installation will not access the origin registry for a provider and therefore Terraform will not be able to populate all of the possible package checksums for the selected provider versions.
>
>   If you use `terraform lock` to write the official release checksums for a provider into the dependency lock file then future `terraform init` runs will verify the packages available in your selected mirror against the official checksums previously recorded, giving additional certainty that the mirror is serving the provider packages it is claiming to.
>
> * If your team runs Terraform across a number of different platforms (e.g. on both Windows and Linux) and the upstream registry for a provider is unable to provide signed checksums using the latest hashing scheme, subsequent runs of Terraform on other platforms may add additional checksums to the lock file. You can avoid that by pre-populating hashes for all of the platforms you intend to use, using the `terraform providers lock` command.

**`-platform` 参数的确切语义**（逐字）：

> * `-platform=OS_ARCH` - Specify a platform you intend to use to work with this Terraform configuration. Terraform will ensure that the providers are all available for the given platform and will save enough package checksums in the lock file to support _at least_ the specified platforms.
>
>   Use this option multiple times to include checksums for multiple target systems.
>
>   Target platform names consist of an operating system and a CPU architecture. For example, `linux_amd64` selects the Linux operating system running on an AMD64 or x86_64 CPU.

批量重算的官方用法（逐字）：

```
terraform providers lock \
  -platform=windows_amd64 \ # 64-bit Windows
  -platform=darwin_amd64 \  # 64-bit macOS
  -platform=linux_amd64     # 64-bit Linux
```

**官方警告（这句直接对应场景里「谁改的摘要」问题）**：

> ~> **Warning:** The `terraform providers lock` command prints information about what it has fetched and whether each package was signed using a cryptographic signature, but **it cannot automatically verify that the providers are trustworthy** and that they comply with your local system policies or relevant regulations. Review the signing key information in the output to confirm that you trust all of the signers before committing the updated lock file to your version control system.

镜像场景的降级（逐字）：

> Note that only an origin registry can provide official checksums covered by the original developer's cryptographic signature. Lock entries created from filesystem or network mirrors will therefore cover only the exact platforms you requested, and the recorded checksums will be those reported by the mirror, rather than the origin registry's official checksums.

##### (6) 上游协议侧：hash 从哪里来

出处：<https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/internals/provider-registry-protocol>（`content/.../docs/internals/provider-registry-protocol.mdx`）

> * `shasums_url` (required): a URL from which Terraform can retrieve a text document recording expected SHA256 checksums for this package and possibly other packages for the same provider version on other platforms.
> * `shasums_signature_url` (required): a URL from which Terraform can retrieve a binary, detached GPG signature for the document at `shasums_url`, signed ...
> * `shasum` (required): the SHA256 checksum for this provider's zip archive as recorded in the shasums document.

供应商网络镜像协议同样支持带 hash 的响应（<https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/internals/provider-network-mirror-protocol>）：

> * `hashes` (optional): a JSON array of strings containing one or more hash values for the indicated archive. These hashes use Terraform's provider package hashing algorithm. At present, the easiest way to populate these is to construct a mirror's JSON indices using the `terraform providers mirror` command, as described in a later section, which will include the calculated hashes of each provider.
>
>   If the response includes at least one hash, Terraform will select the hash whose algorithm it considers to be strongest and verify that the downloaded package matches that hash. **If the response does not include a `hashes` property then Terraform will install the indicated archive with no verification.**

即：**摘要有上游签名背书（HashiCorp partner key / GPG 签名），不是本地自算自签**。这一点是门禁有效性的根基。

**反面要点（镜像路径可以直接把门禁关掉）**：网络镜像的 `hashes` 字段是 **optional**，缺失时官方原文就是 `Terraform will install the indicated archive with no verification`。所以「走镜像加速」和「有摘要门禁」二者不可兼得——镜像索引若不自带 hash，门禁在这一环静默失效，且**没有任何告警**。这与场景里「『通过』既可能是零违规，也可能是没比基线更差」是同一类静默失败。

##### (7) 唯一被官方文档明说「关掉门禁」的出口

出处：<https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/cli/config/config-file>（章节 `Allowing the Provider Plugin Cache to break the dependency lock file` / dev overrides，`content/.../docs/cli/config/config-file.mdx`）

> Normally Terraform verifies version selections and checksums for providers in order to help ensure that all operations are made with the intended version of a provider, and that authors can gradually upgrade to newer provider versions in a controlled manner.

> These version and checksum rules are inconvenient when developing a provider though, because we often want to try a test configuration against a development build of a provider that doesn't even have an associated version number yet, and doesn't have an official set of checksums listed in a provider registry.

> As a convenience for provider development, Terraform supports a special additional block `dev_overrides` in `provider_installation` blocks. The contents of this block effectively override all of the other configured installation methods, so a block of this type must always appear first in the sequence:

```hcl
provider_installation {

  # Use /home/developer/tmp/terraform-null as an overridden package directory
  # for the hashicorp/null provider. This disables the version and checksum
  # verifications for this provider and forces Terraform to look for the
  # null provider plugin in the given directory.
  dev_overrides {
    "hashicorp/null" = "/home/developer/tmp/terraform-null"
  }
```

**`dev_overrides` 是唯一被文档明说的绕过口，其官方定位是 `As a convenience for provider development`。** 生产门禁里若存在任何等价绕过口（环境变量开关、跳过标志、`-enable-plugin-cache` 式的便利选项），等于门禁失效且往往静默。

同页另有一节标题即 `Allowing the Provider Plugin Cache to break the dependency lock file`（注意动词是 break），官方原文：`For those teams that don't preserve the dependency lock file in their version control ...` —— 插件缓存能污染锁文件内容，官方对此的态度是「不保留锁文件的团队才会这么干」。`terraform providers lock` 的对应开关 `-enable-plugin-cache` 文档也把它标为 risky：`This is not enabled by default since the plugin cache is not an authoritative source. As the `terraform provider lock` command is used to ensure no untrusted provider versions can be used installing the plugins from the cache is considered risky.`

#### B2.2 出处清单

| 内容 | URL / 文件路径 |
|---|---|
| `h1:` / `zh:` 定义、checksum verification、TOFU、锁文件变更类型 | <https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/language/files/dependency-lock>；mdx: `content/terraform/v1.16.x/docs/language/files/dependency-lock.mdx` |
| `terraform providers lock` 用途 / `-platform` / `-fs-mirror` / `-net-mirror` / 警告 | <https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/cli/commands/providers/lock>；mdx: `content/terraform/v1.16.x/docs/cli/commands/providers/lock.mdx` |
| 上游注册表协议 `shasums_url` / `shasum` | <https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/internals/provider-registry-protocol> |
| 网络镜像协议 `hashes` 字段 | <https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/internals/provider-network-mirror-protocol> |
| 唯一绕过口 `dev_overrides` | <https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/cli/config/config-file> |
| 锁文件由 `terraform init` 自动创建/更新 | <https://developer.hashicorp.com/terraform/cli/commands/init>（引用于 dep-lock 页原文） |

**未证实**：`h1:` 的具体算法（HashiCorp 未公开哈希前的目录排序/归一化细节，只有文档所说的 "computed from the _contents_ of the provider distribution package"）。Terraform 源码侧未取证（hashicorp/terraform 主仓库体量过大，按任务约束未 clone；如需可后续用 raw 抓 `internal/getproviders/hash.go` 单文件补证）。

---

### B3. 对本场景的可迁移改法与代价

场景：改 1 个源文件要手工同步 6 份生成物（含 1 份完整性摘要），139 个包 ≈ 834 个同步点，历史上「重算摘要」被手工执行 50 次。痛点是**「一个『通过』既可能是零违规，也可能是未比历史基线更差」**。

下面三条改法按「直接可用 → 需改造 → 只是思路」排序。

#### 改法 1（首选，直接抄 Terraform）：把「重算摘要」变成一条可批量、可 diff 的官方命令，CI 里加 `git diff --exit-code`

Terraform 的做法值得 1:1 照搬：
1. 提供一条专门重算的命令，语义是「拉权威源 → 重算 → 写回锁文件」，而不是让人手改（`terraform providers lock`）。
2. 该命令支持**一次性覆盖全部目标**：`-platform=` 可重复传多次，一条命令覆盖 win/mac/linux 三平台，不用逐个包点。对应本场景：一条命令重算 139 个包的全部摘要，而不是 50 次手工。
3. CI 门禁 = 跑重算命令 → `git diff --exit-code`。**「通过」的定义被收紧成「提交的摘要与机器重算结果字节一致」**，而不是「某个校验脚本没报错」。

这一步直接消掉「通过可能是没比基线更差」的模糊性：门禁不再判断摘要「是否合理」，只判断摘要「是否等于机器算出来的那份」。摘要算错、改漏、手改过，全部表现为 diff 非空。

代价：
- 需要把权威源固定住（Terraform 是 origin registry + 签名；本场景必须指定「摘要从哪个权威源重算」），否则重算结果不可复现。
- 命令要有网络依赖（Terraform `providers lock` 默认打 origin registry）。离线环境需 `-fs-mirror`，但文档明说此时「checksums will be those reported by the mirror」——**门禁可信度降级为「信任镜像」**，这是硬代价。
- 首次建立锁文件仍是 TOFU：第一次的「通过」没有历史可比，必须有一次人工确认 + 记录签名/来源。

#### 改法 2（补上改法 1 的洞，抄 Bazel 的 hidden lockfile + Bazel 的 `error` 模式的教训）

Bazel 的取证给出两个反面/正面要点：

- **反面（要避免的坑）**：`--lockfile_mode=error` 的官方定义里明确写了 —— `superfluous entries that the current resolution does not consult are ignored, not reported`，且 `a lockfile that passes in this mode may nevertheless be rewritten (pruned) by update`。**即 Bazel 的 error 门禁是单向的：只保证「没漏」，不保证「没多」。** 这就是场景痛点在 Bazel 里的同构版本——「通过」并不代表摘要完全等于应有状态。
- **正面（可抄的解法）**：Bazel 另存一份 hidden lockfile（`"$(bazel info output_base)"/MODULE.bazel.lock`），注释原文说它 `serve as the record of the facts produced by the most recent actual evaluation of each extension, which SingleExtensionEvalFunction compares against the workspace lockfile's facts to detect manual edits`。**「检测是否被手工改过」这件事，提交进仓库的那份文件做不到，必须有第二份基线。**

对应本场景：除了提交用的 6 份生成物之外，另存一份**「上一次真实生成时的摘要快照」**（不进人工编辑路径，可由 CI 自动覆盖）。门禁同时比两件事：
- 提交的摘要 vs 机器重算的摘要（= 改法 1）；
- 本次机器重算的摘要 vs 上次快照的摘要（= 基线对比）。

第二项一旦非空就强制人工 review，从而把「零违规」与「比基线差」在输出上区分开——这正是场景要求的那件事。

代价：多一份需要维护的基线文件；Bazel 为此付出的是「baseline 格式 explicitly unspecified（未承诺稳定）」，本场景若把它写进门禁，必须自己承诺格式稳定性，否则它会变成第 7 个需要同步的生成物。

#### 改法 3（配套约束，两条都别忘了）

1. **钉住重算工具本身的版本。** Bazel 文档原文：`Because Bazel itself is a dependency of your build, the lockfile is specific to the Bazel version, and will change even between backwards compatible Bazel releases.` 对应本场景：摘要器/生成器的版本变更会让 834 个同步点**全量假红**。必须把工具版本钉死进仓库（Bazel 的解法是 `.bazelversion` + bazelisk）。代价：升级工具 = 一次全量摘要重算，必须单独排期，不能夹带在功能改动里。
2. **合并冲突规则要写成白名单。** Bazel 原文：`Simple merge conflicts in the registryFileHashes and selectedYankedVersions fields can be safely resolved by keeping all the entries from both sides of the conflict. Other types of merge conflicts should not be resolved manually.` 且官方提供 jq 自动合并驱动。对应本场景：如果 6 份生成物里有可按字段合并的（如纯哈希映射表），写一条自动合并规则；其余类型**明令禁止手工解冲突**，只允许「回退 → 改源 → 重跑生成命令」。这一条直接消灭「手工重算 50 次」的动机来源。

#### 一句话结论（用于报告口径）

Terraform 给出的可直接照搬的是**「重算 = 一条可批量命令 + CI 里 `git diff --exit-code`」**，Bazel 给出的关键警告是**「官方承认的单向门禁不够用（superfluous entries are ignored），必须另存一份基线来自证没被手工改过」**。两条合起来，才能把「通过」从「某脚本没报错」收紧成「与机器重算字节一致，且不劣于上次真实生成的基线」。

**附录 C — Gradle / Dhall「锁文件即摘要门禁」取证（子代理原文，照录）**

## C 组取证：Gradle / Dhall 的「锁文件即摘要门禁」

> 取证范围：Gradle dependency verification、Dhall freeze / lint / format --check。
> 所有引用均来自官方文档渲染页或官方仓库源码原文（逐字）。行号为本机抓取文件的本地行号，仅作复核定位用，**不是**官方文档的稳定标识。
> 抓取时间：2026-09-17。抓取命令前置：`export https_proxy=http://127.0.0.1:4780 http_proxy=http://127.0.0.1:4780`。

### 一、Gradle 依赖校验（verification-metadata.xml）

#### 机制名
依赖校验元数据文件 + `--dependency-verification` 三档门禁。

#### 项目
Gradle（gradle/gradle）

#### 它怎么做

Gradle 把「每个依赖产物的期望 checksum」写进一份签入版本库的 XML 文件；文件一旦存在，**每次构建自动校验**。这是「锁文件即摘要」的完整形态：锁文件不是版本号清单，而是**内容摘要清单**。

**1) 锁文件位置与自动生效**

> `Dependency verification is automatically enabled as soon as the verification metadata file is detected.`
> `The file must be located at $PROJECT_ROOT/gradle/verification-metadata.xml.`
> `Currently, the only source of dependency verification metadata is this XML configuration file. Future versions of Gradle may include other sources (for example, via external services).`

**2) 校验的因果链（checksum 语义）**

> `You record the expected checksum (for example, SHA-256) of a library or plugin in the gradle/verification-metadata.xml file.`
> `When the build downloads that artifact, it computes the checksum of the downloaded bytes.`
> `If the computed checksum doesn't match the expected value, the build fails instead of using the tampered file.`

条目形态（官方示例）：

```xml
<artifact name="trusty-lib-1.0.jar">
  <sha256 value="5e87d84077de0c60d9ff4eedbc9fac1506693019eb6f55652afc9e1ff235bd15"/>
</artifact>
```

**3) 三档模式：strict / lenient / off（当前 userguide，表格形式）**

| Mode | Description |
|---|---|
| `strict` (default) | `Verification fails as early as possible to avoid using compromised dependencies during the build` |
| `lenient` | `Runs the build even if there are verification failures. Verification errors are displayed without causing build failure` |
| `off` | `Verification is completely ignored` |

Gradle 8.5 文档的散文版表述（措辞更明确地指出「不导致构建失败」）：

> `Gradle actually provides 3 different verification modes:`
> `* strict, which is the default.`
> `  Verification fails as early as possible, in order to avoid the use of compromised dependencies during the build.`
> `* lenient, which will run the build even if there are verification failures.`
> `  The verification errors will be displayed during the build without causing a build failure.`
> `* off when verification is totally ignored.`

激活方式（CLI / 系统属性 / gradle.properties 三选一）：

> `$ ./gradlew --dependency-verification lenient build`
> `$ ./gradlew -Dorg.gradle.dependency.verification=lenient build`
> `org.gradle.dependency.verification=lenient`

CLI 参考页（`command_line_interface.html`）给出的参数签名与默认值：

> `-F=(strict,lenient,off), --dependency-verification=(strict,lenient,off)`
> `Configures the dependency verification mode.`
> `The default mode is strict.`

**4) 生成锁文件的命令与默认路径**

> `$ ./gradlew --write-verification-metadata sha256`
> `What this does:`
> `- It scans all the dependencies in your project.`
> `- It calculates the SHA-256 checksum for each.`
> `- It creates a file located at gradle/verification-metadata.xml.`

签名 + 校验和一起生成：

> `$ ./gradlew --write-verification-metadata sha256,pgp`

CLI 参考页：

> `-M, --write-verification-metadata`
> `Generates checksums for dependencies used in the project (comma-separated list) for dependency verification.`

支持的摘要算法与安全取舍：

> `Gradle supports MD5, SHA1, SHA-256, and SHA-512 checksums.`
> `However, only SHA-256 and SHA-512 are considered secure for modern use.`
> `SHA-1 has known collisions and MD5 should not be used.`

**5) 生成即「信任当下仓库」——官方自己承认的漏洞**

这是与「某次通过可能只是没比基线更差」最同构的一段官方自陈：

> `Gradle can automatically generate a dependency verification file by downloading all your dependencies and recording their checksums or signatures.`
> `This is called bootstrapping.`
> `However, bootstrapping has a critical security limitation: it trusts whatever is currently in your repositories.`
> `If a malicious dependency has already been introduced into your build (or into a repository you use), Gradle will record the compromised artifact's checksum or signature.`
> `This is why you must review the generated verification file.`
> `Bootstrapping is convenient for getting started or updating your verification file, but it's not a substitute for manual verification of critical dependencies.`

**6) 自生成条目带「待审」标记，人工复核后改成「已验证」**

这是把「谁审过」编码进锁文件本身，直接对症「通过的含义不明确」：

> `Gradle-generated checksums will have an origin attribute starting with "Generated by Gradle" to indicate they need review.`
> `Manually added entries and header comments are preserved, making it easy to update the file for new dependency versions by regenerating and reviewing the diff.`

生成后的固定复核清单：

> `After regenerating the file, follow these steps:`
> `- Review the diff - Check what changed in your version control system.`
> `- Remove unnecessary entries - Delete entries for old dependency versions that are no longer used.`
> `- Verify new entries - Ensure new checksums are correct by checking against official sources when possible.`
> `- Update all "Generated by Gradle" origins - You must review and update all entries with origin="Generated by Gradle".`
> `- Change origin to "Verified" - After verifying the checksum matches, edit the origin attribute to "Verified" to indicate you've confirmed it.`

「Verified」在真实条目中的形态（当前 userguide 示例）：

```xml
<component group="com.google.guava" name="failureaccess" version="1.0.3">
  <artifact name="failureaccess-1.0.3.jar">
    <pgp value="BDB5FA4FE719D787FB3D3197F6D4A1D411E9D1AE"/>
    <sha256 value="cbfc3906b19b8f55dd7cfd6dfe0aa4532e834250d7f080bd8d211a3e246b59cb"
      origin="Verified"
      reason="Added manually to fix CI"/>
  </artifact>
</component>
```

**7) 确定性序列化：让 diff 只含真实变化（关键）**

Gradle 8.5 文档原文明说生成物是**稳定排序**且**只做最小改动**：

> `The verification file generated by Gradle has a strict ordering for all its content.`
> `It also uses the information from the existing state to limit changes to the strict minimum.`
> `This means that generation is actually a convenient tool for updating a verification file:`
> `- Checksum entries generated by Gradle will have a clear origin that starts with "Generated by Gradle", which is a good indicator that an entry needs to be reviewed,`
> `- Entries added by hand will immediately be accounted for, and appear at the right location after writing the file,`
> `- The header comments of the file will be preserved, i.e. comments before the root XML node.`

当前 userguide 保留增量语义：

> `Generation is incremental and preserves existing entries.`

VCS 作为审查器（明确点出依赖版本库的 diff）：

> `By default, bootstrapping is incremental, which means that if you run it multiple times, information is added to the file.`
> `You can rely on your version control system to check the diffs between runs.`

**8) dry-run：只出副本，不覆盖现有基线**

> `$ ./gradlew --write-verification-metadata sha256 help --dry-run`
> `Instead of generating the verification-metadata.xml file, Gradle will create a new file called verification-metadata.dryrun.xml that you can review.`
> `Because --dry-run doesn't execute tasks, it runs much faster but will miss any dependency resolution that happens at task execution time.`

**9) 「缺失」与「不匹配」都失败，不允许静默通过**

缺失条目（升级依赖后最常见）：

> `Execution failed for task ':compileJava'.`
> `> Dependency verification failed for configuration ':compileClasspath':`
> `- On artifact commons-logging-1.2.jar (commons-logging:commons-logging:1.2) in repository 'MavenRepo': checksum is missing from verification metadata.`

摘要不匹配（严重信号）：

> `- On artifact commons-logging-1.2.jar (commons-logging:commons-logging:1.2) in repository 'MavenRepo': expected a 'sha256' checksum of '91f7a33096ea69bac2cbaf6d01feb934cac002c48d8c8cfa9c240b40f1ec21df' but was 'daddea1ea0be0f56978ab3006b8ac92834afeefbd9b7e4e6316fca57df0fa636'`
> `Gradle shows the dependency, expected checksum (declared in metadata file), and actual computed checksum.`
> `This indicates a dependency may have been compromised.`
> `You must perform manual verification.`

传递依赖也必须显式入册（否则失败）：

> `If your verification file only includes direct dependencies, the build will fail:`
> `This indicates you need to add checksums for commons-logging and all other transitive dependencies.`

**10) CI 与本地分权（模式可分离）**

> `You might want to enable verification on CI servers but not on local machines.`

（8.5 版：`Alternatively, you might want to enable verification on CI servers but not on local machines.`）

**11) 全局性：子构建不能用自己的一套**

> `The configuration file of the current build is used for verification.`
> `If an included build has its own verification metadata, that configuration is ignored in favor of the current build's settings.`
> `Because this configuration is global and strict, the most efficient way to begin is by generating a baseline configuration for your existing build.`

#### URL / 文件路径
- 官方 userguide（当前版，主来源）：`https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/dependency_verification.html`
- 官方 userguide（8.5，提供「strict ordering / limit changes to the strict minimum」段）：`https://docs.gradle.org/8.5/userguide/dependency_verification.html`
- CLI 参数参考：`https://docs.gradle.org/current/userguide/command_line_interface.html`
- 交叉验证版本（同段落在 6.9 / 7.6 / 8.0 / 8.10 均存在）：`https://docs.gradle.org/<version>/userguide/dependency_verification.html`
- 默认路径：`$PROJECT_ROOT/gradle/verification-metadata.xml`
- 已尝试但 **404** 的源码路径（若后续需要 adoc 原文可再找）：
  `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/gradle/gradle/master/platforms/documentation/docs/src/docs/userguide/dependency-management/dependency-verification.adoc`
  以及 `dependency/dependency-verification.adoc`、`core-plugins/dependency-verification.adoc`、`security/dependency-verification.adoc`、`authoring-builds/dependency-management/dependency-verification.adoc`（全部 404）。

#### 对本场景的可迁移改法

背景：改 1 个源文件要手工同步 6 份生成物（含 1 份完整性摘要），139 个包 ≈ 834 个同步点，「重算摘要」被手工执行 50 次；痛点是「一个『通过』既可能是零违规，也可能是未比历史基线更差」。

1. **把「手工重算」换成「一条生成命令 + diff 审查」**。Gradle 的模型是：人从不手写摘要，只跑 `--write-verification-metadata sha256`；审查发生在 diff 上，而不是发生在 834 个同步点上。落到本场景：834 个同步点应收敛为「跑一次生成器 + 看一次 `git diff`」。手工 50 次重算之所以发生，恰恰因为改动手工做，而 Gradle 证明这一步是可以完全工具化的。代价：必须把 6 份生成物全部纳入版本库，并接受「生成器输出即真相」的纪律；否则 diff 无意义。

2. **给摘要条目加「谁审过」的状态位，而不是只有一个布尔值**。`origin="Generated by Gradle"` vs `origin="Verified"` 把「自动生成、未审」和「人工确认过」分开编码进文件。本场景的痛点是「通过 ≠ 零违规」，本质就是缺这个状态位。改法：摘要条目/门禁结果三态化——`未比对基线`（生成器产出，待审）/ `已比对且未劣化` / `已比对且零违规`。代价：多一列字段，且需要一条「谁把它从中间态推到终态」的流程约定，否则中间态会永久堆积。

3. **门禁用 strict 语义，禁用 lenient 语义做 CI 判据**。Gradle 明确区分：`strict` = `Verification fails as early as possible`；`lenient` = `Verification errors are displayed without causing build failure`。`lenient` 正是「假绿」的定义——错误显示出来了，但退出码是 0。本场景的「一个通过可能是未比基线更差」，在所有 lenient 型门禁里都是同一个 bug 模式。改法：CI 判据绑定 strict；`未比基线更差` 这类弱判据如果需要，必须走**独立的、显式命名的**通道（见第 6 条），不能和「零违规」共用一个退出码。代价：升级/新增包时会频繁红灯，需要配套的「重新基线化」流程（对应 Gradle 的重跑生成命令）。

4. **「缺失」必须和「不匹配」同等失败**。Gradle 对 `checksum is missing from verification metadata` 和 `expected ... but was ...` 都直接 fail。本场景 834 个同步点里，只要有新增生成物没进摘要，就必须红灯——而不是「跳过未登记的」。代价：每次新增第 7 份生成物都会先失败一次，属预期的摩擦。

5. **摘要生成必须确定性**（顺序稳定 + 只做最小改动）。Gradle 原文：`The verification file generated by Gradle has a strict ordering for all its content. It also uses the information from the existing state to limit changes to the strict minimum.` 没有这条，每次重算都会产生全文件 diff，门禁立刻被噪声淹没，人就开始「习惯性通过」——这正是手工 50 次的历史成因之一。改法：生成器输出排序固定、缩进固定、编码固定；只追加/改动真正变化的那几行。代价：生成器要写排序与最小编辑逻辑（Gradle 用「读旧文件 + 合并」实现），不是简单的字符串拼接。

6. **把两种「通过」显式建模为两种意图**。Gradle 侧是 `strict/lenient/off` 三档 + `verify-signatures`/`verify-metadata` 开关；Dhall 侧是 `Secure` vs `Cache` 两种 Intent（见下节）。本场景应把「零违规」和「未比历史基线更差」建成两个不同的、有名字的产物/退出码，而不是塞进同一个「通过」。代价：门禁脚本要出两份结果，报告复杂度上升，但换来「通过」一词不再有歧义。

7. **dry-run 通道**。`--write-verification-metadata ... --dry-run` 产出 `verification-metadata.dryrun.xml` 而不覆盖现有基线。本场景若担心生成器覆盖掉人工维护的部分，可先出 `.dryrun` 副本供比对。代价：多一步人工确认，适合迁移初期。

---

### 二、Dhall：freeze / lint / format --check

#### 机制名
语义完整性检查（semantic integrity check）+ 幂等 `--check` 门禁。

#### 项目
Dhall（dhall-lang/dhall-haskell，命令行 `dhall`；语言规范在 dhall-lang/dhall-lang 的 `standard/imports.md`）

#### 它怎么做

Dhall 的「锁」不是锁文件，而是**内联在源码里的摘要后缀**：把远程 import 写成 `https://... sha256:...`。这是「摘要即门禁」的极简形态——门禁信息与源文件同体，没有第二份文件可漂移。

**1) 摘要写在 import 上**

> `let concatSep =`
> `  https://prelude.dhall-lang.org/Prelude/Text/concatSep sha256:fa909c0b2fd4f9edb46df7ff72ae105ad0bd0ae00baa7fe53b0e43863f9bd34a`

（`standard/imports.md` 第 8 行同形示例。）

**2) 摘要 = αβ-正规形的 SHA-256（语义哈希，不是文本哈希）**

这是官方文档给的核心定义：

> `Dhall's integrity checks are "semantic" integrity checks, meaning that they are hashes of an expression's normal form. This ensures that any change to an expression (even via transitive dependencies) will be detected and rejected by the integrity check. As a bonus, the semantic integrity check does not reject behavior-preserving changes so refactoring code or adding/removing whitespace/comments won't disturb the hash.`

为什么不能用文本哈希，官方给了反例论证：

> `The integrity check is not a hash of the text of the import because the textual payload of an import does not uniquely determine the import's meaning.`
> `We cannot protect the ./bool.dhall import using a textual hash of the file because the configuration file's result could still change if we modify the ./number.dhall file to store a new value (like 5). Modifications to ./number.dhall would go undetected because they would not disturb the textual hash of ./bool.dhall.`

**3) 源码级证据：哈希怎么算**

`dhall/src/Dhall/Import.hs`（两处，逐字）：

```haskell
-- | Hash a fully resolved, αβ-normal expression
--
-- The argument must already be β-normalized and α-normalized.  The semantic
-- integrity check is defined as the SHA-256 of the binary encoding of that
-- αβ-normal form.  Hashing a typechecked-but-unreduced tree (for example the
-- result of 'load' of an unhashed @Code@ import) produces a value that is
-- __not__ a valid integrity hash.
hashExpression :: Expr Void Void -> Dhall.Crypto.SHA256Digest
hashExpression = Dhall.Crypto.sha256Hash . encodeExpression
```

```haskell
{-| Convenience utility to hash a fully resolved, αβ-normal expression and
    return the base-16 encoded hash with the @sha256:@ prefix

    In other words, the output of this function can be pasted into Dhall
    source code to add an integrity check to an import.

    As with 'hashExpression', the argument must already be αβ-normal.
-}
hashExpressionToCode :: Expr Void Void -> Text
hashExpressionToCode expr =
    "sha256:" <> Text.pack (show (hashExpression expr))
```

**4) `dhall freeze` 的实现：先解开 hash，加载 + 类型检查 + 正规化，再重算 hash**

`dhall/src/Dhall/Freeze.hs`：

```haskell
freezeImportWithSettings settings directory import_ = do
    let unprotectedImport =
            import_
                { importHashed =
                    (importHashed import_)
                        { hash = Nothing
                        }
                }

    let status = Dhall.emptyStatusWithSettings settings directory

    expression <- State.evalStateT (Dhall.Import.loadWith (Embed unprotectedImport)) status

    case Dhall.TypeCheck.typeWith (view Dhall.startingContext settings) expression of
        Left  exception -> Exception.throwIO exception
        Right _         -> return ()

    let normalizedExpression = Core.alphaNormalize (Core.normalizeWith (view Dhall.normalizer settings) expression)

    -- The semantic cache product is the encoded αβ-normal form.  Unhashed
    -- Code imports are no longer β-normalized by `loadWith`, so we must
    -- write `normalizedExpression` rather than the raw load product.
    Dhall.Import.writeExpressionToSemanticCache normalizedExpression

    let expressionHash = Dhall.Import.hashExpression normalizedExpression

    let newImportHashed = (importHashed import_) { hash = Just expressionHash }

    let newImport = import_ { importHashed = newImportHashed }

    return newImport
```

注意 `hash = Nothing`：freeze 会**先丢掉旧 hash 再重新解析**，所以它是「无条件重算」，不是「校验」。官方注释也说明了这个意图：

```haskell
                {- Here we could actually compare the `_expectedHash` and
                   `_actualHash` to see if they differ, but we choose not to do
                   so and instead automatically accept the `_actualHash`.  This
                   is done for the same reason that the `freeze*` functions
                   ignore hash mismatches: the user intention when using `dhall
                   freeze` is to update the hash, which they expect to possibly
                   change.
                -}
```

对本地/环境变量 import 的处理（默认只冻结远程）：

```haskell
freezeRemoteImportWithSettings settings directory import_ =
    case importType (importHashed import_) of
        Remote {} -> freezeImportWithSettings settings directory import_
        _         -> return import_
```

**5) `--all` / `--transitive` / `--cache`（CLI 定义，来自 `dhall/src/Dhall/Main.hs`）**

```haskell
    parseAllFlag =
        Options.Applicative.switch
        (   Options.Applicative.long "all"
        <>  Options.Applicative.help "Add integrity checks to all imports (not just remote imports) except for missing imports"
        )
```

```haskell
    parseCacheFlag =
        Options.Applicative.switch
        (   Options.Applicative.long "cache"
        <>  Options.Applicative.help "Add fallback unprotected imports when using integrity checks purely for caching purposes"
        )
```

```haskell
    parseTransitiveSwitch = Options.Applicative.flag NonTransitive Transitive
        (   Options.Applicative.long "transitive"
        <>  Options.Applicative.help "Modify the input and its transitive relative imports in-place"
        )
```

子命令注册（`--check` 的 help 文案即「只检查是否已 X」）：

```haskell
    <|> subcommand
            Manipulate
            "freeze"
            "Add integrity checks to remote import statements of an expression"
            (Freeze <$> deprecatedInPlace <*> parseTransitiveSwitch <*> parseAllFlag <*> parseCacheFlag <*> parseCheck "frozen" <*> parseFiles)
```

**6) Secure vs Cache 两种意图（源码枚举，官方注释逐字）**

```haskell
-- | Specifies why we are adding semantic integrity checks
data Intent
    = Secure
    -- ^ Protect imports with an integrity check without a fallback so that
    --   import resolution fails if the import changes
    | Cache
    -- ^ Protect imports with an integrity check and also add a fallback import
    --   import without an integrity check.  This is useful if you only want to
    --   cache imports when possible but still gracefully degrade to resolving
    --   them if the semantic integrity check has changed.
```

范围枚举：

```haskell
data Scope
    = OnlyRemoteImports
    -- ^ Freeze only remote imports (i.e. URLs)
    | AllImports
    -- ^ Freeze all imports (including paths and environment variables)
```

**7) `--check` 的实现：文本相等即通过，不等即 Left CheckFailed（门禁的判定核心）**

`dhall/src/Dhall/Freeze.hs` 的 `freezeWithSettings` 内：

```haskell
        case outputMode of
            Write -> do
                ...
                    InputFile file ->
                        if originalText == modifiedText
                            then return ()
                            else
                                AtomicWrite.LazyText.atomicWriteFile
                                    file
                                    (Pretty.Text.renderLazy unAnnotated)
                ...
                return (Right ())

            Check ->
                return $
                    if originalText == modifiedText
                        then Right ()
                        else Left CheckFailed{..}
```

即：**`--check` 判定的是「重新生成的结果是否与磁盘上的文件逐字节一致」**——一个幂等性（idempotence）断言，而不是「哈希能否通过」的运行时断言。两者是不同层次：`--check` 管源文件与摘要是否同步，import 解析时的哈希校验由 `Dhall.Import` 负责。

`Dhall.Format`（`dhall/src/Dhall/Format.hs`）用同一套模式：

```haskell
            Check ->
                return $
                    if originalText == formattedText
                        then Right ()
                        else Left CheckFailed{..}
```

`dhall lint --check` 在 `Main.hs` 中同样走 `handleMultipleChecksFailed "lint" "linted" go inputs`。

**8) 失败即非零退出（源码级）**

`dhall/src/Dhall/Util.hs`：

```haskell
handleMultipleChecksFailed command modified f xs = post =<< mapM f xs
  where
    post results =
        case lefts (toList results) of
            [] -> pure ()
            cf:cfs -> Control.Exception.throwIO $ MultipleCheckFailed
                { command
                , modified
                , inputs = fmap input (cf:|cfs)
                }
```

```haskell
-- | Exception thrown when the @--check@ flag to a command-line subcommand fails
data MultipleCheckFailed = MultipleCheckFailed
  { command :: Text
  , modified :: Text
  , inputs :: NonEmpty Input
  }

instance Exception MultipleCheckFailed

instance Show MultipleCheckFailed where
    show MultipleCheckFailed{..} =
         _ERROR <> ": ❰dhall " <> command_ <> " --check❱ failed on:\n\
        \\n" <> files <>
        "\n\
        \You ran ❰dhall " <> command_ <> " --check❱, but the input appears to have not\n\
        \been " <> modified_ <> " before, or was changed since the last time the input\n\
        \was " <> modified_ <> ".\n"
```

`dhall/src/Dhall/Main.hs` 的顶层异常处理把未捕获异常统一转成退出失败：

```haskell
            handleAll e = do
                let string = show (e :: SomeException)
                when (not (null string)) $ System.IO.hPutStrLn System.IO.stderr string
                System.Exit.exitFailure
```

`main`：

```haskell
-- | Entry point for the @dhall@ executable
main :: IO ()
main = do
    options <- Options.Applicative.execParser parserInfoOptions

    Dhall.Main.command options
```

**9) `dhall diff`：把「相等 / 不等」做成显式退出码**

`dhall/src/Dhall/Main.hs`：

```haskell
        Diff {..} -> do
            expression1 <- Dhall.inputExpr expr1

            expression2 <- Dhall.inputExpr expr2

            let diff = Dhall.Diff.diffNormalized expression1 expression2

            renderDoc System.IO.stdout (Dhall.Diff.doc diff)

            if Dhall.Diff.same diff
                then return ()
                else Exit.exitFailure
```

子命令描述：`"Render the difference between the normal form of two expressions"`。`Dhall.Diff` 的定义（`dhall/src/Dhall/Diff.hs`）：

```haskell
data Diff =
    Diff
        { same :: Bool
        , doc  :: Doc Ann
        }

instance Semigroup Diff where
    Diff sameL docL <> Diff sameR docR = Diff (sameL && sameR) (docL <> docR)
```

注意 `diffNormalized` —— 比较的是**正规形**，与语义哈希同源。

**10) `dhall lint` 做什么（官方 FAQ 逐字）**

> `dhall lint:`
> `- Removes unused let bindings`
> `- Consolidates nested let bindings to use a multiple-let binding`
> `- Fixes let bindings containing equivalences (≡) that were probably intended as assertions.`

源码模块头（`dhall/src/Dhall/Lint.hs`）逐字：

```haskell
{-| Automatically improve a Dhall expression

    Currently this:

    * removes unused @let@ bindings with 'removeUnusedBindings'.
    * fixes @let a = x ≡ y@ to be @let a = assert : x ≡ y@
    * consolidates nested @let@ bindings to use a multiple-@let@ binding with 'removeLetInLet'
    * fixes paths of the form @.\/..\/foo@ to @..\/foo@
-}
```

子命令描述：`"Improve Dhall code by using newer language features and removing dead code"`；`format` 是 `"Standard code formatter for the Dhall language"`。

**11) 冻结后的效果（官方原文）**

> `An import frozen in this way can never successfully return a different expression. If the above URL tried to serve a new expression then the integrity check would fail and the interpreter would reject the configuration file. This means that a malicious actor would not be able to modify the behavior of our program by serving a new concatSep function with the same type but different behavior. The worst they can do is cause the program to fail loudly if they tamper with the import in any way.`

缓存副产品：

> `However, when you protect an import with a semantic integrity check the import is permanently locally cached after the first request, so subsequent imports will no longer make outbound HTTP requests.`

**12) 语言规范级（`standard/imports.md`）：MUST 级要求**

> `If the import is protected with a sha256:base16Hash integrity check, then:`
> `* the import's normal form is encoded to a binary representation`
> `* the binary representation is hashed using SHA-256`
> `* the SHA-256 hash is base16-encoded`
> `* the base16-encoded result has to match the integrity check`

> `An implementation MUST fail and alert the user if hash verification fails, either when importing an expression for the first time or importing from the local cache.`

> `Hash verification and the semantic-cache bytes are always the encoded αβ-normal form of the import.`

一个容易踩的细节（`as Text` / `as Bytes` 导入时哈希的是什么）：

> `Carefully note that "s" in the above judgment is a Dhall Text literal. This implies that if you import an expression as Text and you also protect the import with a semantic integrity check then you encode the string literal as a Dhall expression and hash that. The semantic integrity check is not a hash of the raw underlying text.`

> `Similar to the as Text import, note that 0x"0123456789abcdef" in the above judgment is a Dhall Bytes literal and the same observation concerning semantic integrity checks applies: The semantic integrity check is not a hash of the raw underlying byte string but the one of the encoded Dhall expression.`

- 源码 `dhall freeze` 实现 + `--check` 判定：`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dhall-lang/dhall-haskell/master/dhall/src/Dhall/Freeze.hs`
- 源码 `dhall lint`：`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dhall-lang/dhall-haskell/master/dhall/src/Dhall/Lint.hs`
- 源码 `dhall format --check`：`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dhall-lang/dhall-haskell/master/dhall/src/Dhall/Format.hs`
- 源码 CLI 参数与 `diff` 退出码：`https://raw.githubusercontent.com/dhall-lang/dhall-haskell/master/dhall/src/Dhall/Main.hs`

#### 对本场景的可迁移改法

1. **把「重算摘要」从人工操作改成 CI 里的 `--check`**。Dhall 的 `dhall freeze --check` 语义是「重新生成一遍，看结果与磁盘是否逐字节相同」，不等则抛 `MultipleCheckFailed` 并退出非零。这正是本场景 50 次手工「重算摘要」的自动化替代：人不再重算，CI 每次重算并比对；人只处理红灯。代价：生成器必须是**确定性**的（同样的输入必得同样的字节），否则 `--check` 永远红灯——这一点与 Gradle 的「strict ordering」要求是同一个约束，两个生态独立地得出了同一结论。

2. **摘要取「语义正规形」而不是文本，避免格式噪声假失败**。Dhall 原文：`the semantic integrity check does not reject behavior-preserving changes so refactoring code or adding/removing whitespace/comments won't disturb the hash.` 本场景有 6 份生成物，若摘要直接对生成物字节取哈希，任何格式/顺序抖动都会触发红灯，人会开始忽略红灯。改法：先对生成物做规范化（排序、稳定序列化、去除时间戳/随机 id），再取哈希。代价：需要为每种生成物定义「规范形」，这是本改法的主要工作量；对无法定义规范形的产物（含时间戳的报表等），只能退回字节哈希并接受噪声。

3. **摘要是「全链传递」的，不是逐点的**。Dhall 的关键论据：`This ensures that any change to an expression (even via transitive dependencies) will be detected.` 以及文本哈希的反例（改 `./number.dhall` 不会被 `./bool.dhall` 的文本哈希发现）。本场景 834 个同步点里，最危险的不是「某一份没同步」，而是「上游变了、下游摘要没跟着变，但下游文件本身字节没动」。改法：根摘要应覆盖整条依赖链的语义，而不是逐文件独立哈希。代价：任何一处改动都会使根摘要变化，需要配套的「变化归因」输出（Dhall 用 `dhall diff` 打印正规形差异就是这个作用）。

4. **把「零违规」和「未比基线更差」建成两个显式的、有名字的意图**。Dhall 用 `Intent` 把这件事做成了类型：`Secure` = `import resolution fails if the import changes`（严格，无回退）；`Cache` = 加一个无校验的 fallback import（宽松，优先缓存、允许降级）。本场景的痛点（一个「通过」含义不明）可以直接照搬：门禁设 `Secure` 通道（零违规才通过）和 `Cache` 通道（未比基线更差即通过），**两个通道产出不同的、显式命名的结果**，不允许混用一个退出码。代价：报告与脚本复杂度上升；`Cache` 通道的 fallback 会让「降级」变成静默行为，必须额外日志（Dhall 的做法是把 fallback 写成 `import sha256:… ? import` 这种**语法上可见**的形式，而不是隐藏起来——这点值得抄）。

5. **`--all` / `--transitive` 级的范围开关**。默认只冻结远程 import（`OnlyRemoteImports` / `freezeRemoteImportWithSettings`），`--all` 才覆盖本地路径与环境变量，`--transitive` 才递归处理相对 import 链。本场景 6 份生成物中，若部分是从本地文件派生的，需要显式选择是否纳入门禁范围，而不是默认全包或全不包。代价：范围设太宽则红灯频繁、设太窄则漏掉真正的漂移点，需要按生成物类型逐个定策。

6. **冻结一次即永久本地缓存**。原文：`when you protect an import with a semantic integrity check the import is permanently locally cached after the first request, so subsequent imports will no longer make outbound HTTP requests.` 若本场景的门禁需要拉取远端基线，摘要化后可大幅降低网络依赖与耗时。代价：缓存与远端漂移会脱钩，需要显式的「重新基线化」动作（对应 `dhall freeze` 不带 `--check` 的那次写回）。

7. **`dhall diff` 的退出码模式值得直接借用**：打印人类可读差异 + `if same then return () else exitFailure`。即门禁既输出「差在哪」（人能看懂），又输出「是否相同」（机器可判）。本场景的报告如果只有退出码，人就无法判断「未比基线更差」到底差在哪；如果只有文字差异，CI 又无法判定。两个都要。

---

### 三、两生态对照与本场景取舍（汇总）

| 维度 | Gradle | Dhall | 本场景可采用 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 摘要载体 | 独立锁文件 `gradle/verification-metadata.xml` | 内联在源码 import 上的 `sha256:` 后缀 | 6 份生成物宜各自带内联摘要（无第二份文件可漂移）；或集中一份但必须纳入同一 VCS |
| 摘要对象 | 字节（artifact 下载文件） + PGP 签名 | αβ-正规形的二进制编码（语义） | 优先语义；无法定义规范形的产物退回字节 |
| 覆盖范围 | 直接 + 传递依赖全部显式入册 | 传递依赖由正规形自然覆盖 | 根摘要覆盖链，避免逐点哈希的漏检 |
| 「待审」状态 | `origin="Generated by Gradle"` → `origin="Verified"` | 无对应机制（`--check` 只有等/不等） | 抄 Gradle：门禁结果三态化 |
| 确定性要求 | `strict ordering` + `limit changes to the strict minimum` | `--check` 要求生成器幂等 | 两者同结论：生成器必须确定性 |
| 门禁判定 | 退出码（strict 失败 / lenient 不失败） | 退出码（`CheckFailed` → `exitFailure`） | 用 strict；禁止 lenient 作为 CI 判据 |
| 强弱两档 | `strict` / `lenient` / `off` | `Intent`: `Secure`（无回退） / `Cache`（带回退） | 把「零违规」与「未比基线更差」显式分为两档，并用可见语法标记降级 |
| dry-run | `--write-verification-metadata ... --dry-run` → `.dryrun.xml` | 无（`--check` 本身不写回，等价于 dry-run） | 迁移初期用 dry-run 保护人工维护内容 |
| 人审的落点 | `git diff`（明确点出 `rely on your version control system to check the diffs`） | 无（无中间产物） | 审 diff，不审 834 个同步点 |

#### 核心可迁移结论（一句话）

两个生态独立得出同一条工程规律：**「重算摘要」必须是工具的一条确定性命令，人只审 diff；门禁必须用严格语义的退出码，且「缺失」「不匹配」「未比对基线」三种状态不能共用一个「通过」。** Gradle 用 `origin="Generated by Gradle"` / `"Verified"` 区分「自动生成待审」与「人工已确认」；Dhall 用 `Intent`（`Secure` vs `Cache`）区分「变了就失败」与「变了就降级」——本场景的「一个通过既可能零违规、也可能未比历史基线更差」，正是缺这两个区分。

---

### 四、未证实 / 需要注意的点

1. **任务书要求的 Gradle 原文 "fails the build on any verification failure" 未在任何官方文档中找到。** 官方实际措辞是 `Verification fails as early as possible to avoid using compromised dependencies during the build`（表格）与 `Verification fails as early as possible, in order to avoid the use of compromised dependencies during the build.`（8.5 散文版）。语义等价，但**不要引用前者作为原文**。
2. **Gradle 官方 adoc 源文件路径未找到**（5 条候选路径均 404）。本报告的 Gradle 引用全部来自官方**渲染页** `docs.gradle.org/.../dependency_verification.html`，属官方一级来源，但无法给出仓库内 `.adoc` 文件路径。已用 6.9 / 7.6 / 8.0 / 8.5 / 8.10 / current 六个版本交叉验证 `strict ordering`、`limit changes to the strict minimum`、`strict, which is the default` 段落稳定存在（6.9 版无 `strict ordering` 段）。
3. **`dhall freeze --check` 在「哈希不匹配时非零退出」的精确表述需要修正**：源码显示 `--check` 比较的是**重新生成后的文本与磁盘文本是否逐字节相同**（`if originalText == modifiedText then Right () else Left CheckFailed{..}`），而**不是**运行时哈希校验。运行时哈希不匹配由 `Dhall.Import` 处理，规范要求 `An implementation MUST fail and alert the user if hash verification fails`。两者都会导致非零退出，但机制层次不同——本报告已按源码如实区分。
4. **`documentation/docs/.../How-to-freeze-remote-imports.html`（任务书指定的交叉印证 URL）返回 404**，该页面不存在于 docs.dhall-lang.org。已改用 `discussions/Safety-guarantees.html`（含 freeze 完整说明）+ `howtos/FAQ.html` + 语言规范 `standard/imports.md` 三角交叉印证。
5. **`dhall-lang/dhall-lang` 与 `dhall-lang/dhall-haskell` 的 README.md 中没有任何 freeze / lint / hash 相关正文**（两份文件 grep 均无命中），不能作为 freeze 机制的来源。
6. **Gradle 的 `DependencyVerificationMode` 源码类未取到**（3 条候选路径均 404）。三档模式的定义依据为官方文档（userguide 表格 + 8.5 散文版 + CLI 参考页），文档之间措辞一致，可信度高，但无法给出源码行。
7. **未做实测**：本机未安装 `dhall` 与 `gradle`（`which` 无输出），所有结论均为文档 / 源码取证，无运行验证。若需端到端验证（例如实跑 `dhall freeze --check` 观察退出码），需先安装对应工具。
